# 第 23 章　沉于生者的忘川

*4.3 版本主视觉　·　图源：米游社*

> The Lethe Below the Living　·　沉于生者的忘川

## 196　Illusion of the End　误以为结束的错觉

> 世界：Astral Express　|　区域：Parlor Car　|　角色：March 7th; Dan Heng; Himeko; Pom-Pom; Poofy; Snowie; Sunday; Trailblazer; Welt; Yao Guang; "Shush"　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Arcadia Revisited'' completed

### Story Recap

During the events of "Star Rail FEST," Ashveil discovered what the Happiness Surgery truly entails — implanting the flesh and blood of the Abominations of Abundance. Given the gravity of the situation, we decided to go to Graphia Academy and first launch an investigation into Fulwish.

During the course of events, we unexpectedly crossed paths with Fulwish herself. Also a survivor of the Bloodstained Games, she openly joined the Phantasmoon Games and invited Supplicants to travel to Seafeld City, where the winner would be decided in a televised debate.

During our exchange with Fulwish, Himeko realized that Fulwish might be a Masked Fool. As a result, I asked Sparxie to investigate the tavern for leads, and through that we identified Fulwish's ability, the "Happiness Syntax."

Evanescia and Yao Guang established a cooperation agreement. After Evanescia's attempt to assess Fulwish in her capacity as arbitrator failed, they encountered Blade and his group and learned that the flesh implanted in the Happiness Surgery originated from Shuhu, an Emanator of Abundance.

Himeko and I went to the TV station to take part in the debate and met up with Sunday and Ashveil, who had arrived ahead of us. However, the debate turned out to be a carefully orchestrated trap. In full view of the public, all those who had undergone the surgery transformed into terrifying monsters, and Fulwish framed both the IPC and the Astral Express as the masterminds behind it all.

When the disaster erupted, Evanescia willingly violated a forbidden taboo and revealed the real purpose of the Phantasmoon Games — to suppress the terrifying entity slumbering within Planarcadia. Through this act, she unlocked the original power of the Ambrosial Arbor within herself and intervened in the unfolding situation.

At the same time, Silver Wolf, entrusted by Pearl, set out to investigate the anomalous signal responsible for the monsters' emergence, only to be obstructed by "Mr. Meanie." As the force backing Fulwish, he wields tremendous power, powerful enough to interfere with the Stellaron Hunters' "script."

Meanwhile, Fulwish continued to livestream our movements in the TV Tower, distorting the truth by every possible means. Drawing upon the Wishpower of two hundred thousand victims, she manifested the "Meanie Phantasmoon", in an attempt to defeat us individually. Thankfully, Silver Wolf regained the power of the Aether Cartridge "LV.999" and dealt with it before it was too late.

After overcoming every obstacle in our path, we reached the top of the TV Tower and confronted Fulwish head-on. Drawing on the people's Wishpower, she briefly held the upper hand. Ultimately, I used the Stellaron within me to bear that Wishpower, thereby stepping onto the Path of Elation and defeating her once and for all.

Even so, those who had undergone the surgery did not recover. At Blade's proposal, everyone joined forces to concentrate all of Shuhu's flesh within his body, freeing the victims from the influence of the Happiness Surgery.

Just when I thought the crisis was over, Evanescia requested that I warn the creator of the World in Canvas. According to her, Asat Pramad, the Lord Ravager who once attempted to eliminate Planarcadia, has returned again...

### Return to the Express and check the news

> *The commotion stirred up by Fulwish has come to a close, but the dark clouds shrouding Phantasmoon have yet to clear. The Phantasmoon Games, the World in Canvas, Shuhu's flesh, a Lord Ravager... There are just too many thorny issues to deal with, and it's getting a little hard to breathe. Deep breaths, (Trailblazer). Deep breaths. March seems to be watching the news. Let's go see what's happening with Planarcadia.*

[Recap] You returned to the Express, where March 7th invited you to watch the news about Fulwish TV together.

**News Broadcast:** Moving on to our special segment: The Phantasmoon Brief.

**Snowie:** Hi everyone, this is your old friend, Snowie!

**Snowie:** Yes, we are. And after the director of Fulwish TV and sponsor of the Research Society of Happy Smiles, Fulwish, announced that she was joining the Phantasmoon Games, that terrifying tragedy of fifteen years ago once again swept through this Aeonic Game!

**Poofy:** This imagenesis plague, centered on Seafeld City, spread rapidly. Even Fulwish herself turned into a monster atop the Seafeld TV Tower!

**Snowie:** As of the latest reports, nearly two hundred thousand human and imagenae inhabitants across Planarcadia have been affected by the plague. Countless individuals are injured or missing, and total losses are estimated to have reached 8.6 billion credits!

**Snowie:** And in a chilling echo of the Bloodstained Games fifteen years ago, footage left behind by Fulwish points to the disaster as the work of Supplicants from beyond the sky.

**Poofy:** She identified the culprits as the famed cosmic hero, (Trailblazer) of the Astral Express, and the wanted criminal, Stellaron Hunter Blade, claiming they were responsible for the tragedy.

**Snowie:** But a spokesperson for the Department of Aberration Defense said the disaster was planned and executed by Fulwish herself through her organization, the Research Society of Happy Smiles. They added that the investigation will continue, and the organization will be shut down.

**Poofy:** How familiar that sounds. Fifteen years ago, they made the same excuses. What is the real story behind this? The Phantasmoon Brief will continue to keep you updated.

**Snowie:** Over 500 years ago, a prophet made a prediction: "In year 1999, a terrifying devil will descend from the sky"! Will this prophecy come true? Can Planarcadia safely make it through this crucial year? We will have to wait and see!

### Rest for a while on the sofa

> *My legs feel like lead, and my head is spinning. Fatigue is numbing every nerve, and I'm losing control of my body. No, first, I need to rest for a bit.*

**(Trailblazer):** (Nah, I've had a long day. I should just get some rest.)

> **开拓者：**你是说能够一口一个星球的古兽奥博洛斯的那个…「贪饕」？

[Recap] You awakened from a deep sleep. Himeko encourage you to keep moving forward with her own trailblaze experience and promised to share the story of how she began her journey once you returned from the World in Canvas. Yao Guang visted the Express, wanting to speak with you privately.

*(Sit on the couch)*

A sofa with a plush velvet surface like a cradle. The moment you sit down, you practically melt into it.

The static from the news broadcast, March's relentless questioning, the looming presence of the Lord Ravager, the dangerous flesh writhing on Blade's body, and the future of Phantasmoon Games — all these tangled problems had knotted themselves into one chaotic mess.

What can you do? You're just a poor little raccoon. You want to unravel the mystery, but now you're tangled up in its threads. You struggle uselessly for a bit, then give up, letting fatigue shroud your eyes in darkness.

**(Trailblazer):** ...

**Himeko:** ...Shh, Welt. He's/She's tired. Let him/her sleep.

**(Trailblazer):** ...

You never knew you could sleep this much.

**Himeko:** You're obviously tired. I never thought this vacation would become the prelude to yet another major war.

- ▸ As a Nameless, I am not surprised...
- ▸ There's so much to deal with, and I don't know where to begin.

**(Trailblazer):** From our very first steps, trouble has always followed the Nameless.

**(Trailblazer):** But this is way too much.

**Himeko:** Look at you now. It takes me back to when I first got the Express running again. Aside from Pom-Pom, I was the only crew member on board, constantly stretched thin and feeling out of my depth.

**Himeko:** All I could do was note every problem in the navigation log, one by one: a world's coordinates that needed an update, a glitch in the Express's warp system... The notes were as dense as the coordinates on the astral charts, leaving me completely overwhelmed.

**Himeko:** Back then, I never imagined the Astral Express would one day have to face a Lord Ravager. But strangely, now that the time has come again, I'm not panicking at all.

**Himeko:** Come to think of it, it's because of you, because of all of you. Along the way, we have defeated two Lord Ravagers.

**Himeko:** The Nameless who guided me before I set out once told me...

**Himeko:** "Keep moving forward, and the obstacles that once felt impossible to conquer will soon be behind you. And along the way, you'll find companions who are just like you."

**Himeko:** I've seen you grow through the Phantasmoon Games, becoming someone others can trust and lean on. And I heard the declaration you made atop the tower. (Trailblazer), I'm truly proud of you.

**Himeko:** That's why I want to share a story from the past with you. A promise from fifteen years ago.

- ▸ Is it about how Himeko got the Express running again?
- ▸ A promise from fifteen years ago?

**Himeko:** I promised someone that when the moon over my hometown was full again, I would go back to see her, to let her know I had made the most of this borrowed life, and found companions I could trust with all my heart.

**Himeko:** I want to share this story with you all. The story of how my journey began.

- ▸ I can't wait to hear it!

**Himeko:** It's a long story filled with many memories of my past.

- ▸ How come you never mentioned it before?

**Himeko:** Because it's a long story filled with many memories of my past.

**Himeko:** I'd also like to invite you all to the place where I repaired the Express, and tell you everything bit by bit.

**(Trailblazer):** Alright, it's a deal. When I return from the World in Canvas, you can make us... um, let Shush make us some coffee. Then we'll settle in and listen to your story.

**Himeko:** Mm, speaking of the World in Canvas, March told me about what Evanescia said to you.

**Himeko:** Evanescia's request was not made lightly, and she was worried for a reason. Silver Wolf saw that Lord Ravager in action too.

**Himeko:** According to the records from the data bank and the IPC's investigation, Asat Pramad's campaigns often span centuries, and the planets he targets all end up destroyed in freak accidents, leaving their surfaces covered in only smiling faces.

**Himeko:** A thousand years ago, my ancestor Graphia created the World in Canvas. If that was to resist Asat Pramad's invasion of Ahatopia, then could this disaster unleashed through the Phantasmoon Games be the prelude to his next invasion?

- ▸ It astonishes me that Graphia is still alive.

**Himeko:** That's the first of many mysteries... Based on our family history, we are clearly not descendants of any long-lived species.

**Himeko:** Quite the opposite, the Weathering Curse has shortened my family's lifespan for generations. What could my ancestor have gone through to still exist today? That, too, leaves me deeply curious.

- ▸ Could you tell me more about Graphia?

**Himeko:** There are far too many legends about her. It's hard to tell what's true and what's not.

**Himeko:** Only two things are beyond doubt. First, during the Cindearth Age, she created countless imagenae and sent them into battle against the demon legions from beyond.

**Himeko:** Second, near the end of one of the Phantasmoon Games, she created the World in Canvas to shelter inside it what was left of civilization and life.

**Himeko:** Which means... she is not only familiar with Asat Pramad, but has also witnessed the manifestation of the Aeon of Elation.

- ▸ About the World in Canvas...

**Himeko:** Nobody knows just how big the plane Graphia created actually is. Over 140 years ago, the IPC tried to salvage the World in Canvas, but they were only able to reconstruct part of it in the real world. That portion became what we know as Planarcadia.

**Himeko:** Legend has it that Graphia used a vast ocean as her canvas and channeled the power of Elation to step into the painting. Which means... she has not only seen Asat Pramad, but has also seen the Aeon of Elation descend in all THEIR glory.

- ▸ Where do I begin?

**Himeko:** She should have some clues to guide us in the right direction.

- ▸ Good news?
- ▸ Bad news?

**Himeko:** Go talk to General Yao Guang then. Don't keep her waiting. After that, come to the Party Car and fill us in on your next move.

### Talk to Yao Guang

> *As expected of General Yao Guang, bringing a gift on her visit to the Express! Wishing her great fortune! I wonder if the news arriving alongside her mung bean gift is good or bad?*

[Recap] The Voracity was sealed at the core of Ahatopia. Perhaps that was what Asat Pramad was after. Blade had accepted Yao Guang's proposal to use the Voracity to devour Shuhu's flesh. You might just cross paths with him in the World in Canvas.

**Yao Guang:** Nice to meet you, īśvara.

**Yao Guang:** I always thought the arbitrator Evanescia would step in and punish Fulwish for the disaster she caused, but to my surprise, it was you who defeated Fulwish.

**Yao Guang:** In my capacity as an Arbiter General, I extend my deepest gratitude to you for halting the plague and sparing countless lives from suffering.

- ▸ You are too kind, general.
- ▸ General, you want to talk to me in private?

**Yao Guang:** What I am about to tell you is something Evanescia risked breaking the rules to share with me. Please keep it a secret. Not a word of this leaves the Express.

**Yao Guang:** Sealed within Ahatopia's core is Oroboros the Voracity.

- ▸ That's cosmic-level bad news!

**(Trailblazer):** You mean the Leviathan that can swallow whole planets... That Oroboros the Voracity?

> **开拓者：**大家都聚在那儿干嘛呢？

**Yao Guang:** Looks like you know a thing or two about Oroboros.

**(Trailblazer):** I've seen that terrifying thing in Herta's Simulated Universe!

> **开拓者：**谢谢你们，因为有你们在身边，我才有勇气面对未来。这份力量，我会带去接下来的旅途。

- ▸ The Voracity... What is that?

**Yao Guang:** Wait, you've never heard of the Voracity before?

**Yao Guang:** Come to think of it, THEY must be unfamiliar to most people. THEY were supposed to be recorded in the List of Archenemies as an Author of Calamity... THEY are an ancient god of desire and consumption, an Aeon that evolved from a Leviathan.

**Yao Guang:** But THEY disappeared so long ago that the Xianzhou Alliance never witnessed any of THEIR deeds, and THEY were never recognized as an Author of Calamity.

**Yao Guang:** Well, THEY need no such title anyway... Beast of Calamity, Star Devourer, Void Maw. Just hearing these names, one can imagine what kind of monstrosity THEY were: a vast, writhing form whose slightest movement could crush entire planets, before swallowing what remained into an insatiable abyss.

**Yao Guang:** Rumor has it THEY were last seen during the era of the Swarm Disaster. THEY were hardly there to aid the Swarm, but rather to attend a cosmic-level feast, in which the Swarm served merely as appetizers and the planets THEY passed were devoured as the main course.

**Yao Guang:** No one expected THEM to be here of all places, sealed away on this very planet, after vanishing for years.

- ▸ Sealed... Are THEY dead?
- ▸ Is the Voracity still alive?

**Yao Guang:** The true situation remains unknown. But with this information, we can understand why Asat Pramad set his sights on this world.

**Yao Guang:** Aren't you a little afraid, facing a huge disaster like this?

- ▸ I've seen worse.

**Yao Guang:** That's impressive, īśvara. No wonder you survived the calamity of Irontomb.

- ▸ I have a poker face.

**Yao Guang:** Interesting. You wouldn't flinch even if a star fell upon you. A true īśvara.

**Yao Guang:** Fortune follows when fate hits its lowest ebb. Every crisis comes with an opportunity.

**Yao Guang:** Now that we know a force capable of devouring stars is deep in slumber at this planet's core, perhaps we could make it "devour" the problem inside Blade's body. It might just be a risky path worth taking.

**Yao Guang:** Blade has agreed to my proposal and will venture into the World in Canvas to seek out the Voracity. From what I've heard, you are going there as well?

**Yao Guang:** I cast a divination, and the hexagram shifted between the marsh and fire trigrams, yielding the judgment, "Heaven and earth are separate and apart, but the work which they do is the same." In other words, though your paths may differ, they are destined to intersect.

- ▸ Is this gonna be... "Blade's funeral"?

**Yao Guang:** A funeral, or an adventure? Will it be a battle with only a one-in-ten chance of survival to tear away Shuhu's flesh from his body and eliminate the Xianzhou's Archenemy? Or will it be a journey in which he settles all grudges and never looks back? It all depends on how he perceives it.

**Yao Guang:** Blade agreed to my proposal, even though that little Stellaron Hunter girl wouldn't stop objecting.

- ▸ General, are you betting everything on me?

**Yao Guang:** Betting? No, I don't see it that way.

**Yao Guang:** We are not chips laid on a gaming table, nor dice tossed at someone's whim. We trust in people: in ourselves, and in those beside us, knowing they can change the course of even the most desperate battle.

**Yao Guang:** That belief is what gave you the strength to overcome Fulwish. It is also the only reason I stay for this world. My purpose has never changed. I am going to draw the Elation into my divination, and overturn a fate marked for Great Woe.

**Yao Guang:** I have a plan for how to handle Asat Pramad if he strikes unexpectedly, but I still need more hands to make it work.

- ▸ Looks like the Phantasmoon Games will have to come to an end soon...

**Yao Guang:** What "miracle" could cap off these games better than a finale that humbles a Lord Ravager? It will be a tribute to the Elation!

**Yao Guang:** The shadow of the Lord Ravager looms, and from here on, everyone must carry their part of the burden. I will take my leave first.

### Head to the Party Car and chat with everyone

**March 7th:** Exactly! You knocked out the mastermind behind all those creepy Joy... whatever their name is. I didn't get to see it myself, but Dan Heng described it for me. You killed it!

**Himeko:** Now, let's make a toast.

**Himeko:** Here's to everyone who fights to protect this arcadia.

**Crowd:** Cheers!

Glasses clink together, like your hearts beating as one.

After finishing the drinks, the Nameless each enjoy a brief moment of respite.

**Pom-Pom:** Oh, it's you, (Trailblazer)! Pom-Pom heard you've got a solo mission coming up. Be safe, will you?

- ▸ Conductor, do you remember anything from before the Express got back up and running?

**Pom-Pom:** Sadly, Pom-Pom can't remember most of what happened right after the Express crashed onto this world!

**Pom-Pom:** Pom-Pom's memory of those years is like a fogged-up Express window. No matter how hard Pom-Pom tries, Pom-Pom just can't wipe it clean!

**Pom-Pom:** But it's okay! After Passenger Himeko repaired the Express, Pom-Pom got to share a one-of-a-kind journey with all of you!

- ▸ How did you and Himeko meet, Pom-Pom?

**Pom-Pom:** How did Passenger Himeko appear? That's odd... Pom-Pom can't remember a thing. All Pom-Pom recalls is having a long and fuzzy sleep, then being woken by a knock at the door. And just like that, Passenger Himeko was on board.

**Pom-Pom:** The day Passenger Himeko knocked, it felt like Pom-Pom was waking up from a dream. The Express's heart started beating again, and the lights flickered on, one after another.

- ▸ Pom-Pom, have you heard of the name Graphia?

**Pom-Pom:** Graphia? Pom-Pom hasn't.

**Pom-Pom:** But Pom-Pom always dreams of strangers when Pom-Pom's asleep. They seem to be bustling around the Express, and there are soft clinking and clanking sounds, just like... like the sound of stars twinkling.

**Pom-Pom:** But every time Pom-Pom wakes up, their faces slip away. Pom-Pom is only left with a warm and comforting feeling, knowing someone had taken good care of Pom-Pom.

- ▸ Conductor, it's time for me to say goodbye.

**Pom-Pom:** Safe trip! Pom-Pom will take good care of the Express while you're away!

*(Talk to Pom-Pom again, optional)*

**Pom-Pom:** Safe trip! Pom-Pom will take good care of the Express while you're away!

*(Talk to Himeko, optional)*

Himeko is focused on her phone screen, seemingly messaging someone.

*(Talk to "Shush", optional)*

"Shush" remains silent, intently scrubbing a glass.

[Recap] Dan Heng wanted to accompany Blade on his final journey, but was turned down. At the request of Yao Guang, Dan Heng and Welt needed to help with some guard formation work. March 7th volunteered enthusiastically to head to the tavern with Ashveil to investigate Asat Pramad.

*(Talk to Dan Heng and March 7th)*

**Dan Heng:** There you are. March and I were talking about you.

> **丹恒：**你来了。我和三月正在聊你呢。

- ▸ You two missed me?

**Dan Heng:** Yeah, of course.

> **丹恒：**嗯，当然了。

**March 7th:** When did Dan Heng start expressing his feelings like this? I guess pigs really do fly!

> **三月七：**丹恒什么时候开始说话这么直白了啊？太阳打西边出来啦？

- ▸ Worried about me?

**Dan Heng:** A little. But March is obviously worrying too much.

> **丹恒：**有一些。但三月显然担心得有些多余了。

**March 7th:** Hey, what's that supposed to mean? I... I have a reasonable amount of concern for my companions!

> **三月七：**喂！什么叫多余啊！我那是…那是合理的同伴关怀！

- ▸ What about?

**Dan Heng:** You, me, and March. The three of us.

> **丹恒：**你、我还有三月。我们三个。

**March 7th:** Exactly! The power trio of this Express!

> **三月七：**没错，就是关于咱们列车铁三角的事儿！

**Dan Heng:** This trip to Planarcadia is the first time we weren't necessarily working together toward a common goal.

> **丹恒：**这趟二相乐园的旅行，似乎是头一回我们三个没有为共同的目标而行动。

**Dan Heng:** You dove into the Phantasmoon Games. I... crossed paths with that relentless old friend-and-foe again. As for March... Well, she spent her time wandering and eating. Her perfect little vacation...

> **丹恒：**你踏入了幻月游戏。我…和那位穷追不舍的敌友再度重逢。至于三月…她度过了一个逛吃逛吃的圆满假期……

**March 7th:** Wait, wait, hold up! How come all I get is "wandering and eating"? I grabbed plenty of comics as well!

> **三月七：**欸欸打住，怎么到我这儿就剩下「逛吃逛吃」啊？我还买了不少漫画！

**March 7th:** I call it cultural exploration, with a little extra time for me to recharge, of course! Right, (Trailblazer)?

> **三月七：**本姑娘这叫深入异乡文化，体验风土人情，顺便完成了非常重要的自我充电保养！对吧，开拓者？

**Dan Heng:** Now that we're having this little break, it hit me that I might have neglected you and March.

> **丹恒：**停下来的这个时刻，我意识到是不是疏忽了对你和三月的关心。

- ▸ You're overthinking it!

**March 7th:** Yeah, we are actually more concerned about your condition, Dan Heng!

> **三月七：**没错，我们其实更关心你的状态，丹恒！

- ▸ To us, this is just Trailblazing!

**March 7th:** Well said. This vacation has expanded my horizons and my stomach capacity!

> **三月七：**说得好。本姑娘的假期开拓了眼界和…胃容量！

- ▸ Dan Heng, we are peers!

**March 7th:** Why are you suddenly acting like the adult here!?

> **三月七：**突然之间装什么大人啊！

**Dan Heng:** Blade has accepted General Yao Guang's proposal. Perhaps he has finally moved on from past grudges and that Abundance curse, and is now ready to do something for himself.

> **丹恒：**刃接受了爻光将军的提案。也许意味着他已从过往的恨意或丰饶诅咒中醒来，打算为自己做些什么了。

**Dan Heng:** It's going to be a path fraught with danger, so I had hoped to accompany him for a bit longer, but he refused.

> **丹恒：**这条路九死一生，我本想陪他走一程，但他拒绝了。

**Dan Heng:** From now on, I will turn my attention to this world.

> **丹恒：**接下来，我也要把目光放在这个世界上了。

- ▸ What are you going to do next?

**Dan Heng:** Mr. Yang and I have been asked by General Yao Guang to help with setting up some defensive formations.

> **丹恒：**我和瓦尔特先生要应爻光将军之请做些布阵护卫的工作。

**March 7th:** I've got work to do too. Mr. Ashveil wanted someone to accompany him to the tavern to investigate clues about Asat Pramad. Yours truly figured she'd been resting long enough, and it was time to stretch her legs, so I volunteered.

> **三月七：**我这边也是，不死途先生希望有人和他一同前往酒馆调查归寂的线索，本姑娘寻思休息了那么久，也该活动活动身子骨了，就主动请缨。

- ▸ Can't believe we're splitting up again...
- ▸ Stay safe. Don't make me worry too much.

**March 7th:** No problem! When you come back, you have to share everything you saw in the World in Canvas!

> **三月七：**嗯！等你回来，也要给我们讲讲画中世界的见闻啊！

### Talk to Himeko and get ready to set off

[Recap] Pearl would arrange everything you needed to reach the World in Canvas, and Himeko asked you to give her regards to her ancestor, Graphia. A special ship would take you from Seafeld City to Inkford Hermitage.

*(Talk to Himeko)*

**Himeko:** Just in time, (Trailblazer). I've talked to Madam Pearl.

> **姬子：**你来得正好，开拓者。我和真珠女士谈过了。

**Himeko:** She will handle all the arrangements for your passage, so you can enter the World in Canvas to find my ancestor Graphia.

> **姬子：**关于绘世先祖和画中世界的事。她会为你安排好一切通行事宜。

- ▸ Pearl is so efficient!

> ▸ 真珠的效率真高啊。

**Himeko:** This is an important matter. Furthermore, your journey gives her the chance to uncover something she's been yearning to understand for years: the secret to Graphia's art of imagenesis.

> **姬子：**事关重大，更何况，你的这场旅行意味着她能触碰到自己渴望已久的秘密——绘世幻造技艺的秘密。

- ▸ When should I set off?

> ▸ 我什么时候能出发？

**Himeko:** Actually, I hoped you could take some time to rest first.

> **姬子：**知道吗，我更希望你能好好休息。

**Himeko:** Once you've fully rested, you may make your way to the dock in Seafeld City.

> **姬子：**当你休息好之后，可以随时启程前往海原市的码头。

**Himeko:** Madam Pearl has a special ship waiting for you there.

> **姬子：**真珠已经安排好，那里会有一艘特别的船在等候你。

**Himeko:** It will take you to the border between this world and the World in Canvas, a place known as "Inkford Hermitage."

> **姬子：**它将载你前往此世与画中世界的边界，一个名为「渡画泉隐」的地方。

**Himeko:** That's your final stop before entering the World in Canvas, a place to gather your supplies and rest up before you set out on the journey ahead.

> **姬子：**那是你在进入画中世界前的最后一站，也是最后的补给与休整之所。

**Himeko:** I know you'll have to go alone this time, but I don't think it'll be dangerous. After all, that realm was created by Graphia, my ancestor.

> **姬子：**我知道，这一次需要你独自行动。但我认为那儿应该不会危险，毕竟是绘世先祖创造了那方天地。

**Himeko:** The thought of it makes me feel calm, in a strange but comforting way. If you get to meet her, please give her my regards.

> **姬子：**想到这一点，我甚至有一丝奇妙的安心。如果见到先祖，请代我致意。

- ▸ Himeko, you're not coming with me?

> ▸ 姬子不和我一同前往吗？

**Himeko:** I am indeed quite curious. I'd love to see this ancestor of mine for myself and hear her story in her own words.

> **姬子：**我很好奇，想见见这位先祖的模样，也想亲口问问她的经历。

**Himeko:** But someone must stay here and keep watch. Madam Pearl wishes to confer with me and General Yao Guang about our next steps in dealing with Asat Pramad. So, I'll be entrusting this mission into the World in Canvas to you.

> **姬子：**但这里也需要有人守望。真珠想与我、还有爻光将军共同商议应对归寂的下一步计划，因此，这趟画中世界之旅就要拜托你了。

- ▸ I will send your regards to Graphia.

> ▸ 我会把你的问候带给绘世的。

**Himeko:** Please tell her that one of her descendants became a Nameless on the Express.

> **姬子：**请告诉她，她的后代成为了列车的无名客。

**Himeko:** Go ahead. Set out once you've rested up. I'll be here with Welt and the others, awaiting good news from you.

> **姬子：**去吧，当你休息够了，就出发吧。我会和瓦尔特他们一起，在这里等着你的好消息。

## 197　Living Sacrifice for Gods and Mortals　神人共食的生贽

> 世界：Planarcadia　|　区域：Seafeld City　|　角色：Ashveil; Blade; Dan Heng; Evanescia; Kuchiba; Pearl; Silver Wolf; Welt; Yao Guang　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Illusion of the End'' completed

### Head to Seafeld City and continue the adventure

**Silver Wolf:** In other words, that woman wants you to take Shuhu and jump straight into the Voracity, and leave the rest to the Aeon's stomach acid?

> **银狼：**也就是说，那女人要你带着倏忽跳进「贪饕」体内，剩下的交给星神的胃酸？

**Blade:** ...

> **刃：**……

**Silver Wolf:** And... this is between me and him, right?

> **银狼：**还有，这是我跟他的事吧？

**Silver Wolf:** Why are you tagging along?

> **银狼：**你怎么也跟来了？

**Ashveil:** Relax. I'll say my goodbyes and be gone.

> **不死途：**别急，道个别就走。

**Ashveil:** "A dignified funeral," huh... If you ask me, this ending is indeed grand enough.

> **不死途：**「一场体面的葬礼」…要我说，这结局算得上轰轰烈烈。

**Silver Wolf:** I don't care about any of that.

> **银狼：**我不在乎这些有的没的。

**Silver Wolf:** There's only one thing I care about. Does he have to die?

> **银狼：**我只关心一件事，他就非死不可？

**Ashveil:** Here's a piece of advice, little cub.

> **不死途：**狼崽，给你句忠告。

**Ashveil:** Whether he lives or dies is his choice. That's the one thing you don't get to decide for him.

> **不死途：**是生是死，看他自己。唯独这件事，别替他做主。

**Blade:** ...It has landed.

> **刃：**…落下来了。

**Silver Wolf:** What?

> **银狼：**什么？

**Blade:** Nothing. Just a seabird, landing on the rocks.

> **刃：**没什么。一只海鸟落在了礁石上。

**Blade:** A blade forged of mortal steel cannot sever an undying fate.

> **刃：**凡铁铸就的剑，斩不断不死的宿命。

**Blade:** If Shuhu is to be put down, the options are few, and that Leviathan is one of them.

> **刃：**要置倏忽于死地，选择不多，那头古兽是其中之一。

**Ashveil:** In time, people will say this: The culprit of the Zhuming gave his life for the greater good and plunged into the abyss alongside the abomination.

> **不死途：**日后人们会说：朱明案犯舍生取义，与孽物一同投入万丈深渊。

**Blade:** I'm not as noble as you make it sound.

> **刃：**我没你说得这么高尚。

**Blade:** Many years ago, you invited me to join the Galaxy Rangers. I refused for one reason...

> **刃：**许多年前，你邀请我加入巡海游侠，那时我拒绝，原因无他——

**Blade:** I'm a petty man. I was never born to carry some greater good.

> **刃：**我心胸狭隘，生来不是心怀大义的人。

**Blade:** This is between Shuhu and me. Nothing more.

> **刃：**这是我和倏忽的恩怨，仅此而已。

**Ashveil:** Hah... Can't argue with that. A Galaxy Ranger through and through.

> **不死途：**呵，没有错。这就是天生的巡海游侠。

**Ashveil:** Well then, I won't say anything more.

> **不死途：**那，我也不多说什么了。

**Silver Wolf:** Blade... you sure about this?

> **银狼：**刃…你真的想好了？

**Blade:** For years, death has clung to me like a curse etched into my bones.

> **刃：**多少年来，死亡于我，如附骨之疽。

**Blade:** If there is a chance to make a god feel the same pain, I would do it, even if this body is reduced to ash.

> **刃：**若有机会让神明感受相同的痛苦，即便将此身付之一炬，我也乐意至极。

**Blade:** As for everyone else, there's no need to drag them into this.

> **刃：**至于旁人，没有牵连的必要。

**Blade:** We'll proceed as Yao Guang proposed. Bury Shuhu at the end of the World in Canvas, taking me with him, and let the Voracity dissolve us both.

> **刃：**就按爻光的提案：连我一同，将倏忽葬在画中世界的尽头，由「贪饕」销熔吧。

**Silver Wolf:** ...

> **银狼：**……

**Ashveil:** ...Take care.

> **不死途：**…保重。

**Silver Wolf:** How about you stay as well? You might as well see it through to the end with us.

> **银狼：**要不你别走了？送人送到底，陪我们走一遭也好。

**Ashveil:** Planarcadia has bigger problems that need my attention.

> **不死途：**二相乐园还有更大的麻烦要我去处理。

**Ashveil:** Your instincts were right. That "meanie" pulling strings from the shadows is none other than Asat Pramad.

> **不死途：**你的直觉是对的：一直暗中使绊子的那混球，背后就是归寂。

**Silver Wolf:** Who else would want to kick Aha's head like a ball?

> **银狼：**想把阿哈的头当球踢的，除了他，还能有什么人？

**Ashveil:** So, I need to get back and finish my job.

> **不死途：**所以，我该去完成本职工作了。

**Silver Wolf:** Investigating a Lord Ravager sounds like a job you don't come back from.

> **银狼：**调查绝灭大君，听着是个有去无回的单子。

**Ashveil:** Therefore it also pays very well.

> **不死途：**自然会有笔不菲的报酬。

**Silver Wolf:** Then here's hoping you live long enough to collect your payment.

> **银狼：**那祝你活着拿到报酬。

**Silver Wolf:** ...

> **银狼：**……

**Blade:** ...

> **刃：**……

**Silver Wolf:** Tch. Every time. When it's just the two of us, you go quiet.

> **银狼：**啧，每次都这样。跟我独处的时候，你就不说话了。

**Blade:** You know what I'm going to say.

> **刃：**你知道我会说什么。

**Blade:** ...The seabird's flown off.

> **刃：**…海鸟飞走了。

**Blade:** It's time for us to go too.

> **刃：**我们也该走了。

### Head to Lookout Cloud Station and continue your adventure

> *Heavenly lights patrol the sky, earthly spirits guard the land. Horologers divine our fate, the Great Void mirrors the soul. By the mystic decree of the Yuque, I entreat the Purple Imperial Enclosure: May Reignbow's light shine, piercing the ten directions wide. I command you to traverse the Path of Three Lights, overseeing the traces of good and evil across the land. Through the gloom and shadow, shatter every blight. Shine on the lost and blinded, ferry them to light. Deca-Light Reflection Barrier, so mote it be!*

**Evanescia:** "The terror slumbering at the heart of Planarcadia is..."

> **绯英：**「沉睡在二相乐园世界核心的恐怖之物是——」

**Evanescia:** "Oroboros."

> **绯英：**「奥博洛斯」

**Yao Guang:** After the Swarm Disaster, the Voracity vanished, never to appear again for more than seven hundred Amber Eras.

> **爻光：**寰宇蝗灾后，「贪饕」便销声匿迹，七百多个琥珀纪未曾现身。

**Yao Guang:** THEIR last rumored sighting was five hundred years ago.

> **爻光：**最后一次出现在传言中，也是五百年前的事。

**Pearl:** History Fictionologists claim that Oroboros lies in wait at the edge of the cosmos, guarded by THEIR children, awaiting the day of THEIR return.

> **真珠：**虚构史学家声称，奥博洛斯潜伏在银河边境，由祂的子嗣看守，静待回归之日。

**Yao Guang:** I've heard that story before, but it wasn't about the edge of the cosmos. It was Pier Point...

> **爻光：**这故事我听过，但说的不是银河边境，而是庇尔波因特——

**Yao Guang:** They say that the Preservation is nothing more than a facade, and the IPC is, in truth, a cult devoted to the Voracity.

> **爻光：**他们说，「存护」只是表面上的伪装，公司是信仰「贪饕」的势力。

**Pearl:** Organic life has quite the imagination. They've said other things, too: that Louis Fleming seeks to ascend as the Aeon of Wealth, that the Council of Mundanites are mere hounds of Dr. Primitive, that the Marketing Development Department colludes with the Destruction, turning a blind eye to the spread of Stellarons...

> **真珠：**有机生命热爱幻想。他们还说过，路易斯•弗莱明企图成为「财富」星神，庸众院是原始博士的爪牙，市场开拓部更与「毁灭」勾结，纵容星核流通……

**Pearl:** Such rumors are endless. I believe the general should be able to tell which are true and which are not.

> **真珠：**流言不胜枚举，孰真孰假，相信将军能够分辨。

**Yao Guang:** I've never heard the last one. So let's assume it's the only one that's true.

> **爻光：**唯独最后一条，我没听说过，就当只有它是真的吧。

**???:** Come... Come... Come here... Come to my side...

> **？？？：**来吧，来吧，来这里，来我身边……

**???:** Just one more step, one more...

> **？？？：**只需向前一步，再一步……

**Yao Guang:** Such terrible breath. Once this Leviathan awakens, the arcadia's destruction would be all but instantaneous.

> **爻光：**好大的口气。这古兽一旦苏醒，恐怕乐园覆灭只在旦夕之间。

**Pearl:** For now, this remains conjecture. The sealed remains appear to be a husk that Oroboros once shed.

> **真珠：**目前仍是猜想：这具被封印的残骸，疑似奥博洛斯的兽蜕。

**Pearl:** Why it's here and how it's connected to the Elation remain unknown.

> **真珠：**它为何会出现在这里，和「欢愉」又有何渊源，暂且不得而知。

**Pearl:** Yet even a fragmented remnant of an Aeon is a threat that can't be ignored. The calamity of the Leviathan must not be allowed to re-emerge in this world.

> **真珠：**但星神的半了残躯，是不容忽视的威胁。决不能让古兽之灾重现世间。

**Yao Guang:** Naturally, that is why I'm here. And it's about time for the two Nameless to arrive.

> **爻光：**当然，本座正是为此而来。算算时候，二位无名客也该到了。

**Yao Guang:** Oh, you're just on time.

> **爻光：**喔，来得正是时候。

**Welt:** General Yao Guang, as instructed, warding talismans have been placed at over a dozen sites across Planarcadia.

> **瓦尔特：**爻光将军，二相乐园十余处地点，已照你嘱咐摆上了镇物。

**Yao Guang:** My thanks. It was Mr. Yang's talent of creation out of nothing that allowed us to reproduce dozens of plumes, and complete the deployment with such speed.

> **爻光：**多谢。全凭瓦尔特先生一手无中生有的妙法，幻造出数十枚翎羽，我方才能迅速完成布设。

**Yao Guang:** Now, with yin and yang aligned, and all four aspects in place, as long as we set up the last Formation Core...

> **爻光：**眼下，阴阳俱合，四相齐备，只需填上这最后一处阵眼——

**Yao Guang:** The Deca-Light Reflection Barrier will be complete.

> **爻光：**「十方光映法界」便可告成。

**Dan Heng:** Using the divine eye of the Spiritus to weave a giant web, one that ensnares countless possible futures... General, have you truly made up your mind?

> **丹恒：**借助威灵神眼，编织一张网罗万千可能的巨网…将军果真下定决心了？

**Yao Guang:** Though Shuhu has been eradicated, the signs of doom have not faded. On the contrary, they grow ever clearer.

> **爻光：**倏忽虽已诛除，死兆却未消退，反而愈加清晰。

**Yao Guang:** After much consideration, the source can only be this restless "corpse."

> **爻光：**思来想去，源头也只能是这蠢蠢欲动的「尸体」了。

**Dan Heng:** One crisis has yet to pass, and another is already upon us. The arcadia's leylines were already riddled with scars. And now even the ancient tree's seal is starting to loosen.

> **丹恒：**一波未平，一波又起。乐园的地脉本就千疮百孔，现在古木封印也开始松动了。

**Dan Heng:** While Evanescia is asleep, someone must take up her duty to guard this place.

> **丹恒：**绯英沉睡期间，得有人替她接过看守的职责。

**Yao Guang:** But defending isn't the way of The Hunt. If you don't want to become the prey, you must become the hunter.

> **爻光：**但一味防守可不是「巡猎」的做派。不想沦为猎物？只能先当猎人咯。

**Welt:** General, do you intend to go on the offensive and suppress the remains of the Voracity?

> **瓦尔特：**将军是要主动出击，镇压「贪饕」遗骸？

**Yao Guang:** How could I? The Joyseeker could barely confine that husk using the Abundance's Ambrosial Arbor as its cage. I'm not that arrogant as to place myself alongside the Gods.

> **爻光：**怎么会？常乐天君借丰饶建木，才堪堪将这兽蜕关入牢笼。本座还没狂妄到自比神明。

**Yao Guang:** I'm not here to guard against an Aeon, but against a lesser menace...

> **爻光：**此番前来，防的不是星神，而是小人——

**Yao Guang:** Lord Ravager Asat Pramad. This Barrier is prepared for him.

> **爻光：**绝灭大君「归寂」，这法界是为他准备的。

**Pearl:** Asat Pramad has been meddling with the game from the shadows, his intentions unclear. We know little about him or the Voracity.

> **真珠：**归寂暗中扰乱游戏，意图不明。对于他和「贪饕」，我们都知之甚少。

**Pearl:** By chance, however, a "friendly citizen" has provided us with intel...

> **真珠：**但机缘巧合，一位「热心市民」递来了情报——

**Kuchiba:** Oroboros's remains are in Ahatopia, aren't they?

> **朽叶：**奥博洛斯的神骸就在哈托彼亚，对吗？

**Pearl:** ...

> **真珠：**……

**Kuchiba:** Silence is an answer in itself. I've seen that reaction too many times.

> **朽叶：**沉默也是一种肯定。类似的反应，我见过太多。

**Pearl:** I'm not surprised that you are the first to discover it. You were in the "torn scroll," too. The probability of you noticing was 24%.

> **真珠：**第一个发现的人是你，我不意外。你当时也在「残卷」，察觉的概率是：24%。

**Kuchiba:** That's a little low, don't you think? I'd put it at well over ninety.

> **朽叶：**未免有些低了，至少在九成以上吧？

**Pearl:** What I don't understand is this: There were countless ways to verify your hypothesis, yet you chose the most unconventional one.

> **真珠：**但我不明白，验证猜想的方法有很多，你却选了最反常的一种。

**Pearl:** You bypassed security and broke into the building to confront me in person. What is your objective?

> **真珠：**不惜越过安保，闯入大厦与我对峙，目的是什么？

**Kuchiba:** This is no confrontation, it's a warning, Madam Pearl. I'm a security officer, and there is only one reason I'm here.

> **朽叶：**这不是对峙，而是警告，真珠女士。我是一名治安官，来见你的原因只有一个。

**Kuchiba:** While everyone else is fixated on the Phantasmoon Games, only the Department of Aberration Defense is watching other places.

> **朽叶：**当所有人都被幻月游戏吸引，只有异常防御部会将目光投向别处。

**Pearl:** Like the location where the New Herald of Death case was closed?

> **真珠：**比如，「新告死魔」事件的结案地点？

**Kuchiba:** Correct. The incident involved four Daybreak Warriors, three Nameless, one detective, and one officer...

> **朽叶：**没错。事件涉嫌：破晓战士四人，无名客三人，侦探一人，治安官一人……

**Kuchiba:** But just moments ago, a tenth set of footprints appeared at the scene.

> **朽叶：**但就在刚才，现场出现了第十组脚印。

**Pearl:** After the investigation, we learned that the traces were left deliberately. A blatant provocation.

> **真珠：**经过调查，痕迹是故意留下的。直白的挑衅。

**Yao Guang:** The Lord Ravager bent on destroying the Elation came uninvited. Make no mistake: Planarcadia is the stage he chose with care, and drawing the Abundance into the fray was hardly a whim.

> **爻光：**毁灭「欢愉」的大君不请自来。不必多说，二相乐园是他精心挑选的舞台。挑拨「丰饶」入场，绝不只是心血来潮。

**Welt:** Our top priority is to dispose of Shuhu's flesh properly to prevent it from falling into the wrong hands and causing another calamity.

> **瓦尔特：**当务之急，是妥善处理倏忽血肉，以免落入他手，再生祸端。

**Dan Heng:** That said, we cannot afford to ignore Asat Pramad's next move.

> **丹恒：**但归寂的下一步行动也不可不防。

**Yao Guang:** This giant web is prepared for that very purpose. Once the entire planet is folded into the divination's trajectory, under the gaze of the omniscia, all possibilities will be laid bare.

> **爻光：**这张巨网正是为此准备。当整颗星球被纳入卜算的轨迹，法眼之下，无穷的可能将一览无遗。

**Yao Guang:** When that time comes, he won't wish to leave any footprints behind.

> **爻光：**待到那时，他不会希望自己留下脚印。

**Pearl:** One final question: Why did the Lord Ravager choose to visit this place?

> **真珠：**最后一个问题：绝灭大君为什么要拜访这里？

**Pearl:** Does he have an ulterior motive, or is this merely some twisted joke?

> **真珠：**别有所图，还是单纯的恶作剧？

**Yao Guang:** That question should be left to the professionals. Don't forget, we have a detective who can't seem to rest.

> **爻光：**专业的事，就交给专业的人吧。别忘了，我们还有一位闲不下来的「侦探」。

**Yao Guang:** Let's hope the accumulation of variables can tip the scales in our favor.

> **爻光：**但愿诸多变数累加，能让天平倒向这边哪。

**"Golden Teacher":** And so, the people set out, and the gondola bound for distant lands set sail.

> **「黄金教师」：**于是，人们出发了，远行的贡多拉起航了。

**"Golden Teacher":** At the end of the world, the Elation once again declared war upon the Voracity...

> **「黄金教师」：**在「世界尽头」，「欢愉」又一次对「贪饕」宣战——

**"Golden Teacher":** "Ke-ke-ke! Frail little humans!" Oroboros split THEIR mouth wide open!

> **「黄金教师」：**「桀桀桀！弱不禁风的人类！」奥博洛斯咧开大嘴！

**"Golden Teacher":** "Whoever can fill my whole mouth with but a single thing shall receive a reward: my tongue!"

**"Golden Teacher":** The bartender Bragi opened the wine cellar, yet THEY only grew drowsy, drifting off to sleep.

> **「黄金教师」：**酒侍「布拉琪」打开酒窖，祂却困得打起了盹。

**"Golden Teacher":** And Enroute? He crept up to the Voracity's ear and told THEM a corny joke. The Voracity pondered it for ten Amber Eras, then burst into laughter at THEIR own amusement!

> **「黄金教师」：**「未抵」呢？他跑到贪饕耳边，讲了一个冷笑话。「贪饕」思考了整整十个琥珀纪，被自己逗乐了！

**"Golden Teacher":** Enroute spread his hands and said: "See? I filled your mouth with laughter."

> **「黄金教师」：**未抵摊开手：看，我用「笑声」填满了你的嘴巴。

**March 7th:** Uh... Then what happened?

**"Golden Teacher":** Then? Then the Voracity said:

> **「黄金教师」：**然后？然后贪饕说——

> **「黄金教师」：**「你耍我呢？！」便张开血盆大口，把未抵啃了个稀巴烂！

**March 7th:** What kind of nonsense is that!

> **三月七：**什么乱七八糟的！

**"Golden Teacher":** Those are the facts, my inquisitive auditor.

> **「黄金教师」：**事实就是这样，好学的旁听生。

**"Golden Teacher":** If you want to hear more stories, you're welcome to discuss with your classmates!

> **「黄金教师」：**如果你想知道更多故事，欢迎和同学们交流！

**March 7th:** We came here to investigate the Voracity and Asat Pramad, but all we got were stories that only a three-year-old would believe!

> **三月七：**咱们是来查「贪饕」和归寂的，结果这里只有三岁小孩才会信的故事。

**Ashveil:** Rumors don't just appear out of thin air. It's just a matter of how much truth you think they hold.

> **不死途：**传闻总归不会无中生有，就看你觉得里面有几分真。

**March 7th:** At least ninety percent of that was made up.

> **三月七：**至少九成是编的吧。

**Ashveil:** Sometimes, that last ten percent is all you need.

> **不死途：**有一分真，往往也足够了。

*(Quick read)*

How to Poke an Aeon and Get Away

*A book signed by "Scarecrow" that records tales of Masked Fools who dared to offend Aeons. They view this as a rite of passage in a Fool's career, rushing headlong into it one after another, never tiring of the thrill.* Pay-Per-Second, One-Day Tour of the Amber World Borrowing some poor sap's iris, the Fool "Shapeshifter" donned a sharp suit and boarded a shuttle at Pier Point's skyport. He flashed a warm smile at every passenger in the cabin, billionaires wealthy enough to rival nations, monarchs of galactic belts, board members of industry-monopolizing foundations, all of whom had emptied their coffers for a single pilgrimage: an audience with the Amber Lord. The shuttle pierced through the debris disk, drawing near the Amber Lord's colossal form. THEY had stood there since before sentient life was born, and would likely remain long after sentient life had vanished. Following the countless marks left over the eons on the Subspace Crystalline Barrier, some prayed in silence, some wept softly, while the Amber Lord offered no response. Who would notice a single speck of dust in the vast expanse of space? The Fool carved "Shapeshifter was here" into the barrier, then took his leave without a second glance. Remnant Dream of the Primeval Imperium, Into the Mouth of Voracity Oroboros, the ancient Leviathan that survived the Dusk Wars, was said to have chased Aha all the way to the roots of the Imaginary Tree. A Fool named "Enroute" attempted to confirm the truth of this tale, but alas, he couldn't run as fast as Aha and was caught by the Voracity and swallowed whole. Who would've guessed that inside the Voracity's belly lay a wondrous world of its own? The Voracity was so massive that the lives previously devoured had established their own kingdoms on THEIR stomach walls: thumb-sized dwarves, clever horse-folk, talking comet cicadas... Enroute explored this place with delight and encountered fellow swallowed Fools, Dorothy, Bragi, and Shepherd. Together they adventured, found the Voracity's heart and gave it a good kick, and were belched out into the universe with a mighty retch! Enroute quickly plucked off the Voracity's shadow and spread it over the Fools like a blanket. Shepherd then tossed out a comet, and the dim-witted Oroboros was drawn to its light. The Fools finally escaped with their lives. After that, countless Fools sought the treasures within the Voracity's belly, but none ever caught so much as a glimpse of the Voracity again. "Everyone is Idrila!" After the Aeon of Beauty vanished, the Fool "Davin" made a proclamation at the tavern, instantly attracting a massive following. Chanting "Everyone is Idrila," they sparked an artistic storm and launched a reckless charge toward pushing the boundaries of the Path of Beauty. For example, they stuck bananas to agates with polyvinyl chloride tape, scribbled graffiti on every household item in sight and proclaimed them great works of art, and even found time to fight a few copyright lawsuits with the IPC. That was, until they arranged space junk across an entire planet's surface to form Idrila's visage, at which point the Knights of Beauty finally rounded them all up. However, after seven days and seven nights of deliberation, the hastily convened roundtable of Knights of Beauty ultimately decided to release the Fools, since to punish them, they would have to acknowledge that these depictions actually resembled Idrila. Afterword: Say No to Aha Having come this far, it seems I must tell my own story. The night before I finished this piece, a visitor casually strolled into the tavern, sat down beside me, and ordered us each a "Bomb's First Drop." THEY didn't introduce THEMSELF, but I already knew who THEY were. THEY said: "Buddy, I hear you're writing a little book. I've read it — quite interesting. But there are a few points worth discussing..." I listened patiently as THEY spoke, and rejected every single change THEY proposed. THEY downed the last drop of the bomb in one gulp, then left the tavern. THEIR retreating figure looked utterly forlorn.

**March 7th:** This feels completely made up... but, uh, thanks anyway...

**"Scribblenibs":** As for me, I'm just the one who keeps the books circulating.

> **阮•梅：**反应意外地平静呢。也对，毕竟你右手的「影子」也是证据之一。

**Ruan Mei:** Why don't you tell everyone? What happened to the Galaxy Rangers back on Vonwacq?

> **阮•梅：**不妨和大家说说吧，当年在翁瓦克，巡海游侠经历了什么？

**Ashveil:** Vonwacq...

> **不死途：**翁瓦克……

**Tingyun:** Sprightly Vonwacq. I've heard there's a peculiar phenomenon that occurs on that rainforest planet.

> **停云：**生命的翁瓦克，小女子听闻那颗雨林星球有一种奇特的现象。

**Tingyun:** Plants multiply and grow, and are then harvested like wild grass, a cycle repeating every few decades...

> **停云：**植物繁衍生长，然后像野草一样被收割，数十年一循环……

**Tingyun:** Almost identical to the Phantasmoon Games.

> **停云：**和「幻月游戏」相差无几。

**March 7th:** But how are the two connected? When you hear "plants" and "growth," shouldn't that make you think of Abundance?

> **三月七：**这是怎么联系上的？说起植物、生长，不应该想到「丰饶」吗？

**March 7th:** Huh, wait...

> **三月七：**咦，等等……

**Tingyun:** It seems you've figured it out.

> **停云：**看来你意识到了。

**Tingyun:** By coincidence, Abundance is another thing the two worlds have in common.

> **停云：**恰巧，「丰饶」也是两个世界的共同点。

**Ruan Mei:** When coincidences pile up, they cease to be coincidences. Mr. Ashveil...

> **阮•梅：**偶然过多，就不是偶然。不死途先生……

**Ruan Mei:** No, leader of the Rangers, Mr. La Mancha, perhaps you could explain this to our Nameless friend?

> **阮•梅：**不，游侠之首，拉曼查先生，为这位无名客解释一下吧？

**Ashveil:** Back on Vonwacq, I'd already long since stopped being a Ranger.

> **不死途：**翁瓦克那会儿，我早就不是游侠了。

**Ashveil:** Your conjecture is basically correct, Miss Ruan Mei. You're not the only genius to take life as a subject of study.

> **不死途：**你的猜想基本正确，阮•梅女士。拿「生命」当课题的天才，也不止你一位。

**Ashveil:** By the time I arrived, the atavistic experiment was already approaching its final phase. Dr. Primitive had set his sights on an ancient Leviathan...

> **不死途：**我赶到那里时，返祖实验已经进入倒计时。原始博士的目标是太古巨兽……

**Ashveil:** Beneath Vonwacq's surface, there also lay the remains of the Voracity.

> **不死途：**翁瓦克地底，也有一具「贪饕」残骸。

**Ruan Mei:** You stopped him.

> **阮•梅：**是你阻止了他。

**Ashveil:** The Rangers stopped him. Back then, Mister N was still human.

> **不死途：**是游侠们阻止了他。那会儿，老白还是人类。

**Ruan Mei:** Now, deep beneath Ahatopia, another corpse of the Leviathan lies buried.

> **阮•梅：**如今，哈托彼亚地下深处，同样埋葬着古兽尸骸。

**Ruan Mei:** If I'm not mistaken, there are countless other worlds carrying out the same ritual...

> **阮•梅：**如果我没猜错，还有数之不尽的星球，正在进行相同的仪式——

**Ruan Mei:** Aha sealed Oroboros's body among the stars, suppressed it with Abundance, siphoned the power of the Path of Voracity... and called it a "game."

> **阮•梅：**阿哈将奥博洛斯的身躯封入群星，以「丰饶」镇压，又以「游戏」的名义，汲取「贪饕」命途的力量。

**Ruan Mei:** This is what people call the Phantasmoon Games.

> **阮•梅：**这就是所谓的「幻月游戏」。

**March 7th:** Aha? Why would THEY do such a thing?

> **三月七：**阿哈？祂为什么要这么做？

**Ruan Mei:** You may remember, the Express once delivered a Leviathan's carcass to me. That wasn't the only one in my collection.

> **阮•梅：**你或许还有印象，列车曾为我捎来一具古兽遗骸，那不是我唯一的收藏。

**Ruan Mei:** By arranging remains from different eras in sequence, I discovered something rather intriguing.

> **阮•梅：**将不同时期的遗骸依序排列，我发现了一则有趣的事实。

**Ruan Mei:** At a certain moment over a thousand Amber Eras ago, just as Akivili began traveling in pursuit of THEIR ideal...

> **阮•梅：**一千多个琥珀纪前的某一刻，阿基维利刚开始为祂的理想旅行时……

**Ruan Mei:** All creations of the Voracity lost the same organ: their taste buds.

> **阮•梅：**所有「贪饕」造物都失去了同一种器官，味蕾。

**Ashveil:** What does that imply?

> **不死途：**这说明什么？

**Ruan Mei:** The purpose of consumption changed. Be they Spawns, Depth Crawlers, or Void Song Whales, their genetic markers encoding gustatory organs vanished entirely. Creatures walking the Path of Voracity became nothing more than biological black holes, consuming without discrimination.

> **阮•梅：**进食的目的改变了。无论衍体、渊兽还是虚空鲸，遗传信息中记录味觉器官的因子不复存在。行走在「贪饕」命途上的野兽，变成了只知吞吃的生物黑洞。

**Ruan Mei:** This was not natural evolution. Through the Simulated Universe, I identified the point of divergence in history: A concept had disappeared from the Path of Voracity.

> **阮•梅：**这绝不是自然演化的结果。我通过模拟宇宙，找到了历史的分歧点：一种概念从「贪饕」命途中消失了。

**Ruan Mei:** Consider this: What do taste buds provide?

> **阮•梅：**各位不妨想想，味蕾能带来什么？

**March 7th:** Yummy flavors?

> **三月七：**好吃的滋味？

**Ruan Mei:** Precisely.

> **阮•梅：**没错。

**Ruan Mei:** Savoring something's "aftertaste" is an act of "elation."

> **阮•梅：**「回味」，属于「欢愉」的行为。

**March 7th:** So Aha stole... the original Primum Mobile of the Voracity?

> **三月七：**阿哈偷走了…「贪饕」的原动力？

**Ruan Mei:** The Simulated Universe proves nothing on its own. But the primal desires of life have always been entangled.

> **阮•梅：**模拟宇宙不能证明什么，但生命的原始渴望，本就彼此纠缠。

**Ruan Mei:** Think of it as my intuition: The conflict between the Aeons of Elation and Voracity stretches all the way back to primordial times.

> **阮•梅：**就当是我的直觉吧：「欢愉」和「贪饕」，两位星神的恩怨在太古时期就已结下。

**Ashveil:** How many people know about this?

> **不死途：**有多少人知道这个秘密？

**Ruan Mei:** There used to be two. I only told Yu Qingtu. Now there are five. After this, I can't be sure.

> **阮•梅：**以前是两个人，我只告诉了余清涂。现在是五个。以后就说不准了。

**Ashveil:** Exploit the old conflict between Aha and Oroboros to ignite a divine war, or allow intersecting Paths to devour one another.

> **不死途：**利用阿哈和奥博洛斯的梁子掀起神战，或者让交错的命途吞并彼此。

**Ashveil:** Of the three known ways to kill an Aeon, the arcadia fulfills at least two. Now who still thinks Asat Pramad is here on vacation?

> **不死途：**杀死星神的三种办法，乐园至少占了两项。现在，不会有人觉得归寂是来度假的了。

**Ruan Mei:** But you still haven't explained where it came from.

> **阮•梅：**不过，你还是没交代它的由来。

**Ashveil:** You mean the "shadow"?

> **不死途：**你说「影子」？

**Ashveil:** It has nothing to do with Vonwacq.

> **不死途：**它跟翁瓦克无关。

**Ruan Mei:** ...Oh?

> **阮•梅：**…哦？

**Ashveil:** Call it a twist of fate. This isn't my first encounter with the Astral Express, nor my first time dealing with a Lord Ravager.

> **不死途：**算是孽缘吧，我不是第一次和星穹列车打交道了，也不是第一次对付绝灭大君。

**Ashveil:** When my home was destroyed by Zulo, I had a brief encounter with a former Navigator.

> **不死途：**故乡被「诛罗」毁灭的时候，我和一位前领航员有过一面之缘。

**March 7th:** A Navigator of the Express?

> **三月七：**列车的领航员？

**Ashveil:** Yes. Starseer "Isee". He'd already retired by then.

> **不死途：**嗯，观星者「我见」。那时他已经隐退了。

**Ashveil:** He was the one who told me about the "shadow." According to Isee, it was one of many remnants left behind when the Express first carved paths along the edges of the cosmos.

> **不死途：**「影子」的事，是他告诉我的。按我见的说法，这是列车开垦宇宙边境时，留下的众多遗产之一。

**Ashveil:** I led the Rangers to suppress the shadow and devised the plan to assassinate Zulo. But I never learned why it was there in the first place.

> **不死途：**我带领游侠镇压了「影子」，制定了刺杀诛罗的计划。但它为什么在那里，我并不知情。

**Ruan Mei:** ...

> **阮•梅：**……

**Ruan Mei:** Don't you find it strange, Mr. Ashveil?

> **阮•梅：**不死途先生，你不觉得奇怪么？

**March 7th:** Strange?

> **三月七：**奇怪？

**Ruan Mei:** When coincidences pile up, they cease to be coincidences. Like an experiment with results that are too perfect, it's enough to send a chill down your spine.

> **阮•梅：**偶然过多，就不是偶然。就像过于完美的实验曲线，总会令人毛骨悚然。

**Ruan Mei:** If you don't mind, let's walk through the whole thing once more.

> **阮•梅：**不介意的话，我们重新梳理一遍逻辑。

**Ruan Mei:** Earlier, what did you two uncover in the tavern?

> **阮•梅：**此前，两位在酒馆查到了什么？

**March 7th:** Someone suspected to be Asat Pramad knew about the Voracity's secret. Before disappearing, he said he was headed for the end of the world.

> **三月七：**疑似归寂的人知道「贪饕」的秘密。消失前，他说要去世界的尽头。

**Ruan Mei:** And then, who did he say the "shadow" belonged to?

> **阮•梅：**然后，他刚刚说「影子」是谁的遗产？

**March 7th:** A former Navigator. The "shadow" was a remnant left behind when he charted the cosmos.

> **三月七：**一位前领航员，「影子」是他开垦宇宙时留下的遗产。

**Ruan Mei:** ...

> **阮•梅：**……

**Ruan Mei:** If the "World's End" isn't the tavern, then where is it?

> **阮•梅：**如果「世界尽头」指的不是酒馆，那会是哪里？

**March 7th:** I don't know if anyone can answer that question. Not even Akivili...

> **三月七：**这个问题，恐怕没人能回答吧。就连阿基维利也不知道……

...

**March 7th:** Wait, don't tell me...

> **三月七：**不会吧，难道……

**Ashveil:** Enroute only goes where he isn't welcome...

> **不死途：**未抵只会前往不欢迎他的地方……

**Ashveil:** After leaving the tavern, he went to the Astral Express.

> **不死途：**离开酒馆后，他去了星穹列车。

**???:** The detective game is over.

> **？？？：**侦探游戏结束了。

## 199　Best Farewell to the World　告别世界的最佳方法

> 世界：Planarcadia　|　区域：Seafeld City　|　角色：Asat Pramad; Casper; Aeon ★ Aha; Sudscour; Trailblazer; Feld; Graphia　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Arcadia of Mystery and Terror'' completed

### Head to the dock and meet up with the reception staff

> *The last stop before entering the World in Canvas is a place called Inkford Hermitage. According to Pearl's itinerary, a ferry will be waiting to pick me up at the Seafeld City dock.*

**(Trailblazer):** (Pearl said she would arrange a ferry to pick us up at the Seafeld City dock.)

> **开拓者：**（真珠说，她会在海原市码头安排渡船接引。）

**(Trailblazer):** (The place we're going to holds clues about Graphia? Really?)

> **开拓者：**（接下来要去的地方有「绘世」的线索…真的假的？）

*(Approach Feld)*

**Reception Staff:** Mr./Ms. (Trailblazer), it is an honor to... well, shall I say, be of service to you again?

> **接应的专员：**开拓者先生/小姐，很荣幸能…呃，算是再度为您服务吧。

**Reception Staff:** To be honest, when Madam Pearl invited you to visit the Pearluxe Tower, I was supposed to be your guide. I ended up being replaced by a total fraud.

> **接应的专员：**其实真珠女士邀您参观珠星大厦时，本该是我接待的，结果被个骗子顶替了。

**Agent Feld:** My name is Feld. By order of Madam Pearl, I will be escorting you to your destination.

> **菲尔德专员：**我是菲尔德。奉真珠女士之命，接引您前往目的地。

- ▸ Thank you, Mr. Feld.

> ▸ 有劳了，菲尔德先生。

**Agent Feld:** You are too kind. I followed the battle in Seafeld City very closely. Serving a hero such as yourself is a true privilege.

> **菲尔德专员：**您太客气了！海原市的大战我全程关注了，能为您这样的英雄服务，是我的荣幸。

**Agent Feld:** I assume Madam Pearl has already briefed you on our destination?

> **菲尔德专员：**真珠女士应该向您介绍过此行的目的地了吧？

- ▸ No.

> ▸ 没有。

- ▸ She only told me to come here.

> ▸ 她只是让我来这儿。

**Agent Feld:** I see. It so happens that Madam Pearl is tied up with an unforeseen event, so allow me to brief you in her stead.

> **菲尔德专员：**明白。突发事件让真珠女士分身乏术，就由我来为您说明吧。

**Agent Feld:** The destination of our boat journey is a hotel known as "Inkford Hermitage."

> **菲尔德专员：**我们要登船前往的地方，是一座名叫「渡画泉隐」的酒店。

**Agent Feld:** It is situated at the end of a Dovebrook tributary that ultimately flows into the sea. It is one of the points where the World in Canvas intersects with reality.

> **菲尔德专员：**它位于鸽川入海支流的尽头处，是「画中世界」与现实的交界点之一。

- ▸ What does this have to do with Graphia?

> ▸ 这和绘世有什么关系？

**???:** Well, Graphia's former residence was located there.

> **？？？：**因为那里曾是绘世故居的所在地。

- ▸ Graphia lived there?

> ▸ 绘世住在那儿？

**???:** Yes. To be precise, she lived there a thousand years ago.

> **？？？：**没错，但应该说，她千年前在那儿住过。

**Nihilux:** Legend says the miraculous encanvasing of the mortal world happened there. Would Madam Pearl, a die-hard fan, pass up a sacred site like that?

> **虚照：**传说中，「凡世入画」的神迹就是在那儿完成的。像真珠小姐那样的铁杆粉丝，能放过这种风水宝地吗？

- ▸ Nihilux! When did you get here?

> ▸ 虚照！何时来的？

- ▸ Miss Colt, it's you again!

> ▸ 马老师？又是你！

**Nihilux:** Word is that Madam Pearl just flicked her wrist, signed a check with an astronomical figure, and bought the whole plot of land. She also snatched up every single piece of Graphia's "lost works" she could find, without bothering to verify their authenticity.

> **虚照：**听说真珠大人玉手一挥，在支票上刷刷签下一串天文数字，买下了整块地，连带把市面上所有真假难辨的绘世遗作也都打包买下了。

**Nihilux:** Whew... I guess money really does let you do whatever you want.

> **虚照：**啊…有钱真的可以为所欲为啊。

**Nihilux:** That's not even the half of it. I don't know where her obsession comes from, but she's dead set on the idea that Graphia is still alive. If she gets even a whiff of anything remotely related to Graphia, she's got her people on it at the snap of a finger.

> **虚照：**这还不算完呢。不知她哪来的执念，铁了心认定「绘世还活着」，于是撒开人马，把和绘世沾边的流言全部掘地三尺……

**Nihilux:** But all they could find were imagenae knock-offs: random, pretty red-heads based on nothing but imagination.

> **虚照：**结果呢，找到的尽是些冒牌幻造种：人们按照想像，画出来的红发美人儿。

**Nihilux:** I bet her reaction went something like this: "Objective: Locate the original. Error: Detecting excessive quantity of low-quality merchandise."

> **虚照：**我猜她一定是这反应…「目标：寻找本尊。错误：发现大量劣质周边。」

**Agent Feld:** You! I remember you! You're that fraudster from Pearluxe Tower!

> **菲尔德专员：**你！我认得你！你就是珠星大厦那个骗子！

**Nihilux:** Pearluxe Tower!? I've never set foot in that place in my life!

> **虚照：**珠星大厦？！我这辈子就没去过那儿啊！

**Agent Feld:** Oh, I know it's you! I've seen the footage. You're wearing the exact same outfit!

> **菲尔德专员：**就是你没跑了！我看过录像，你连衣服都没换一身！

**Nihilux:** Argh... A slip-up in an otherwise perfect plan! Relax, your job is safe this time.

> **虚照：**…可恶，百密一疏！别激动，我今天可不是来抢你饭碗的——

**Agent Feld:** Cut it out, you scoundrel! You can't talk your way out of this! Who exactly are you?

> **菲尔德专员：**混蛋，该讲的都被你讲了！不该讲的你也全都说了！你到底是谁啊？

- ▸ Allow me to introduce her.

> ▸ 我来介绍一下。

- ▸ This is the author of The Fluffy.

> ▸ 这位是绒绒号的作者。

**(Trailblazer):** This is the author of Fluffy Across the Blue, Blurry_QR_Colt.

> **开拓者：**这位是《苍天航路绒绒号》的作者，模糊二维马老师。

**Agent Feld:** Huh... Hmm... Is that so.

> **菲尔德专员：**呃…啊…这样子啊。

**Agent Feld:** My apologies, how rude of me. Miss Colt, it's an honor to finally see the face behind the work. Please pardon my yelling. I didn't know.

> **菲尔德专员：**失敬失敬。马老师，无缘得见真容。刚刚是我说话太大声了，您不介意吧？

**Nihilux:** Hehe, no worries, no worries.

> **虚照：**嘿嘿，好说好说。

- ▸ Are you headed to Inkford Hermitage as well?

> ▸ 你也要去渡画泉隐？

**Nihilux:** Well yes. Would you believe it if I said I was going on vacation?

> **虚照：**没错。去度假，你信吗？

- ▸ Who are those two big fellas behind you?

> ▸ 你身后那两个壮汉是？

**Nihilux:** It's only normal for a famous comic artist to travel with bodyguards, no?

> **虚照：**知名漫画家带俩保镖，很合理吧？

**Bodyguard A:** Miss Blurry_QR_Colt, let's be clear. You are being transported under escort.

> **保镖甲：**模糊二维马老师，请认清现状！您当前的状态为「在押运途中」。

**Bodyguard B:** Your current credit balance is in the negative. Madam Pearl has offered you a lifeline by asking you to complete the next volume of The Fluffy.

> **保镖乙：**您的资产信用额度为：负数。真珠女士给了您一条活路，完成下一卷绒绒号漫画。

**Nihilux:** Whoa, this is not my fault. Remember Alloy King Pom-Pom from Pearluxe Tower? I tried to put everything on Pearl's tab, but she found out and charged it back to me.

> **虚照：**这不赖我，记得珠星大厦那个「合金帕姆王」吗？我想把这笔钱赖在真珠账下的。结果被她发现，又原封不动算我头上了。

**Nihilux:** Who knew a figurine could be so expensive? It cost me more than my entire savings.

> **虚照：**谁知道一个手办这么贵，存款完全不够用。

> ▸ 你居然还有存款？

**Nihilux:** Hey, I pay for my own meals. Is that so shocking to you? Is it?

> **虚照：**一顿饭钱还是有的，值得你这么惊讶吗？值得吗？

- ▸ Oh right, so, it's mine then?

> ▸ 所以，赖我咯？

**Nihilux:** No way! It's obviously... all Madam Pearl's fault!

**Nihilux:** I lost every single penny!

> **虚照：**血本无归啊！

**Agent Feld:** So you're escorting her to... uh, shall we say, an "intensive creative retreat"?

> **菲尔德专员：**所以，你们这是要押送她去…呃，封闭式创作？

> **保镖乙：**猜的不错。目标：清空稿债。预计周期：56天。

**Nihilux:** Hear that, (Trailblazer)? It's not a hotel. It's hell! Where you get whipped for not finishing your scripts on time!

> **虚照：**听到了吗，开拓者，那不是酒店，那是地狱，地狱啊！画不完稿要挨鞭子的地狱！

> ▸ 你就自求多福吧。

**Agent Feld:** The boat has arrived. When you're ready, let's begin our journey to Inkford Hermitage. Please, mind your step as you board.

### Wait for the ferry to dock

> *The last stop before entering the World in Canvas is a place called Inkford Hermitage. According to Pearl's itinerary, a ferry will be waiting to pick me up at the Seafeld City dock.*

**Agent Feld:** We have arrived. This is the snowy shore of the canvas world, Inkford Hermitage.

> **菲尔德专员：**我们到了——飘雪的画之畔，渡画泉隐。

**(Trailblazer):** Are those wispae floating on the water?

> **开拓者：**水上飘着的这些，是尘灵吗？

> **尘灵：**大！愿力！大！明星！

**Agent Feld:** Oh dear, there are so many wispae. They are blocking the boat's route.

> **菲尔德专员：**我的天，尘灵太多，航线堵塞了。

**Agent Feld:** But no need to panic. Please follow my lead.

> **菲尔德专员：**不过二位不用慌张，请跟随我指引前进。

### Take the ferry to Inkford Hermitage

> *"It takes ten years of good karma to share the same boat." Little Raccoon, it's destiny! So please, for Aha's sake, put in a good word for me with Pearl and get her to spare me—*

*(Enter Interplanar Jump!)*

(In the background)

**Feld:** Come on, let's catch up with (Trailblazer)!

**Nihilux:** Being mobbed by fans is just part of a hero's destiny!

*(Exit Interplanar Jump!)*

**Nihilux:** Phew, a safe docking. For a second I thought we were going to hit an iceberg or something.

**Nihilux:** Hmm. I was imagining a Benzaitengoku-style manor or something.

> **虚照：**等等，我还以为这里会是座古弁才天国样式的庄园呢……

**Nihilux:** So you demolished your idol's old residence... and replaced it with a luxury hotel?

> **虚照：**结果，你们是把偶像故居铲平，盖了座豪华酒店？

**Agent Feld:** Of course not. Benzaitengoku vanished more than a thousand years ago. The original structure is long lost to time. The IPC only chose this location because this is one of the weakest points between reality and the World in Canvas.

> **菲尔德专员：**您说笑了。古弁才天国已经消亡千年，原貌早已无人知晓。公司驻扎此地，只因这里是现实与画界最薄弱的交界点之一。

**Agent Feld:** Many imagenae are born every day. But just as many ancient lineages of imagenae hide themselves away in the World in Canvas once their Wishpower dry up, or as they grow weary of the mortal world.

> **菲尔德专员：**每天都有许多幻造种诞生，也同样有古老谱系的幻造种因愿力枯竭或厌倦尘世，选择将画中世界作为遁世的桃源。

**Agent Feld:** This is the final stop before they enter into the World in Canvas, and this is where they bid farewell to reality. They rest here, organize memories spanning centuries, and then pass beyond the point of no return.

> **菲尔德专员：**这里，便是他们启程入画、告别现实的最后一站。他们在此暂歇，整理跨越百年的记忆，然后踏上永不回头的路。

**Agent Feld:** Rumor has it that Graphia's soul appears from time to time to guide these weary imagenae on their path.

> **菲尔德专员：**有传言说，绘世的灵魂会时不时出现，接引厌倦尘世的幻造种。

**Nihilux:** There's a sense of parting in the air. No wonder the soul of the Mother of Imagenesis feels an attraction. For a creator, this must be the finest well of inspiration.

> **虚照：**空气中充满了告别的气氛，也难怪能吸引那位幻造之母的灵魂。对创作者来说，这就是最好的素材库吧。

**Agent Feld:** Miss Colt, now that you're in this "well" too, you ought to make the most of it. Let's go, I'll lead the way.

> **菲尔德专员：**马老师，既然到了素材库，您就加油吧。走吧，我来为各位引路。

### Go to the hotel lobby

> *Finally off the ferry. Let's go over my main mission — find Graphia and deliver Evanescia's warning. The staff at Inkford Hermitage have been stationed here long-term, so they might have some intel on Graphia. Miss Colt, here's hoping your ideas flow like a spring, inspiration never leaves you, your brush glides effortlessly, and you work at lightning speed... so you can finish those overdue drafts ASAP!*

*(Approach the marked location)*

**(Trailblazer):** Is that...?

> **菲尔德专员：**听说它和海原飞岛，还有幻月秘庭中的神木同出一系。

**???:** Honored guests, my apologies for the late welcome...

> **卡斯佩：**前•漫画家，虚照女士哟。

> **卡斯佩：**上头的大人物打过招呼哟…您现在是珠星资产…要在这儿住到画完下一卷为止…哟呵呵。

**Casper:** Hehe... Every guest in this hotel is an imagenae with quite a history.

> **卡斯佩：**呼呼，这座酒店里…每一位住客都是很有来头的幻造种…哟。

**Casper:** That Arcadian over there, that's a former superhero.

> **卡斯佩：**那边那位，乐园人，是前•超级英雄…哟。

**Casper:** That one over there is one of the oldest strangelings. They're a former kappa. These days, they work as the hot springs manager.

> **卡斯佩：**那边那位，是最古老的妖怪之一，前•河童。它现在是温泉管理员…哟。

**Nihilux:** A kappa? Didn't that lineage of imagenae go extinct when the IPC first arrived?

> **虚照：**河童？这一支幻造种，不是在公司刚来的时代就消亡了吗？

**Casper:** There are still a few survivors that slipped through the net.

> **卡斯佩：**旧时代幸存的漏网之鱼也是有的哟。

**Casper:** As for Casper myself, I am a former Frost Giant!

> ▸ 有没有前•绘世的消息？

> **卡斯佩：**卡少我哟，曾经是个又高又大又威风的巨人哟…当然，是在烬土纪元哟。

**Nihilux:** So you're basically an antique who knows a bunch of old secrets? What corner did they dig you out of?

> **虚照：**那你岂不是个知道许多历史秘密的老古董？他们是从哪个角落把你挖出来的？

> **卡斯佩：**是冰块哟！可惜，时间会让大冰块化成小冰块。很多朋友的名字…想不起来了哟。呜呜，从那时候起，也再没有吃到焦脆焦脆的虚卒了…哟。

**Casper:** That... is a secret.

- ▸ Any info on the "former" Graphia?

> ▸ 我是现•无名客。

**Casper:** Sightings of Lady Graphia are as common as the snowflakes here!

> **卡斯佩：**绘世大人的目击报告，多得像这里的雪花哟！

**Casper:** Ever since the news spread that her soul would reside at the hotel, all those imagenae who mimic her and the weirdos who insist they have a connection to her have flocked here!

> **卡斯佩：**打从她的灵魂会入驻酒店的消息传开，那些模仿她的幻造种、硬要和她扯上关系的怪家伙，全都凑过来了哟！

**Casper:** You see, they all claim they have some sort of connection to her, or hold some secret about her.

> **卡斯佩：**看看，这些人都宣称自己和绘世有关，要不就是掌握着绘世的秘密。

> **虚照：**我看这帮人只是想骗点愿力和钱花花吧，就像许多人信誓旦旦地说自己是阿哈一样。

**Casper:** You're not wrong... but whenever the higher-ups ask for a report, little ol' Casper here can always pad it out and hand over a long list of names.

> **卡斯佩：**是没错啦…但上头要报告的时候，卡少我总能交出一长串名单应付过去了…呼哟。

**Casper:** Nameless, good luck with your inquiries. Bye now.

> **卡斯佩：**无名客，好好打听哟，告辞。

**Grafea:** I am a wispae formed from the concentrated longing for Graphia in Planarcadia...

> **绘使：**我是五十二集大型动画剧集「烬灭天魔•机甲大战」里的装甲•绘使。

**Graphea:** Care to witness my signature move, Dance of the Inkblade?

> **绘使：**要不要给你来一招「墨舞破锋杀」？

> **绘诗：**我见过太多即将耗尽愿力的幻造种来到这里，祈求幻造之母能挥笔延续生命，但从来没人成功过。到最后，这儿也只剩下靠着绘世招牌吃饭的我们了。

> **绘时：**毕竟烬土纪元距今千余年呢。可能留下的，只有对绘世的憧憬了啵……

- ▸ Why have you choosen to stay in the real world?

> ▸ 太好了，多多益善！

**Sudscour:** It is hard to leave one's homeland. Even when my kin have entered the canvas, even when the waters here are no longer clear, and even when the new Ploo tribe has replaced us...

> **垢尝：**虽然很久很久以前，哈托彼亚每一条河流、每一片池塘，都有我族栖息。我们为绘师们莳弄庄稼，绘师为我们绘画后代。

**Sudscour:** This is still my home. It needs someone who remembers the old days. As long as I remain, the kappa line is alive.

> **垢尝：**故土难离啊。纵使族人都已入画，纵使河水已不再清澈，纵使新生的「噗噜」一族取代了我们……

- ▸ Do you know Graphia?

> ▸ 没一个真的就算了…

Upon hearing that name, the withered body of Ahatopia's last kappa begins to tremble faintly.

**Sudscour:** Lady Graphia is the one who guided all imagenae. Her name shines like moonlight through my dimming memories.

> **垢尝：**此地仍是故土，需有记得往日之人。只要我还存在一天，「河童」一族就没有消逝。

**Sudscour:** Back then, the Demons From Beyond swept in like a dark tide. The filth and flames they brought could turn even the clearest stream into a toxic swamp.

> **垢尝：**绘世大人…是引领所有幻造种的存在。她的名字，像月光照亮了老朽昏暗的记忆。

**Sudscour:** We kappa were weak and could only shiver at the bottom of the rivers. But it was Lady Graphia... who found us. She encouraged us, saying that every drop of clean water held power against the darkness.

> **垢尝：**那时，界外天魔的军团像黑潮般涌来，它们带来的污秽和火焰，连最清澈的河水都能染成毒沼。

**Sudscour:** She taught us how to use water to protect the displaced mortals and wash away those impure monsters. Though our impact was limited, that was the most heroic era for my kind.

> **垢尝：**我们河童力量弱小，只能躲在河底瑟瑟发抖。是绘世大人…她找到了我们，是她鼓舞我们，说每一滴洁净的水，都是对抗黑暗的力量。

- ▸ What was Graphia like?

> ▸ 没一个有胆。

**Sudscour:** Legend says that when she was a painter, she was a woman of radiant beauty.

> **垢尝：**她教我们如何用水庇护流离失所的凡人，冲刷那些不洁的怪物。虽然效果有限，但那是我族…最像英雄的时代了。

**Sudscour:** But in my fading memories, on the battlefield, she was a warrior with flowing red hair and pigment-stained armor.

> **垢尝：**传说，她作为画师时，是一位容色艳丽的佳人。

**Sudscour:** Some say she fought like a demon, but to me, her eyes shone brighter than the smoothest river stone.

> **垢尝：**但在老朽模糊的记忆里，在战场上…她是红发披散、身染颜料的战士。

- ▸ Is she really still alive?

> ▸ 绘世真的入住过这里？

**Sudscour:** As long as the World in Canvas exists, Lady Graphia must as well.

> **垢尝：**有人说她战斗起来形如厉鬼，但要我说，她的眼神亮过最光滑的鹅卵石。

**Sudscour:** There is a saying that if one who is weary of the world prays to her with true devotion, she will arrive on a ferry to lead them into the canvas.

**Sudscour:** If you seek clues regarding Lady Graphia, you should visit the mountain behind the hotel.

> **垢尝：**传说只要厌世者诚心向她祈愿，她便会渡筏而至，引人入画。

**Sudscour:** There are "atonement statues" left by imagenae there, filled with their prayers. It is also the site of Graphia's former residence.

> **垢尝：**客人，如果想寻找绘世大人的线索，可以去酒店后山看看。

**Sudscour:** Casper likes to wander around there. He is a strangeling far older than I. Perhaps if you play in the snow with him, he might show you the way.

> **垢尝：**那山坡有幻造种留下的还愿像，载满往日祈愿，也是绘世故居旧址。

- ▸ Thank you.
- ▸ Farewell.

**Sudscour:** That is all I can tell you. After all, I am just a stubborn old kappa trying to keep this one pool of water a little cleaner.

> **垢尝：**卡斯佩总喜欢去那儿闲逛。他是个年纪比老朽更悠久的妖怪。也许陪它玩玩雪，它可以为您指明方向。

*(Talk to Sudscour again, optional)*

**Sudscour:** That is all I can tell you. After all, I am just a stubborn old kappa trying to keep this one pool of water a little cleaner.

> **垢尝：**老朽能告诉您的，就只有这么多了。毕竟，我只是个想把眼前这池水弄干净一点的、固执的老河童罢了。

*(Talk to Nihilux)*

**Nihilux:** So you're here looking for Graphia's whereabouts on behalf of that Intellitron executive?

**Nihilux:** I think it's unlikely that the Mother of Imagenesis will see her.

- ▸ Why?

**Nihilux:** If she wanted to see her, it would already have happened. The IPC has practically turned the World in Canvas upside down.

**Nihilux:** You know this, right? Over 140 years ago, they brought in hackers from Punklorde and did everything they could to blast open the passage between reality and the World in Canvas.

**Nihilux:** I think disturbing her World in Canvas like that must have upset the Mother of Imagenesis.

- ▸ I received the request from a friend of Graphia's.

**Nihilux:** A friend of Graphia's? Just how long has that person been alive...?

**Nihilux:** So that friend wanted you to find Graphia. But they didn't tell you how?

Aside from a single parting request, Evanescia had no time to leave behind any tokens or guidance. But that's not to say she left nothing at all—

**Nihilux:** But since you're asking for a "bright idea"...

**Nihilux:** You must have heard that in a certain corner of the universe, there's a mysterious ritual that can summon historical figures to the present day...

**Nihilux:** The! Holy! Grail! War!

- ▸ I know.

> ▸ 您为什么选择留在现世？

- ▸ ...

**Nihilux:** Oh right, that was your big adventure in Penacony! How nice. I hope Planarcadia holds one too.

**Nihilux:** Then I don't need to explain the method. First, we need a Catalyst. Do you have Graphia's brush or palette? Doesn't look like it.

**Nihilux:** No matter! Miss Colt has got you covered. We use leyline resonance! This hotel is built on Graphia's former home. It still has a connection to her. Like that vibrant golden tree...

**Nihilux:** Wait, you aren't actually falling for this, are you?

- ▸ But you're absolutely right!

**Nihilux:** Hahaha... I was just kidding.

**Nihilux:** Honestly, I'm a chronic deadline-misser who can barely save her own skin. How am I supposed to help you?

**Bodyguard A:** I've heard enough. Break time is over, Miss Nihilux!

**Bodyguard B:** Now that you understand your situation, stop wasting time. Start drawing!

**Nihilux:** I just checked in and I have to work already!? I'm not some fully automated painting mechatron. You do know that, right?

**Bodyguard A:** A mechatron can run for hundreds of hours straight. You cannot. That's exactly why every second counts! Now, right this second, start increasing your value as a quality asset!

**Nihilux:** Hey! Wait! This is kidnapping! (Trailblazer)! Help me...

Nihilux is hauled away by two bodyguards, leaving nothing but her drawn-out cries for help echoing through the lobby.

**Casper:** It feels like I had snow ball fights every single day, a long time ago. Shaping the snow into balls and, hehe, flinging them at whatever strange intruders came barging in.

> **卡斯佩：**打…雪仗…？哦哟…这个游戏…这个名字……好亲切啊。

**Casper:** Well then, come at me, Nameless! I'm a snowball fight veteran, I won't lose.

### Ask Casper for information about Graphia

- ▸ I've had my fill of fun.

- ▸ Ring any bells?

> ▸ 想起些什么没有？

**Casper:** Phew... things from the Cindearth Age are still a bit blurry.

> **卡斯佩：**唔…烬土纪元的事嘛，还是模模糊糊的哟。

**Casper:** But I definitely met Lady Graphia! I had snowball fights with her!

> **卡斯佩：**但绘世大人，卡少确实见过的！我们还一起打过雪仗呢！

- ▸ What was she like?

> ▸ 她是个什么样的人？

**Casper:** She was a great painter who led us in snowball fights! She'd even paint portraits for everyone!

> **卡斯佩：**是个会带领我们打雪仗的大画家哟！还会给大家画肖像！

**Casper:** Back then, when we wanted to see her, we'd go to her doorstep, light the ground lamps, and shout "snowball fight!"

> **卡斯佩：**那时候，我们要是想她了，就会去她家门口，点亮地灯，大喊——打雪仗哟！

**Casper:** Then Lady Graphia would poke her head out the window upstairs, unfurl a scroll, and whoosh—slide right down onto the snow.

> **卡斯佩：**绘世大人就会从窗户探出头，把画卷从楼上铺下来，咻地滑到雪地里。

**Casper:** Then we'd have snowball fights against the Voidrangers together, under the giant moon.

> **卡斯佩：**在大大的月亮下面，我们一起和虚卒打雪仗。

**Casper:** If we won, I got to swallow the Voidrangers whole. Mmm, they were so crispy and tasty.

> **卡斯佩：**打赢了，卡少就能把虚卒吞进肚子。嗯，焦脆焦脆的，可香了哟。

- ▸ Can you remember anything else?

> ▸ 还能想起别的事情吗？

**Casper:** There was someone else. Just thinking about them makes my blood boil...

> **卡斯佩：**还有个一想起来，就让卡少心里燃起来的家伙哟。

**Casper:** He was a snowball fight master. I remember one time, eight of us teamed up to challenge him.

> **卡斯佩：**那可是个雪仗高手。记得有一回，我们一共八个人，约好了要一起去挑战他。

**Casper:** But that guy knocked us all down with his snowballs like it was nothing, all from so far away!

> **卡斯佩：**结果那家伙，在好远好远的地方，用雪球轻而易举就把我们全砸倒了哟。

**Casper:** Six of my friends were lying on the ground, feeling beat. Losing the game got everyone so down that no one uttered a word.

> **卡斯佩：**六个小伙伴，遗憾地躺在地上。输了游戏，大家心里很难过，都一声不吭的。

**Casper:** As for me, I think I got hit right in the neck. I saw my own giant body thud onto the ground, and my chin felt ice-cold.

> **卡斯佩：**而卡少我呢，好像被砸到脖子了哟。我看到自己巨大的身躯轰地倒在地上，下巴冰冰凉。

**(Trailblazer):** (Eight people... in the Phantasmoon Games? Perhaps "snowball fight" is a euphemism for the Cindearth Age wars...?)

> **开拓者：**（八个人…幻月游戏？如果「打雪仗」是指烬土纪元的战争……）

**(Trailblazer):** (So in that round of the Phantasmoon Games, Graphia must have fought a terrifyingly powerful opponent. It seems only she and Casper survived the fight.)

> **开拓者：**（那么，在那场幻月游戏中，绘世曾与某个强大的对手交战，最终只有她和卡斯佩幸存了下来……）

- ▸ Have you seen Graphia since?

> ▸ 后来，你还见过绘世吗？

**Casper:** I saw her once... just once.

> **卡斯佩：**见过一次…哟。

**Casper:** By then, Ahatopia had already been transported into the canvas. It was so quiet and lonely.

> **卡斯佩：**那时候，哈托彼亚已经进画里了，冷冷清清的。

**Casper:** I couldn't find my other six friends, but I remembered where Lady Graphia lived.

> **卡斯佩：**我找不到其它六个小伙伴了，但还记得绘世大人家住在哪。

**Casper:** So I went to her front door again, lit the ground lamps, and shouted: "Snowball fight!"

> **卡斯佩：**我就又去她楼下，点亮地灯大喊——打雪仗哟！

**Casper:** It felt like only a second had passed, just the blink of an eye, yet somehow it also felt like forever. At last, I saw her drift out.

> **卡斯佩：**好像只是一眨眼，又好像等了很久，我看到她飘飘悠悠地出来了。

**Casper:** She held me and cried, saying "I'm sorry" and "you've done well" over and over...

> **卡斯佩：**她抱着我流眼泪，嘴里说着对不起、辛苦了…哟。

**Casper:** Hmm, it was just one lost game. We could have just won it back some other time. But... that was the last I saw of her.

> **卡斯佩：**唉哟，不过是输了一场游戏，以后赢回来不就好了。只是…自那以后，她就再没出现过哟。

- ▸ Thank you, Casper.

> ▸ 谢谢你，卡斯佩。

**Casper:** I only shared some memories of a snowball fight. If it helped you, Nameless, then I'm happy.

> **卡斯佩：**只是聊了些打雪仗的回忆，能帮到无名客就太好了。

- ▸ You've done well all these years.

> ▸ 这些年，你辛苦了。

**Casper:** Why are you saying that too? It's making me feel all strange.

> **卡斯佩：**怎么连你也这么说…感觉怪怪的哟。

**Casper:** That's about all Casper can remember. If you're still up for fun and games, come find me for another snowball fight sometime!

> **卡斯佩：**卡少也只能想起这么多了。要是没玩尽兴，下次还能来找我打雪仗哟！

> **开拓者：**（所以，总结一下，卡斯佩总喜欢点亮地灯，在绘世的故居附近呼唤她。）

> **开拓者：**（地灯…要上哪找呢？）

Technically, you shouldn't be moving the set pieces here without permission. But in a crisis like this, who cares about a few measly hotel management regulations?

In any case, once you're done borrowing it, you can always play a couple rounds of snowball fights with Casper to make up for it.
