> **开拓者：**你说…你就是绘世？

**(Trailblazer):** (She really does look a lot like Himeko.)

> **开拓者：**（她长得…确实很像姬子。）

- ▸ Can you prove who you say you are?

> ▸ 你要怎么证明身份？

**Graphia:** Child, you were the one who lit the ground lamps, were you not? If memory serves, that was the secret signal my old friends used to call for me.

> **绘世：**如今我应邀前来，你竟然还质疑我的身份，怪哉。

**Graphia:** I simply answered the call, yet you doubt my identity. How strange.

> **绘世：**我想想——我知道你来找我的目的。

**Graphia:** Let me think... I believe I know why you've come to find me.

> **绘世：**幻月游戏的仲裁者托你转告我…我们曾联手对抗的敌人「界外天魔主」，又回来了。对吗？

**Graphia:** The arbitrator of the Phantasmoon Games asked you to tell me that our old enemy, the Demon Lord From Beyond, has returned. Am I correct?

> **绘世：**自从哈托彼亚入画后，绯英与我分别约定镇守现实与画界，此生不复相见。

- ▸ You know about that?

> ▸ 你连这个都知道？

- ▸ I'm starting to believe you really are Graphia.

> ▸ 我有点信你是绘世了。

**Graphia:** Since Ahatopia's encanvasing, Evanescia and I agreed to guard reality and the World in Canvas separately, never to meet again.

> **绘世：**千年里，我常常期盼能够与她以某种方式重逢。

**Graphia:** But over the past thousand years, I often hoped we might reunite in some way.

> **绘世：**想不到，重获音讯时没有嘘寒问暖，只有一场迫在眉睫的危机。

**Graphia:** Who would've thought, when word of her finally reached me again, there'd be no warm greetings, but instead only an impending crisis.

> **绘世：**开拓者，你现在一定有许多疑惑吧？

**Graphia:** (Trailblazer), you must have many questions.

> **绘世：**…嘘，你要问的实在太多了。不过，在为你解惑之前……

- ▸ What exactly happened a thousand years ago?

> ▸ 千年前，究竟发生了什么？

- ▸ Did you fight Asat Pramad?

> ▸ 你们曾经和归寂交手过吗？

- ▸ How do you know who I am?

> ▸ 你是怎么知道我的来历的？

- ▸ How have you lived for a thousand years? What about the curse?

> ▸ 你如何活过千年？诅咒呢？

- ▸ Pearl asked me to find out the secret to creating your World in Canvas.

> ▸ 真珠托我问你绘世的诀窍……

**Graphia:** My, you certainly have a lot of questions. But before I enlighten you...

> **绘世：**烬土纪元，那位「界外天魔主」曾化作友人的模样，混入反抗军中，造成了惨烈的伤亡。我需要确认你的身份，无名客。

**Graphia:** During the Cindearth Age, that Demon Lord From Beyond once took the form of a friend to infiltrate the resistance, causing catastrophic casualties. I must confirm your identity, Nameless.

**Graphia:** Do you carry a token from Evanescia?

> **绘世：**…哦，我发现你带着个或许足以证明你身份的东西……

- ▸ A token? I don't remember having anything like that.

> ▸ 信物？我不记得有这东西。

**Graphia:** Hmm, I see you're carrying something that might be proof enough...

> **绘世：**你是说，她为你绘画了一副谒者面具？

- ▸ If you mean something Evanescia gave me, there's only this.

> ▸ 要说绯英给的，只有这个……

**Graphia:** She painted a Supplicant's Mask for you?

> **绘世：**哼，粗陋的笔触，这不愧是她的手笔。

The red-haired girl reaches out and takes the mask.

- ▸ Hey, give it back. It's very important.

> ▸ 快还给我，那是很重要的东西。

- ▸ Does this mean you'll tell me everything now?

> ▸ 这下你该如实道来了吧？

The red-haired girl hands the mask back to you.

**Graphia:** The secrets you seek are many and tangled. Perhaps I should start by telling you a story.

> **绘世：**你想知道的秘密千头万绪，不妨让我为你讲个故事吧。

**Graphia:** A thousand years ago, the Legions of the Demons From Beyond descended upon Ahatopia, marking the beginning of the Cindearth Age.

> **绘世：**千年以前，界外天魔的军团降临哈托彼亚，「烬土纪元」由此开启。

**Graphia:** For the Destruction, taking a life is easier than brushing aside a feather. Among the countless victims, a baby girl was saved by a painter from the brink of death. He took her in as his daughter and named her Graphia.

> **绘世：**夺走生命，对「毁灭」来说，比吹落一根羽毛更简单。而在无数受害者中，一个垂死的女婴，幸运地被一位画师所救。她被画师收养为女，取名绘世。

**Graphia:** To protect his daughter, the painter kept her confined to their home. While the fires of disaster swept across the land, Graphia lived in seclusion.

> **绘世：**为了保护女儿，画师将她禁足家中。灾火燃遍大地，唯有绘世深居简出。

**Graphia:** Until one day, a falling star streaked across the dark sky, and a deafening roar shattered the silence of the wasteland.

> **绘世：**直到有一日，一颗流星划过幽暗的天际，震天的巨响撕破了荒土寂静。

**Graphia:** Unable to contain her curiosity, the young girl slipped out of the house to follow the sound.

> **绘世：**少女难耐好奇，悄悄溜出家门，循声寻找。

**Graphia:** And so she saw it: beneath the cold Phantasmoon, a "Tower of Infinity" stood on the mountain range at the edge of the horizon.

> **绘世：**于是她看到了，在冰冷的幻月之下，一座「无限的高塔」矗立在地平线尽头的山脉上。

**Graphia:** This "tower" had countless windows and rose section by section, slender and tall. More than an actual tower, it looked like a wounded iron dragon that had fallen to the earth.

> **绘世：**这座「高塔」有着数不清的窗口，纤细修长、节节攀升…比起塔，它更像是一条受伤坠落在大地上的铁龙。

- ▸ Is that the origin of the "Murata" name?

> ▸ 这就是「无量塔」的来由？

- ▸ Was that... a wrecked train?

> ▸ 这是…坠毁的列车？

**Graphia:** Indeed. That tower, which stood only for a moment, was the crashed Astral Express. And Graphia's family name, "Murata," came from the wondrous sight she saw.

> **绘世：**没错，这座只矗立片刻的高塔，正是坠毁的星穹列车。而绘世的姓氏「无量塔」也来自她所目睹的奇景。

**Graphia:** At the time, the wrecked train was surrounded by countless abominable monsters. Amidst these foul enemies, a few warriors in strange attire fought tirelessly, but they fell one by one, unable to withstand the endless tide of monsters.

> **绘世：**那时，在列车周围，盘桓着无数形象可憎的怪物。在怪物的包围中，寥寥几位奇装异服的勇士战斗不休，却不敌源源不断的敌潮，逐一牺牲。

**Graphia:** It was only much later that Graphia understood that those who fought and died for Ahatopia shared a common name...

> **绘世：**很久以后，绘世才明白，这些为了哈托彼亚而搏命参战的人，有一个共同的名字……

**Graphia:** The Nameless, who walk the path of the Trailblaze.

> **绘世：**行于「开拓」的无名客。

**Graphia:** That was the day that Graphia stepped into the mortal world. Her life as the Mother of Imagenesis began.

> **绘世：**从那一日起，绘世踏入尘世，开启了自己作为「幻造之母」的人生。

**Graphia:** In a long, drawn-out war fought at a severe disadvantage, she was forced to seek the aid of the Phantasmoon Games, attempting to use the mighty power of an Aeonic ritual to lock the terrifying Demon Lord From Beyond within a limited battlefield.

> **绘世：**在敌我悬殊的漫长战争中，她不得不寻求幻月游戏的助力——试图以星神仪式的伟力将恐怖的界外天魔主锁死在有限的战场中。

**Graphia:** This was also the first time in the history of the Phantasmoon Games that the eight Supplicants joined forces to confront a powerful enemy from beyond: the Lord Ravager, Asat Pramad.

> **绘世：**这也是幻月游戏史上首次八位谒者齐心协力，对抗自界外降下的强敌——绝灭大君「归寂」。

- ▸ Did she win?

> ▸ 她赢了？

- ▸ Did she lose?

> ▸ 她失败了？

**Graphia:** She lost. Graphia did not win the Phantasmoon Games, nor did she manage to kill Asat Pramad.

> **绘世：**她失败了。绘世既没有赢得幻月游戏，也没有杀死归寂。

**Graphia:** In the final days of the Cindearth Age, she gathered every ounce of Wishpower to paint the world into a scroll. Using herself as a nail to steady the canvas, she sealed the Lord Ravager outside.

> **绘世：**在烬土纪元的尾声，她汇聚所有的愿力，将世界绘入画中，以自身为钉、稳固画卷，将绝灭大君隔绝在外。

- ▸ She made a seal capable of shutting out a Lord Ravager?

> ▸ 能够隔绝绝灭大君的封印？

**Graphia:** Yes. As long as "the scroll" still enshrouds reality, he won't be able to lay a finger on the Leviathan's remains buried deep within Ahatopia.

> **绘世：**是的。只要「画卷」依旧包裹着现实，他就无从染指哈托彼亚深处的古兽遗蜕。

**Graphia:** The story is over. I have received Evanescia's message.

> **绘世：**故事讲完了。绯英的嘱托，我已收到。

**Graphia:** I also have a token I'd like you to give her. Please, let us journey together to the other side.

> **绘世：**我也有信物想托你转交她，还请你同我一起渡往彼岸。

- ▸ Are we leaving right now?

> ▸ 现在就出发？

**(Trailblazer):** So, are we leaving right now?

> **开拓者：**所以，我们这就出发？

**Graphia:** No, there is no need to rush. You have a friend here who is lingering with a heart full of farewells.

> **绘世：**不必急于前进，你还有一位同样怀着告别之心的朋友也在此处逗留。

**(Trailblazer):** You mean... Blade?

> **开拓者：**你是说…刃？

**Graphia:** Follow this path down to the riverbank of Myriad Wish Grove. You'll find many wispae gathered there.

> **绘世：**沿着这条道路向下，栽种着「千愿之荫」的河畔聚集着不少尘灵。

**Graphia:** When you are ready, choose a wispae. Whisper Graphia's name and your wish to cross over to the other shore, then release the wispae into the river.

> **绘世：**等你准备好了，就挑选一只尘灵，默念绘世的名字和渡往彼岸的心愿，将它放入河中。

**Graphia:** When the time comes, I will bring the ferry to deliver you into the canvas.

> **绘世：**届时，我会驶来渡船，将你们送入画中。

**(Trailblazer):** (Graphia vanished? How elusive.)

> **开拓者：**（绘世…消失了？真是神出鬼没。）

**(Trailblazer):** Anyway, I should head to the riverbank of Myriad Wish Grove first.

> **开拓者：**总之，先去「千愿之荫」的河畔吧。

*(Unlock Achievement Light the Lamps of Yesteryear)*

## 200　Primeval and Other Times　太古和其他的时间

> 世界：Planarcadia　|　区域：Inkford Hermitage　|　角色：Ashveil; Baiheng; Blade; Hanguang; Huaiyan; Jing Yuan; Jingliu; Kafka; Shuhu; Silver Wolf; Trailblazer; Graphia; Dan Feng; Dan Heng　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Best Farewell to the World'' completed

### Head to the riverbank of Myriad Wish Grove

> *The warning of the Lord Ravager's return has been delivered. But to retrieve the token I must pass on to Evanescia, I must accompany Graphia on a trip to the World in Canvas. Blade has also arrived at Inkford Hermitage. If we truly find the sealed maw of Voracity on this trip, I fear this will be my final journey alongside him. Should I feel relieved that his heavy burden will finally be lifted? Or... No, I don't know how to face this impending farewell.*

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Silver Wolf:** I've heard that in the same forest, giant trees will fight each other for nutrients...

> **银狼：**听说，在同一片森林里，大树会彼此争夺养分……

**Silver Wolf:** How about this: we just feed Shuhu to that tree in the Courtyard. It would save us a long trip.

> **银狼：**你说，有没有一种可能，咱们把倏忽喂给秘庭那棵树，就不用跑这一趟远路了？

**Blade:** ...

> **刃：**……

**Silver Wolf:** Say something.

> **银狼：**说话。

**Blade:** Didn't know you were a forestry expert.

> **刃：**…没想到你对森林这么了解。

**Silver Wolf:** I'm not joking. I'm serious.

> **银狼：**没跟你开玩笑，我认真的。

**Blade:** If the Elwood absorbed another Emanator of Abundance, it would likely bring about an even greater catastrophe.

> **刃：**吸收了另一尊「丰饶」令使，只怕神木本身会成为更大的灾祸吧。

**Blade:** Right now, there is no other option besides the Voracity husk.

> **刃：**此时此刻，除了「贪饕」兽蜕，别无可能。

**Silver Wolf:** ...

> **银狼：**……

**Silver Wolf:** If the Abundance grows too strong, the Ambrosial Arbor loses control, and the arcadia is doomed. If the Abundance is too weak, the husk spirals out of control, and the arcadia is doomed just the same...

> **银狼：**「丰饶」强了，建木失控，乐园完蛋；「丰饶」弱了，兽蜕失控，乐园也完蛋……

**Silver Wolf:** It's called an "arcadia", but the whole thing is built on a razor's edge. Does that mean the essence of the Elation is just dark humor?

> **银狼：**明明是乐园，却整个建在刀尖上，合着「欢愉」的本质是黑色幽默？

**Blade:** You're afraid.

> **刃：**你在害怕。

**Silver Wolf:** I'm not. I respect your choice.

**Silver Wolf:** ...

**Silver Wolf:** Aaarghh, fine! You're right! I'm afraid! So what?

> **银狼：**啊啊啊…是！没错，我害怕！那咋了？

**Silver Wolf:** You think I don't know what that Xianzhou person meant? "One-in-ten chance"? There is no chance at all!

> **银狼：**你以为我不知道那仙舟人的意思？什么「九死一生」…只有九死，没有一生！

**Silver Wolf:** Listen — I just can't stand by and watch one of my kin sacrifice himself.

> **银狼：**听好了——对「家人」的死无动于衷，我做不到。

**Blade:** Kin...

> **刃：**「家人」……

**Silver Wolf:** What else? Friend? Colleague? Playmate? The guy who carries my bags when we go shopping?

> **银狼：**不然呢？朋友？同事？陪玩？出门逛街时后边拎包的？

**Blade:** I suppose all of those fit.

> **刃：**都对吧。

**Silver Wolf:** Fit how!?

> **银狼：**对在哪？！

**Silver Wolf:** Sometimes, I can't tell if you're actually dumb or just playing dumb...

> **银狼：**有时候，看不出你是真傻还是装傻……

**Blade:** I hear you. Just assume it's the former. Simpler that way.

> **刃：**我听得见。你就当是前者，这样简单些。

**Blade:** My mind is made up. You should understand: misplaced emotions will only cause the mission to fail.

> **刃：**我心意已决。你应该明白：不合时宜的情绪，只会让任务功亏一篑。

**Silver Wolf:** You really are just playing dumb.

> **银狼：**…你果然在装傻。

**Silver Wolf:** Ugh... To be honest, I'm the same way.

> **银狼：**唉…其实，我也一样。

**Silver Wolf:** If I could see Firefly cured of her illness, Kafka finally finding the "heart" she's looking for, and (Trailblazer) reclaiming his/her future...

> **银狼：**如果能亲眼看见流萤治好她的病，卡芙卡找到梦寐以求的「真心」，让开拓者夺回自己的未来……

**Silver Wolf:** Then I'd be willing to pick up that "LV.999" cartridge too, even if... it means becoming nothing but a shadow of the Dark Zone.

> **银狼：**我也愿意拿起「LV.999」的卡带，哪怕…迟早变成黑域里的一道影子。

**Blade:** Don't. At the TV Tower, you already severely overspent your time. Don't touch that thing again.

> **刃：**说说就好。在电视塔，你已经严重超支时间。别再碰那玩意儿了。

**Blade:** Besides, why are you telling me this? You should tell them yourself.

> **刃：**何况，和我说这些干什么？你应该亲口告诉他们/她们。

**Silver Wolf:** Because just like you... I'm a total! Stuck-up! Softie! Happy now?

> **银狼：**因为我和你一样…是个死、傲、娇！！！行了吧？

**Blade:** Feel better?

> **刃：**舒服点了？

**Silver Wolf:** No. Unless you give me a sincere apology and a proper goodbye, we're not settled.

> **银狼：**没有。不诚心诚意和我道个歉、告个别，这事不算完。

**Blade:** Not settled... is that right?

> **刃：**不算完…吗？

**Blade:** Fair enough.

> **刃：**也对。

**Blade:** (Whether as "Yingxing" or as "Blade," my journey has reached its end.)

> **刃：**（无论是「应星」还是「刃」的人生，我都已走到尽头。）

**Blade:** (Before the final verdict...)

> **刃：**（在最后的决断到来前……）

**Blade:** (I should say a proper goodbye to my "kin.")

> **刃：**（我应该和「家人」…好好道个别。）

**Blade:** ...Who's there?

> **刃：**…谁？

- ▸ Hold your fire, it's me.

**Silver Wolf:** Next time, it'd be better if you didn't try to sneak up on us like that.

> **银狼：**下次，最好别偷偷摸摸靠近了。

- ▸ When did you get here?

**Silver Wolf:** We've been here for a while. If you'd come any later, you'd be looking for him inside the belly of Voracity.

> **银狼：**有一会儿了。你再迟点，就得去贪饕肚子里找他了。

- ▸ Sorry, didn't mean to eavesdrop.

**Silver Wolf:** I get it. It was a deliberate "accident."

> **银狼：**懂，是故意不小心的。

**Blade:** I knew you would come.

> **刃：**你果然来了。

- ▸ I found a way into the World in Canvas.
- ▸ I can call for a boat to teleport us into the Canvas.

**(Trailblazer):** I met the legendary Graphia. She told me a way to enter the World in Canvas.

> **开拓者：**我见到了传说中的绘世，她告诉我进入画中的方法。

**(Trailblazer):** If we simply chant her name and release a wispae into the river, she'll come to ferry us.

> **开拓者：**只要默念她的名字，将尘灵放入河里，她就会前来渡我们入画。

**Silver Wolf:** A legend from a thousand years ago... And she's actually still alive?

> **银狼：**千年前的传奇人物…居然还在线？

**Silver Wolf:** It really makes sense. After all, you two are practically the same age. Knowing that gives me a bit more hope that you can actually make it back.

> **银狼：**也不奇怪，毕竟你们之间也没差着几岁。这样一来，我对你能回来也有几分信心了。

**Blade:** Then let's begin.

> **刃：**那么，开始吧。

### Summon Graphia's ferry

*(Talk to Silver Wolf or Blade, optional)*

**Blade:** ...You are unusually talkative today. Are you trying to buy time?

> **刃：**…今天的你格外话多，是在拖时间么？

**Silver Wolf:** ...

> **银狼：**……

*(Enter Interplanar Jump!)*

**(Trailblazer):** Little one, I choose you.

> **开拓者：**小家伙，就决定是你了。

**Wispae:** Everyone, let's go find Graphia!

> **尘灵：**大家一起，去找绘世！

**Wispae:** World in Canvas, here I come!

> **尘灵：**画中世界，我来了！

**(Trailblazer):** That should do it, right?

> **开拓者：**这样，应该就行了吧？

**(Trailblazer):** I summon Graphia, Mother of Imagenesis. May she come forth and deliver us into the World in Canvas...

> **开拓者：**我在此呼唤幻造之母绘世，请她到来，将我们渡入画中……

**(Trailblazer):** No wispae were harmed in the making of this operation.

> **开拓者：**本次行动中，没有任何尘灵受到伤害。

*(Exit Interplanar Jump!)*

**Wispae:** Graphia, Graphia! Everyone, into the World in Canvas!

> **尘灵：**绘世，绘世！明星们，要进画！

**???:** How odd. This time, the visitors actually know of my existence?

> **？？？：**怪哉。这一次的访客…竟知晓我的存在？

**(Trailblazer):** (Strange... Why is the boat coming from downstream?)

> **开拓者：**（奇怪…船怎么是从下游来的？）

**Graphia:** I heard your call and have come by boat to guide you across.

> **绘世：**我听到了你们的呼唤，驱船前来引渡。

**Graphia:** Greetings, travelers who seek to leave the mortal world behind. It is a pleasure to meet you.

> **绘世：**初次见面，意欲了却尘缘的客人。

**Graphia:** Oh? Not an imagenae? But someone here to settle the past, and... family members seeing them off?

> **绘世：**哦？不是幻造种？而是决断旧事的人，以及…送行的家属？

**Silver Wolf:** I guess so.

> **银狼：**算是吧。

**Graphia:** ...The power of Abundance. What a nostalgic scent. There is something terrifying slumbering within you.

> **绘世：**…「丰饶」之力，令人怀念的气息。你身体里沉睡着可怕的东西。

**Blade:** I've heard that something lies at the end of the World in Canvas with the power to suppress it. Will you show us the way?

> **刃：**听闻画中尽头沉睡着足以镇伏它的东西，能否为我们指路？

**Graphia:** I see. You are better informed than I thought.

> **绘世：**看来，你们知道的不少。

**Graphia:** I understand. Time is of the essence. Please, step aboard.

> **绘世：**我明白了。时间不等人——几位，登船吧。

**Blade:** Thank you.

> **刃：**多谢。

**Blade:** The mountains lie distant, the waters still... and snow settles.

> **刃：**山远水静、飞雪渐止……

**Blade:** A fine moment to bring this weary life to its end.

> **刃：**真是个了却残生的好时节。

**Graphia:** Bring a weary life to its end? To my eyes, the power taking root within you is far from withering away.

> **绘世：**了却残生？依我看，在你身躯中滋长的力量，很难枯萎啊。

**Blade:** It's just a matter of time.

> **刃：**…时候未到罢了。

**Graphia:** The journey downstream is long. As is custom, I must collect your past stories as fare. I shall use them to paint the path forward, which will guide us to the other side.

> **绘世：**随波而下的水路还很漫长。依照惯例，我要收取您的往事作为渡航的船资，也用来绘画航路，抵达彼岸。

**Blade:** My past stories...

> **刃：**我的往事……

**Blade:** I am not much of a storyteller.

> **刃：**我不是讲故事的料。

**Graphia:** There is no need to weave your thoughts into a complete story. A single memory, a single breath... These too are parts of your past.

> **绘世：**不必把心中所想当作故事，就当是一次回忆，一次呼吸。

**Graphia:** Only by letting go of the past can you truly set forth with an open heart. Is that not so?

> **绘世：**放下往事，才能坦然踏上旅程，不是么？

**Silver Wolf:** Go ahead, say something. I'm curious too.

> **银狼：**随便说点吧，我也想听。

**Blade:** ...

> **刃：**……

### Board the ferry and head to the distant shore within the canvas

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Blade:** ...Then I shall begin with that lone island of Insumousu.

> **刃：**…那便从伊须磨洲的孤岛说起吧。

**Blade:** It was a desolate wasteland, untouched and forgotten. Desolate, cold. The perfect place to lay the dead to rest.

> **刃：**那是片无人问津的荒原。萧瑟、冷清，最适合收殓亡魂。

**Blade:** I planted rusted, broken blades in the wasteland as a mark of my departure. Little did I know that even after joining the Stellaron Hunters, I would find myself returning there time and again, bringing along a few new swords with me.

> **刃：**我把锈蚀的残剑种在那荒原上，当作启程的纪念。谁知道，加入星核猎手后，我还是时常会去到那里，捎上几把新剑。

**Blade:** Their owners, some of them sought my life, only to fall by my hand. Others simply wished to see me one last time as their own lives flickered out.

> **刃：**它们的主人，有些想要我的命，却死在我手里；有些，只为在垂垂老矣之际见上我最后一面……

**Blade:** I was once a craftsman. And those swords... They were all my own creations.

> **刃：**我曾是一名匠人，而那些剑…都是我的作品。

**Blade:** I raised those wordless monuments for my old friends, one by one, until they became a forest of broken blades.

> **刃：**我为旧友竖起一座座无字碑，一座碑林。

**Blade:** And what about... me?

> **刃：**那…我呢？

**Blade:** And my sword... When will it finally join them in that forest?

> **刃：**我的剑，又能何时葬入那片林海中……

**Graphia:** ...

> **绘世：**……

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Graphia:** I will follow your words with my brush... and paint the ideal end...

> **绘世：**我会依你所述着笔，绘出理想的归宿……

**Graphia:** Sword-Burier...

> **绘世：**葬剑者……

**Graphia:** We've reached the shore you've been seeking.

> **绘世：**我们已抵达你所求的彼岸。

*(Cutscene ends)*

**Blade:** What is this place?

> **刃：**…这是？

**Graphia:** This is the World in Canvas, a mirror to your soul, blooming as your heart dictates. May this meadow be everything you wished for.

> **绘世：**这是画中世界，随你的心境所转。但愿这片花原如你所愿。

**Blade:** Superb artistry...

> **刃：**出色的技艺……

**Blade:** And yet, I thought you would paint the desolation within me instead.

> **刃：**只是，我本以为…你会画出的是我心里的荒芜。

**Graphia:** Desolation?

> **绘世：**荒芜？

**Graphia:** I saw no desolation in your story. Instead, I found the lingering regrets of a master craftsman.

> **绘世：**在你的故事里，我没见着荒芜，我看到了一位匠作的遗憾。

**Graphia:** The fire that tempers black steel and shapes a hundred blades does not burn upon the forge or anvil. It burns within your heart.

> **绘世：**令玄铁冶炼、百兵赋形的火，并不在熔炉与铁砧之上，而在你的胸中。

**Graphia:** That spark within you has yet to fade. It is why this plain has chosen to bloom into flowers.

> **绘世：**你胸中那点心火，尚未灭却，才会在这片原野上绽放成花。

**Blade:** ...

> **刃：**……

**Silver Wolf:** We've got no clue what you two are on about with all this cryptic talk...

> **银狼：**你们这高来高去的黑话，我俩实在听不懂……

**Silver Wolf:** But you smiled just now, didn't you?

> **银狼：**但你刚才笑了，对吧？

**Blade:** ...No, I didn't. We should go.

> **刃：**…………我没有。走了。

### Accompany Blade on his final journey

**Silver Wolf:** He finally smiled, (Trailblazer).

> **银狼：**他终于笑了，开拓者。

> **银狼：**我反而笑不出来，可恶啊……

> **银狼：**…唉。

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Graphia:** We have reached the end.

> **绘世：**不错，你们知晓哈托彼亚深埋的秘密，前来求取解脱。而今，我已向你们展示兽口的轮廓。

**Silver Wolf:** That Blue Maw... Can it truly consume the Abundance?

> **银狼：**那片蓝洞，真的能吞噬丰饶吗？

**Graphia:** Only a beast of insatiable hunger can end a plague of eternal growth.

> **绘世：**只有永无餍足之兽，才能终结永恒孳生之灾。

**Graphia:** I have stood watch in this realm for a millennium, beholding the eternal conflict where the Voracity and the Abundance remain locked in strife.

> **绘世：**我驻守画中千年，目睹了「贪饕」与「丰饶」永无止尽的拉锯。

**Graphia:** O undying soul... The fate that awaits within those depths lies far beyond the reach of our conjecture.

> **绘世：**不死者啊，落入其中的结果远非你我所能推测——

**Graphia:** Perhaps you will vanish into nothingness in a mere heartbeat, or perhaps, you will endure a millennium of gnawing, constantly regenerating only to suffer once more, with liberation remaining an elusive dream.

> **绘世：**也许你会在瞬息间入灭无形，也许你要经历千年的兽噬之苦，却不断再生，难求解脱。

**Blade:** Beneath that calm of yours, I can feel an "old friend" lingering: agony.

> **刃：**我能从您的平静中体会到一位「老朋友」的存在：痛苦。

**Graphia:** This is the price I must bear for becoming one with the "Canvas".

> **绘世：**这是…当我和「画卷」融为一体时，理应背负的代价。

> **银狼：**你是说…他跳下去，还要被千刀万剐那么久？

> **银狼：**我不同意！爻光讲这个计划的时候可没说过！

**Blade:** I have lived for nearly a millennium. I stopped wishing for a swift end a long time ago.

> **刃：**我活了近千年，早已不奢求痛快的解脱了。

> **刃：**若没有被古兽彻底吞噬，我就还活着。而在那一刻到来前，我会将倏忽押上铁砧，挥打锻锤，一遍、一遍，又一遍。

**Silver Wolf:** ...Is there really no other way!?

> **银狼：**…非得这样不可？！

**Blade:** ...

> **刃：**……

> **刃：**银狼，我没煽情的本事。就说一件事。

> **刃：**过去作为铁匠时，我总会给那些处得过去的朋友打造些东西……

**Blade:** But ever since I joined the Stellaron Hunters...

> **刃：**但自从加入星核猎手以后——

**Silver Wolf:** Alright, I get it! You want me to go back and take apart that box Kafka uses as a footrest, right?

> **银狼：**…行了，懂的都懂！你要我回去把卡芙卡当脚撑的那个匣子拆了，对吧？

**Blade:** ...You already took it apart?

> **刃：**…你已经拆了？

**Silver Wolf:** A long time ago.

> **银狼：**早拆了。

**Silver Wolf:** Quite a few treasures in there: a master-crafted blade, a game controller, and a... uh, portable reactor?

> **银狼：**里头宝贝不少啊：一把好刀，一个游戏手柄，一只…呃，便携式反应堆？

**Blade:** ...That's the armor core reforged from the remains of the Glamoth Queen.

> **刃：**…那是用格拉默女王遗骸重铸的战甲核心。

**Silver Wolf:** Oh, and a cat scratching post.

> **银狼：**哦，还有个猫抓板。

**Blade:** When did you find out about these items?

> **刃：**你什么时候发现的？

**Silver Wolf:** "You"? You mean "We". We all found out.

**Silver Wolf:** Think you hid it that well? Please, give me a break. We're a pro team, you know.

> **银狼：**你以为自己藏得很好吗？开什么玩笑，我们可是顶尖专业团队好吧。

**Blade:** ...

> **刃：**……

**Blade:** Just split it among yourselves. Consider it a keepsake.

> **刃：**记得分了，就当是留个念想。

**Silver Wolf:** ...

> **银狼：**……

**Silver Wolf:** Kafka should be the one saying this, but...

> **银狼：**虽然，这话由卡芙卡来说更合适——

**Silver Wolf:** You are the most perfect blade the Stellaron Hunters could ever have.

> **银狼：**但，你就是星核猎手最完美的剑刃。

**Blade:** ...

> **刃：**……

**Blade:** ...Just a broken blade, that is all.

> **刃：**…一柄断剑罢了。

- ▸ Blade, you're actually quite gentle and kind.

**Blade:** You talk too much.

> **刃：**你话太多了。

- ▸ Silver Wolf, I didn't know you had a sentimental side.

**Silver Wolf:** You talk too much.

> **银狼：**你话太多了。

**Blade:** ...Thank you.

> **刃：**…多谢。

**Silver Wolf:** Before you go, you have something to say to (Trailblazer) too, right?

> **银狼：**走之前，你也有话要对开拓者说，对吧？

**(Trailblazer):** ...To me?

> **开拓者：**…我也有份？

**Blade:** Don't take offense, it's just a few words.

> **刃：**别嫌弃，没几句话。

**Blade:** Before joining the Stellaron Hunters, I once lost myself.

> **刃：**成为星核猎手以前，我曾经失去自我。

**Blade:** Unable to see the future, yet unable to reclaim the past... Just like you when you were first reborn.

> **刃：**看不清未来，也无法重拾过去，恰如刚重生时的你。

**Blade:** Whether we chose this path or were forced onto it...

> **刃：**无论我们是否自愿走到如今这一步——

**Blade:** (Trailblazer), leave no room for regret, and never deny the path you've traveled.

> **刃：**开拓者，不要后悔，也不要否认已走过的路。

**Blade:** We use the script to interfere with the future.

> **刃：**我们以剧本干涉未来。

**Blade:** But some things, once spoken aloud before their time arrives, will never come to pass.

> **刃：**但有些事情，一旦提前讲明，便不会发生。

**Blade:** So... I must head into this unscripted path alone. To witness that final, solitary truth for myself.

> **刃：**所以我必须亲赴这场没有剧本的旅途，去见证那个唯一的答案。

> **刃：**时候到了。

> **刃：**对了，还记得那句暗号么？

**(Trailblazer):** (Two pair...)

> **开拓者：**（君命无二……）

> **刃：**（凭城…借一。）

> **刃：**…别了。

> **刃：**让一切结束…或真正开始吧。

### Pick up the sword's shadow in the dream

> *Old dreams are like ashes, washed clear by blood and fire. The blade shatters, all form dissolves and returns to stardust.*

**???:** In the final moments of your life...

> **刃：**……

**Blade:** How much time has passed?

> **刃：**过去了多久…？

**Blade:** Sea of Clouds... Ambrosial Arbor...

> **刃：**云海…建木……

**Blade:** Huh... To think that with death so close, this is the scenery my dreams would conjure.

> **刃：**哼…死期将至，梦中所见竟是这副光景。

> **刃：**（心跳声…也几不可闻。）

> **刃：**师父…？

> **刃：**…不，您认错人了。我不是应星。

**Huaiyan (In Dreams):** Huh. A lad your age, yet your head is more muddled than mine. You're talking nonsense in your sleep, boy.

> **梦中的怀炎：**呵呵。年纪轻轻的小伙子，说起梦话来，竟比老朽还糊涂哟。

**Huaiyan (In Dreams):** It has been ages since we last met. Tell me, have you let your craft go to waste? Your master wishes to see what you can do.

> **梦中的怀炎：**我们师徒经年未见，也不知这些年你的匠艺是否搁下？为师想见见。

> **刃：**……

**Blade:** Not a sweet dream of toasts and laughter, nor a nightmare of clashing steel...

> **刃：**不是把酒言欢的好梦，也不是刀剑相向的噩梦……

**Blade:** ...Instead, it is a dream of returning to the very beginning, to confess my sins before the master who taught me everything.

> **刃：**…偏偏是回到人生之初，向传道授业的恩师告罪之梦。

**Blade:** Typical...

> **刃：**真是的……

### Bid farewell to the master

**Blade:** This is a standard-issue sword I forged for the Cloud Knights, back when I first began my training.

**Blade:** Looking at it now, it is a crude piece of work.

> **刃：**…如今再看，粗陋得很。

**Hanguang (In Reminiscence):** "Senior frater, are you truly from a short-life species?"

> **追忆中的含光：**「师兄，你真是短生种吗？」

**Yingxing (In Reminiscence):** "Why do you ask?"

> **追忆中的应星：**「…为什么问这个？」

**Hanguang (In Reminiscence):** "I mean no offense. I just wondered... Why would you come all the way to the Xianzhou to learn the craft?"

> **追忆中的含光：**「我没别的意思，就想问问你为什么会想到来仙舟求学？」

**Yingxing (In Reminiscence):** "My home became a grazing ground for those borisin beasts. My parents, their fertilizer. Unlike you, I won't live to see them wiped out, but my blades will."

> **追忆中的应星：**「我家，成了那些步离禽兽的牧场。我爹娘，成了养料。我命没你们长，活不到把他们斩尽杀绝的那天。但我铸的剑，可以。」

**Yingxing (In Reminiscence):** "Look at this blade. Fresh from the forge today. I made it."

> **追忆中的应星：**「看这把剑，今天新出炉，我锻的。」

**Hanguang (In Reminiscence):** "That is so cool!"

> **追忆中的含光：**「这也太帅了…！」

**Yingxing (In Reminiscence):** "Stop flattering me. This is just a standard-issue sword for the Cloud Knights. According to the template, its name should be New Blade: Zero One."

> **追忆中的应星：**「少拍马屁，这是给云骑打造的制式佩剑。按铸模命名，应该叫『新的剑•零一』。」

**Hanguang (In Reminiscence):** "Senior frater! How could you so thoughtlessly name your own creation?"

> **追忆中的含光：**「师兄怎么能给自己的作品起如此草率的名字！」

**Hanguang (In Reminiscence):** "The very first assignment Elder Huaiyan gave his disciples is 'What defines a blade?' He always says a name carries the weapon's destiny..."

> **追忆中的含光：**「炎老给入室弟子布置的第一道作业——『何物为刃』——他老人家总说，名字里有武器的命运……」

**Yingxing (In Reminiscence):** "'A lone cloud drifts, a crane departs the woods...'"

> **追忆中的应星：**「『孤云无定鹤辞林』……」

**Yingxing (In Reminiscence):** "Let's call it 'Departure,' junior frater."

> **追忆中的应星：**「就叫它『辞林』吧，师弟。」

**Hanguang (In Reminiscence):** "Haha! Senior frater, I thought you'd go with Lone Cloud or Guyun!"

> **追忆中的含光：**「哈哈，师兄，我还以为你会叫他『孤云』呢！」

**Yingxing (In Reminiscence):** "Look at this blade. Fresh from the forge today. I made it."

**Blade:** Looking at it now, it is a crude piece of work.

**Blade:** Baiheng... And that bow I forged for her.

**Blade:** In the end, it was I who forged the greatest sin.

> **刃：**…最终却是我铸成了大错。

**Baiheng (In Reminiscence):** "For me? Thank you! I told you, didn't I? As long as you set your heart on what you love, nothing can stop you."

> **追忆中的白珩：**「送我的？谢谢你啊。我就说吧，只要一心干你想干的事业，就没有不成的。」

**Baiheng (In Reminiscence):** "Hey, did you give this bow a name?"

> **追忆中的白珩：**「哎，你有给这弓起什么名儿吗？」

**Yingxing (In Reminiscence):** "I named this bow 'Luer,' after a mythical steed from the Primeval Imperium. Legend says it was one of the Emperor's finest mounts, a divine beast that could tread over any chasm and lead its master back from the brink of death..."

> **追忆中的应星：**「我给这弓起名叫『騄駬』，来源于古国神话，乃是古国皇帝神骏之一，传闻曾踏平天堑，助其主人死地回生……」

**Baiheng (In Reminiscence):** "Pfft! How did you get so off track? Why pick a name meant for a horse?"

> **追忆中的白珩：**「噗，怎么离题万里，选了个马儿的名字？」

**Baiheng (In Reminiscence):** "That won't do! It does not suit me at all. I am going to call it Recurve Bow."

> **追忆中的白珩：**「不要，一点本姑娘的特色都没有。我偏要叫它『曲弓』。」

**Yingxing (In Reminiscence):** "That sounds terrible. It is like my mother naming me 'Human.' At least change it to something elegant, like 'Winding Stream'!"

> **追忆中的应星：**「真难听，简直像我娘给我起名叫『阿人』一样。你好歹换个『曲水』之类风雅的名字吧？」

**Yingxing (In Reminiscence):** "Fine. Have it your way."

> **追忆中的应星：**「…罢了，依你。」

**Baiheng (In Reminiscence):** "Look at you, skilled with both the pen and the sword. You really should not be trapped in this Flamewheel Forge for the rest of your life."

> **追忆中的白珩：**「你呀你，能文能武的，真不该一辈子困在这铸炼宫里。」

**Baiheng (In Reminiscence):** "Hey, perfect timing! I have a few friends getting together on the Luofu. Want to come along?"

> **追忆中的白珩：**「哎，正好，我有好些个朋友要在罗浮小聚，想来看看吗？」

> **刃：**一辈子只留在一个地方，那可不行啊。

**Yingxing (In Reminiscence):** "I forged this so you can use it to protect yourself."

> **追忆中的应星：**「这是我为你打造的护身武器。」

**Blade:** In the end, it was I who forged the greatest sin.

> **刃：**…最终却是我铸成了大错。

*(Walk further)*

**Blade:** Jing Yuan's Devastator Glaive... The weapon I promised to forge for him all those years ago.

> **刃：**景元的阵刀…我当年承诺为他打造的武器。

- ▸ View "Starfall Reverie"

**Blade:** Old friends have drifted across the stars, but he remains.

> **刃：**故友星散，他却撑持至今。

**Jing Yuan (In Reminiscence):** "A genius? I suppose Master Jingliu's swordplay should be no trouble for you then."

> **追忆中的景元：**「天才？这么说，镜流师父的剑术也难不倒你咯。」

**Yingxing (In Reminiscence):** "Hmph. You little rascal, do not try to act like my senior just yet."

> **追忆中的应星：**「哼，你这滑头小鬼…别在我面前逞前辈威风。」

**Yingxing (In Reminiscence):** "I am from a short-life species. Xianzhou swordplay takes hundreds, even thousands of years to master. I simply do not have the time."

> **追忆中的应星：**「我是个短生种。仙舟剑艺要修上百年、千年，我学不来。」

**Jingliu (In Reminiscence):** "I heard your bold claim: 'I'd rather leave this world in a blaze than live until the end of time.' It seems you think little of us Xianzhou people, then?"

> **追忆中的镜流：**「我听过你的豪言：宁如飞萤赴火，不作樗木长春。看来，你是瞧不上仙舟人啊？」

**Yingxing (In Reminiscence):** "My, my, the future Sword Champion has sharp ears. It was just drunken rambling. Don't take it to heart."

> **追忆中的应星：**「唉，未来的剑首大人耳朵还挺尖，酒后妄言，你可别当真。」

**Yingxing (In Reminiscence):** "It would be a waste to throw my skills away on the battlefield. You go to the front, and I will forge the divine weapons for you. It is a promise."

> **追忆中的应星：**「我这身手艺白白捐在沙场上，岂不浪费？你们上阵，我为你们锻造神兵，就这么说定了。」

**Jing Yuan (In Reminiscence):** "Alright, deal! Then I want a devastator glaive, four meters long, decorated with green silk, and you should engrave the blade with cloud patterns, or maybe lightning...?"

> **追忆中的景元：**「好，好！那我要一把阵刀，刀长丈八，盘饰青丝，刀面上要雕云纹…呃，还是闪电…？」

**Jingliu (In Reminiscence):** "Wait until you have grown a bit taller!"

> **追忆中的镜流：**「等你再长高些吧！」

**Jingliu (In Reminiscence):** "Then it is settled. When the time comes, you shall arm each of us with a weapon of our own. Let us face the fires of war in your place and handle all that tedious business on your behalf."

> **追忆中的镜流：**「既然如此，那我们便约好了：有朝一日，你可要给在座的朋友们各打一把趁手的兵器，好让我们代你上阵杀敌，做那些没完没了的体力活啊。」

**Jingliu (In Reminiscence):** "Come, friends, drink up! We five come from all walks of life, but here and now, we are one. Together, we shall bring glory to the Xianzhou and peace to all who dwell beneath the stars..."

> **追忆中的镜流：**「来吧，诸位，举杯！我五人虽身世各异，既结为挚友，则同心协力，上报仙舟，下安黎民——」

**Blade:** ...By the Reignbow and the Ten Lords. If I betray our bond, let all of existence hunt me down.

- ▸ View "Starfall Reverie" (again, optional)

**Blade:** ...By the Reignbow and the Ten Lords. If I betray our bond, let all of existence hunt me down.

- ▸ View the Ranger's cane

**Blade:** He came to visit me even after I was cast into the Shackling Prison.

> **刃：**在我沦入幽狱时，他也曾来探望我。

**La Mancha (In Reminiscence):** "Yingxing, oh Yingxing... How did you end up like this?"

> **追忆中的拉曼查：**「应星啊，应星…你怎么就活成了这样？」

**La Mancha (In Reminiscence):** "The shadow has tasted the flesh of Abundance within you."

> **追忆中的拉曼查：**「影子在你身上尝到了丰饶血肉的味道……」

**Yingxing (In Reminiscence):** ...

**La Mancha (In Reminiscence):** "You... You cannot even speak anymore."

> **追忆中的拉曼查：**「…连话都说不出了啊。」

**La Mancha (In Reminiscence):** "Yingxing, look at me. Lift your head."

> **追忆中的拉曼查：**「应星，抬头看我。」

**La Mancha (In Reminiscence):** "It was only thanks to you and your master that the shadow of Voracity was sealed. I owe you both a debt of gratitude."

> **追忆中的拉曼查：**「过去能封住『贪饕』之影，全靠你师徒二人，我欠你们一份人情。」

**La Mancha (In Reminiscence):** "So, I have but one question: Do you want out? If so, just nod."

> **追忆中的拉曼查：**「所以，我只问一个问题：想离开这儿吗？想，就点点头。」

**La Mancha (In Reminiscence):** "There is always a way out. Just give the word, and I will carve a bloody path to get you out right now."

> **追忆中的拉曼查：**「天无绝人之路。只要你点头，我现在就带你杀出去。」

**Yingxing (In Reminiscence):** ...

**Yingxing (In Reminiscence):** "There is... no need."

> **追忆中的应星：**「…不…必了。」

**Yingxing (In Reminiscence):** "I must... atone... for my sins."

> **追忆中的应星：**「这是…我…应赎的…罪。」

**La Mancha (In Reminiscence):** "Perhaps if I had taken you away from Flamewheel Forge back then, your fate would have been entirely different."

> **追忆中的拉曼查：**「…也许当年我带你离开铸炼宫，命运就会截然不同。」

**La Mancha (In Reminiscence):** "I respect your choice, just as I did all those years ago."

> **追忆中的拉曼查：**「我尊重你的选择，像当年一样。」

**Blade:** Pity... so much in this world does not go as we wish.

> **刃：**可惜…这世上许多事都不能如我们所愿。

- ▸ View the Ranger's cane (again, optional)

**Yingxing (In Reminiscence):** It... it was not meant to be like this.

> **追忆中的应星：**事情…本不该如此啊。

**Blade:** Pity... so much in this world does not go as we wish.

> **刃：**可惜…这世上许多事都不能如我们所愿。

> **刃：**这…不是我的作品？

- ▸ View the Hunter's Blade

**Blade:** This was where my life as Blade began.

> **刃：**这是我成为「刃」的开始。

**Silver Wolf (In Reminiscence):** "So you're Blade? You sound like quite the expert on swords."

> **追忆中的银狼：**「你就是刃？听起来很懂刀嘛。」

**Silver Wolf (In Reminiscence):** "Oh, good timing. What do you make of the sword she's carrying?"

> **追忆中的银狼：**「哟，正好，她手里这把刀——如何评价？」

**Blade (In Reminiscence):** "The blade itself is fine, but the technique is wrong. Swordsmanship is no song and dance. Power starts at the feet. Only when the eyes and mind follow through will the strike land."

> **追忆中的刃：**「刀是好刀，但用法不对。用剑不是跳舞弹琴，力从脚掌起，眼到、心到，才能打到。」

**Kafka (In Reminiscence):** ...

**Silver Wolf (In Reminiscence):** "My oh my, Bladie! You really are the ultimate blade critic!"

> **追忆中的银狼：**「我的天，点刀哥！刀剑领域大神！」

**Silver Wolf (In Reminiscence):** "Since you have so much to say, Master, why not forge a legendary blade for each of us?"

> **追忆中的银狼：**「这么有说法，大师，要不您给咱们每人打一把好刀呗？」

**Blade (In Reminiscence):** ...

**Blade (In Reminiscence):** "I put down the hammer a long time ago. My hands cannot do what my mind wants anymore. Fighting with a weapon that does not suit you is just a quicker way to die."

**Blade (In Reminiscence):** "From now on, if a sword is needed, let it be mine."

> **追忆中的刃：**「我放下锻锤太久了，有心无力。不趁手的兵器只会让你们更快送命。」

**Blade:** This shell of mine is the only "Blade" there is.

- ▸ View the Hunter's Blade (again, optional)

**Blade (In Reminiscence):** "From now on, if a sword is needed, let it be mine."

> **追忆中的刃：**「往后，倘若到了用剑的时候，让我来吧。」

**Blade:** This shell of mine is the only "Blade" there is.

> **刃：**这副躯壳…就是唯一的「刃」。

*(Walk further)*

**Blade:** A weapon given by Yingxing to an old friend...

> **刃：**应星送给一位故友的武器……

- ▸ View Cloud-Piercer

**Blade:** But we are no longer who we once were to each other.

> **刃：**但我们都不再是彼此了。

**Blade (In Reminiscence):** "Who I am? Does not matter."

> **追忆中的刃：**「我是谁？不重要。」

**Blade (In Reminiscence):** "What matters is, do you remember this weapon?"

> **追忆中的刃：**「重要的是——你记得这把剑吗？」

**Dan Heng (In Reminiscence):** "I don't understand what you're talking about..."

**Blade (In Reminiscence):** "All that happened... You think you can just walk away from it all?"

**Blade (In Reminiscence):** ...

**Blade (In Reminiscence):** "You would do well to keep Cloud-Piercer by your side, or..."

**Blade (In Reminiscence):** "...Next time we meet, I will be the end of you!"

> **追忆中的刃：**「最好别再让击云离身，否则……」

**Blade:** What a pity. In the end... we still cast our weapons aside.

- ▸ View Cloud-Piercer (again, optional)

**Blade (In Reminiscence):** "All that happened... You think you can just walk away from it all?"

> **追忆中的刃：**「…下次见面，你一定会死于我手！」

**Blade:** What a pity. In the end... we still cast our weapons aside.
