*(Walk further)*

**Huaiyan (In Dreams):** Apprentice, come here.

> **梦中的怀炎：**徒儿，这边来。

**Blade:** Finally... Is the end in sight?

> **刃：**终于…走到结尾了吗？

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Blade:** I am here, Master.

> **刃：**…我来了，师父。

**Huaiyan (In Dreams):** Good. I have waited a long time for you.

> **梦中的怀炎：**好，等你许久了。

**Blade:** You have seen every blade I have forged in this life.

> **刃：**此生所铸之器，您都看过了。

**Huaiyan (In Dreams):** Fine work. Somewhat flawed, perhaps, but their edge is undeniable. The marks of a true master.

> **梦中的怀炎：**好啊，虽有瑕疵，却难掩锋芒，确是良工神兵。

**Blade:** Gazing upon the weapons I forged in days past, I can only feel a surge of endless regret.

> **刃：**看着旧日所铸之器，徒儿只觉无穷遗憾在胸中翻涌。

**Blade:** The hammer falls and cannot be undone, yet I have spent my life taking one wrong step after another.

> **刃：**落锤无悔，可我这一生却步步踏错。

**Blade:** I betrayed my friends and broke my oaths. Even with these divine blades, I failed to protect those I held dear. My hands are stained with blood. I loathe the reflection it casts back from my blade.

> **刃：**背叛故友，毁弃盟誓…纵有神兵，也未能护住珍视之人。手染鲜血，剑中倒影连自己也厌恶。

**Blade:** But what weighs heaviest on my soul is the debt I owe you, master. In this life, I fear I will never have the chance to repay it.

> **刃：**而最令我愧对的…是您的恩情。此身此世，恐怕再无机会报答了。

**Huaiyan (In Dreams):** ...Let it all out. You feel a bit lighter now, don't you?

> **梦中的怀炎：**…都说出来，痛快些了吧？

**Huaiyan (In Dreams):** But isn't there one last piece you've yet to show me?

> **梦中的怀炎：**你是不是还有件作品，没给我看？

**Huaiyan (In Dreams):** It has yet to be named, has it not?

> **梦中的怀炎：**此剑，应该尚未命名吧？

**Blade:** That is a mere scrap from the forging of Shard Sword. I hid it away, fearing it would bring shame upon our school. Master, how did you...

> **刃：**那是锻造「支离」时的残品…我藏了起来，恐辱师门之名。师父是如何——

**Huaiyan (In Dreams):** Can a mere boy keep secrets from his master? I entrusted General Yao Guang to bring it to you.

> **梦中的怀炎：**小娃娃能瞒过师父？我托爻光将军，将它带给了你。

**Huaiyan (In Dreams):** Since the day you left, I have kept this blade hung in the hall, gazing upon it daily. That raw, youthful spirit within it, it burns to the touch.

> **梦中的怀炎：**自你走后，我将这把剑悬于堂上，日日端详。那份初犊锐气，烫人得很。

**Huaiyan (In Dreams):** It is the same light that shone in your eyes the day you bowed to me. It is the keen edge of a craftsman.

> **梦中的怀炎：**就像你拜师那日眼中的光——那是「工匠」的锋芒。

**Blade:** I am but rusted scrap now, my light has long since faded. This is where I belong, buried.

> **刃：**我如今是锈蚀的残铁，早已黯淡无光，合该葬在此处。

**Huaiyan (In Dreams):** Crushed ore, old materials, rusted iron... As craftsmen, we spend our entire lives in such company.

> **梦中的怀炎：**碎矿、旧料、残铁…我们匠人的一生，都在与它们为伴。

**Huaiyan (In Dreams):** "Waste not the bounty of the world, but perfect it through craft." To take what is useless, to take every failed scrap and forge it anew, isn't that our very mission as craftsmen?

> **梦中的怀炎：**「不忍万物靡费，必善尽其巧工」，将一切无用之物、失败的次品重铸一新，不正是我们匠人的使命？

**Blade:** Even for a life that has reached its end, a life built on nothing but mistakes?

> **刃：**即便是走到了尽头，处处是错误的生命？

**Huaiyan (In Dreams):** As it is with the work of a craftsman, so it must be with the life of a craftsman.

> **梦中的怀炎：**匠人的作品如此，匠人的生命也当如此。

**Huaiyan (In Dreams):** Do you still remember the question I once asked you: What defines a blade?

> **梦中的怀炎：**还记得我曾问你的问题吗——「何物为刃」？

**Blade:** ...

> **刃：**……

**Huaiyan (In Dreams):** The answer already lies within you. Go now, and forge it anew.

> **梦中的怀炎：**你胸中已有答案…就将它再造一新吧。

**Yingxing (?):** "Forge it anew." Interesting.

> **应星（？）：**「再造一新」…有趣。

**Yingxing (?):** "Now I wish more than anything that I could just live a bit longer." Yingxing himself said that, back when he was nearing his end.

> **应星（？）：**「我从没这么想要多活片刻」——这句话，可是当年垂暮的应星亲口所说。

**Blade:** Shuhu...

> **刃：**倏忽……

**Shuhu:** You've fought me all this time and leapt into the Leviathan's maw, nothing more than a bid to drag me down with you.

> **倏忽：**你与我缠斗至今，跳入古兽之口，无非是想同归于尽。

**Shuhu:** A pity, truly. For so long as a single soul in this vast world prays for Abundance, I shall return to bestow my grace.

> **倏忽：**可惜，只消世间还有一人祈求「丰饶」，我便会重现，带去恩赐。

**Shuhu:** And now, I have come in answer to your prayer. Within the Leviathan's maw, I shall grant you a thousand years of agony as your flesh will be remade only to be devoured again and again. You shall find no release.

> **倏忽：**如今，我应你祈愿而至。我将令你在此兽口中，历经千年噬身之苦，身躯却不断重塑，永无解脱。

**Shuhu:** Is this not exactly what you call "Forge it anew"?

> **倏忽：**这不正是…所谓的「再造一新」吗？

**Shuhu:** Poor child. You and the Xianzhou citizens are always so short-sighted.

> **倏忽：**可怜的孩子…你、还有仙舟人，你们总是如此短视。

**Shuhu:** Trapped in this waking dream called life, you cling to what is gone and tremble at the thought of being forgotten.

> **倏忽：**坠入人生这场大梦，执迷于失去，害怕被遗忘。

**Shuhu:** Yet when I come to carve through your flesh and awaken your minds, you spurn my beautiful gift.

> **倏忽：**而当我前来为你们开凿血肉，启迪灵智时，你们却又抗拒我美丽的布施。

**Blade:** ...

> **刃：**……

**Shuhu:** You were once a master craftsman. You should know best about the beauty of shaping.

> **倏忽：**你曾是巧匠，最应该懂得「塑造」之美。

**Shuhu:** I can grant you the power of reshaping life and death, bringing those you loved and lost back to life. Let your friends return to this world again and again, giving you the chance to right every single wrong.

> **倏忽：**我能予你重塑生死的力量，令你所爱所憾之人，重获新生！让你的朋友一次又一次获得卷土重来的机会——获得修正一切错误的机会。

**Blade:** Righting wrongs? You mean by carving out more walking husks like me, things that crave an end they can never reach?

> **刃：**修正？用你的方式，造出更多像我一样…求死不得的活尸？

**Shuhu:** That is but a fleeting pang, a necessary tempering on the path to eternity. If you persist in spurning this gift, then this maw shall become your prison for all time.

> **倏忽：**那是短暂的阵痛，迈向永生必需的锤炼。若你执意拒绝这份恩赐…这兽口将成为你永世的囚牢。

**Shuhu:** Your body will churn for millennia, caught between being digested and reborn. Every inch of your flesh will remember the agony of being ground to pulp, until your very will dissolves into nothing, leaving only a hollow husk that can cry out my name.

> **倏忽：**你的身躯会在消化与重塑间千载轮转，每一寸血肉都会记住被磨碎的痛苦，直到你的意志如蜡般融化，只剩一具徒呼我名的空壳。

**Blade:** ...Are you done talking?

> **刃：**…说完了？

**Blade:** Do you truly believe that I am the only prisoner here, Shuhu? You are the one bound most tightly by your chains.

> **刃：**你以为…只有我是囚徒？倏忽。你才是被锁链拴得最紧的那个。

**Blade:** "For so long as a single soul in this vast world prays for Abundance, I shall return to bestow my grace."

> **刃：**「只消世间还有一人祈求『丰饶』，我便会重现，带去恩赐。」

**Blade:** You are nothing but an echo of THEM, a slave trapped within your mission of "Compassion." You mock our obsessions? You are Yaoshi's most pathetic prisoner.

> **刃：**你只是祂的回声，一个被困在「慈悲」使命里的奴隶。你嘲笑我们执迷？你才是药师最可悲的囚徒。

**Shuhu:** ...Are you trying to provoke me?

> **倏忽：**…你想激怒我？

**Blade:** No. I am pronouncing your sentence. I never dove into this abyss for the sake of "a mutual end."

> **刃：**不，我在宣读你的刑期。我跳进这里，从来不是为了「同归于尽」……

**Blade:** I came for "forging."

> **刃：**而是为了「冶炼」。

**Blade:** As long as you cling to life, you must keep reshaping this body to fight back the gnawing from the Voracity. And that struggle is exactly what I want.

> **刃：**只要你还想活下去，就要不断重塑这具身体，对抗贪饕的吞噬…而我要的，就是这份挣扎。

**Blade:** Let the maw of this Aeon husk be the furnace, let our undying flesh be the iron, and let death, a thousand times over, be the hammer...

> **刃：**以这星神残骸的巨口为洪炉，以你我不死的血肉身躯为铁料，以千次万次的死亡为重锤……

**Blade:** Right here, I shall forge and recast you, again and again!

> **刃：**我要在此处，将你反复锻打、熔铸！

**Blade:** Not to destroy you but to create a cage that can hold you prisoner for all eternity.

> **刃：**不是为消灭你——而是造就一具能将你永远困锁的牢笼。

**Blade:** And the thorns of this cage shall forever be aimed at that savior who branded you with your mission, Yaoshi.

> **刃：**而这座牢笼的尖刺，将永远指向赐予你使命的那位救主——药师。

**Blade:** Are you ready... to turn your vengeance against your god?

> **刃：**准备好…向你的神复仇了吗？

**Shuhu:** ...I see now. So this is how you "forge it anew?"

> **倏忽：**……原来如此。这就是你想要的…「再造一新」？

**Shuhu:** Very well... let us see how this duel between the condemned unfolds, this forging within the furnace—

> **倏忽：**那么…就让我们看看，这场死囚间的死斗，熔炉中的冶炼——

**Blade:** ...In the end, which of us shall forge the other into a "Blade"!

> **刃：**——最后，会是谁将谁锻造为「刃」！

Switching to Himeko's POV...

As Himeko pursues the mysteries surrounding her father, she briefly recalls those artworks related to the Destruction...

## 201　Yesterday's World　昨日的世界

> 世界：Astral Express　|　区域：Parlor Car　|　角色："Shush"; Asat Pramad; Belle; Fulwish; Himeko; Pearl; Pom-Pom; Ryusuke; Herald of Death; Meeya; Motoko; Starblazer　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Primeval and Other Times'' completed

### Head to the Parlor Car and continue the adventure

> *The artwork Mr. Ryusuke left in his office seems to reveal an unusual connection to the Destruction. Even though Father helped the Crew at the Phantasmoon Games and during the investigation into Fulwish, I still can't quite shake my suspicions about him.*

A while ago at the Astral Express...

**Pearl:** When reunited with family after a fifteen-year separation, 22% of organic lifeforms will weep and embrace, whereas 59% will exhibit indifference or outright rejection.

> **真珠：**面对十五年不见的家人，22%的有机生命会与对方相拥而泣，59%的人则会表现出冷漠或排斥。

**Pearl:** Yet, choosing to conduct a covert investigation instead is entirely unheard of.

> **真珠：**但选择暗中调查对方，却是闻所未闻。

**Pearl:** You harbor suspicions regarding Mr. Ryusuke. Why is that, Miss Himeko?

> **真珠：**你在怀疑隆介先生。为什么？姬子女士。

**Himeko:** Before Star Rail FEST began, I stopped by his office to look for the family's records on the Phantasmoon Games.

> **姬子：**「星铁FES」开始前，我去了一趟他的办公室，寻找家族对幻月游戏的记录。

**Himeko:** Out of concern, or perhaps curiosity, I leafed through his portfolio. The artwork was magnificent, with each piece serving as a microcosm of a different planet. That was when it suddenly dawned on me...

> **姬子：**出于关心，也可以说好奇，我随手翻开了他的作品集。那些画作壮丽无比，每一幅都是一颗星球的缩影。然后，我忽然意识到……

**Himeko:** Five of the worlds he captured in these paintings are now gone.

> **姬子：**作品里有整整五幅画，它们描摹的世界已经消失了。

**Himeko:** Lost to the fires of war waged by the Antimatter Legion.

> **姬子：**在反物质军团的战火中。

**Pearl:** Just as you suspected, Mr. Ryusuke's travels have been highly suspicious.

> **真珠：**和你的猜测一致，隆介先生的行程疑点重重。

**Pearl:** In the year 1969 of the Arcadian Calendar, he traveled to the Delkor Ring to sculpt a statue for the Doctors of Chaos. Two years later, the IPC received word that the last local resistance group had rejected all aid and launched a suicide charge against the Legion.

> **真珠：**乐园历1969年，他去往德尔科星环，为混沌医师塑造雕像。2年后，公司接到消息，当地最后一支反抗军拒绝了支援，向军团发起自杀式冲锋。

**Pearl:** Also, in 1984 of the Arcadian Calendar, he traveled alone to the planet Goliath's Shepherd. After a century of resistance in the fires of war, the planet was officially declared dead nine years later.

> **真珠：**还有，乐园历1984年，他孤身前往歌利亚牧者星。这颗星球在硝烟中进行了长达百年的抗争，并在9年后正式宣告灭亡。

**Pearl:** Both incidents point back to a single culprit...

> **真珠：**两起事件的元凶都指向同一个名字——

**Himeko:** The Lord Ravager Asat Pramad.

> **姬子：**绝灭大君，归寂。

**Pearl:** However, there is also evidence suggesting that Mr. Ryusuke was still in Planarcadia when Asat Pramad appeared.

> **真珠：**不过，也有证据表明：归寂现身时，隆介先生仍在二相乐园。

**Pearl:** Additionally, the IPC recovered a commission he received. It read, "Please carve a trace of our lasting glory, just before the world meets its destruction."

> **真珠：**此外，公司复原了他收到的委托：「请刻下荣光存在的痕迹，在世界毁灭的前夕。」

**Himeko:** He accepted the commission even though he knew the Legion had surrounded the planet?

> **姬子：**明知那里被军团包围，他还是接受了邀请？

**Pearl:** Legends of the Graphia clan abound within Planarcadia. To this day, many believe the Weathering Curse is a scar seared into your very bloodline from Graphia's struggle against the Legion.

> **真珠：**有关绘世一族，二相乐园流传着诸多传说。直至今日，还有许多人认为，风化诅咒的本质，是绘世与军团对战时被烙印进血脉的伤痕。

**Pearl:** Conjecture: Mr. Ryusuke was tracking the "demon from beyond." He likely believed that finding him was the key to breaking the Graphia clan's curse.

> **真珠：**推测：隆介先生是在追查当年的「界外天魔」，相信如果能找到他，或许就能解开绘世一族的诅咒。

**Himeko:** Any other conjectures?

> **姬子：**还有其他推测吗？

**Pearl:** There is another possibility. Please forgive me if this sounds out of line.

> **真珠：**以下是另一种可能，如有冒犯，还望海涵。

**Pearl:** There is a 52% probability that Mr. Ryusuke was being used by Asat Pramad.

> **真珠：**有52%的概率，隆介先生遭到了归寂的利用。

**Pearl:** He might have even chosen to work for him.

> **真珠：**甚至主动接受了他的雇佣。

**Himeko:** ...

> **姬子：**……

**Himeko:** I'll have a word with him.

> **姬子：**我会找他谈谈的。

**Pearl:** I'll leave it in your hands, Miss Himeko.

> **真珠：**有劳了，姬子女士。

**Himeko:** Dad, do you have a minute to talk?

> **姬子：**爸爸，你现在有空吗？

**Ryusuke:** I do! I'm one thousand percent free for you!

> **隆介：**有！百分之一千的有！

**Ryusuke:** What brings on this surprise call for your old man?

> **隆介：**怎么突然想起来给爸爸打电话啦？

**Ryusuke:** I was worried you were too busy, so I didn't want to bother you these past few days. That Fulwish didn't hurt you, did she? Actually, what am I thinking? A mere Herald of Death is nothing to my Himeko. You probably handled her in no time!

> **隆介：**担心你太忙了，这两天都不敢联系你。满愿那家伙没伤到你吧？想想也是，区区告死魔，我们家姬子三下五除二就能解决掉。

**Ryusuke:** And that (Trailblazer) kid is something else too! I'm telling you, everyone at school is a huge fan of the Astral Express now.

> **隆介：**还有开拓者那小家伙也真是了不得，现在，学校里人人都是星穹列车的粉丝。

**Himeko:** Fifteen years. The Bloodstained Games are finally over.

> **姬子：**十五年了，血涂游戏终于结束了。

**Ryusuke:** And this time, everyone survived.

> **隆介：**而且这一次，大家都活了下来。

**Himeko:** It's time we sat down and talked. Just as father and daughter.

> **姬子：**我们也是时候坐下来，以父女的身份，好好谈谈了。

**Ryusuke:** Mmm... Hmm?

> **隆介：**嗯…嗯？

**Ryusuke:** Am I dreaming? My little girl actually wants to have a real talk with her old man? I don't care if I heard you wrong. I'm taking this to the bank! Praise the Astral Express! Praise Akivili!

> **隆介：**我没听错吧？乖女儿居然主动邀请爸爸谈心。哪怕听错了，我也要当它是真的，谢谢你，星穹列车！谢谢你，阿基维利！

**Ryusuke:** Give me ten seconds. No, make it eight! Your old man is on his way!

> **隆介：**给我10秒，不，8秒！爸爸马上就来！

**Himeko:** There is no need to rush like that...

> **姬子：**也用不着这么急……

**Ryusuke:** Love you, kiddo!

> **隆介：**老爸爱你！

**Himeko:** I should have seen that coming.

> **姬子：**我就知道会是这样。

**Ryusuke:** Himeko! Open up! It's your dad!

> **隆介：**姬子，开门，爹地。

### Greet Ryusuke in the Express

**Ryusuke:** Himeko, come over. Tell me more about this!

> **隆介：**姬子，快来这边给爸爸介绍一下！

*(Talk to Ryusuke)*

**Himeko:** I originally planned for us to meet at the school. But since you're already here, let me show you around the Express.

> **姬子：**本打算约你在学校见面，但既然来了，就带你看看列车吧。

**Ryusuke:** I would love that! I was in such a rush last time. I really want to get a good look at the place where you spent the last fifteen years.

> **隆介：**好啊！上回走得太匆忙。你生活了十五年的地方，我可要好好逛逛。

**Ryusuke:** Who could have imagined it? The thing that connects this nearly infinite universe is not some distant god like Xipe, but a single Express.

> **隆介：**谁能想到，将近乎无穷的宇宙连接起来的，不是希佩那样遥不可及的神明，而是一辆小小的列车。

**Himeko:** I have met many artists who dreamed of boarding the Express just to get a closer look at the legacy of Trailblazing.

> **姬子：**我见过不少艺术家，他们都想登上列车，近距离观摩「开拓」的遗产。

**Himeko:** So I am curious. How will the great artist Ryusuke evaluate it?

> **姬子：**我很好奇，大绘师隆介会怎么评价它？

**Ryusuke:** Me? I'll give it my all!

> **隆介：**我？我会全力以赴的！

**Himeko:** I was only joking. There is no need to be so serious.

> **姬子：**开玩笑的，你别这么认真。

### Catch up with Ryusuke... What's he so surprised about?

> *Mr. Ryusuke sure has... a lot of free time, showing up right on cue. Why don't you take this opportunity to show Mr. Dad around the Express?*

**Ryusuke:** There's actually so much interesting stuff on this Express!

> **隆介：**仔细看，有趣的东西可真不少啊！

*(Approach Ryusuke)*

**Ryusuke:** This table is kind of different from the others. I mean, they all look alike, but this one doesn't seem authentic.

> **隆介：**这张桌子，总觉得和其他的不一样。虽然很像，但似乎不是原产的。

**Himeko:** A lot of old furniture was destroyed when the Express crashed. Pom-Pom and I spent quite some time picking the right replacements.

> **姬子：**列车坠毁的时候，不少旧家具损坏了。我和帕姆挑了好久，才找到合适的替代品。

**Ryusuke:** Out with the old, in with the new. The Express has been running for years, so it's only natural to have to patch things up every now and then.

> **隆介：**旧的不去，新的不来。列车东奔西走这么多年，免不了缝缝补补。

**Ryusuke:** You must've had a hard time keeping it running.

> **隆介：**为了让它一直开下去，你肯定很辛苦吧。

**Himeko:** It's not that hard. I'm used to it.

> **姬子：**还好，习惯了。

### Catch up with Ryusuke and introduce the Express

**Ryusuke:** Just as I thought, the Astral Express looks amazing inside.

*(Approach Ryusuke)*

**Ryusuke:** Himeko, come over. I want to know more!

> **隆介：**不愧是星穹列车，装修真漂亮。

**Ryusuke:** These chandeliers...

> **隆介：**这些吊灯……

**Himeko:** The designer used the skeleton of a Void Song Whale as a reference. They say it's been here since Dolly's days on the Express.

> **姬子：**模仿虚空鲸骨制作的吊灯，据说是朵莉可时期流传下来的。

**Himeko:** It catches the eye of many people who board.

> **姬子：**许多人上车的第一眼，都会被它吸引。

**Ryusuke:** But the light seems a bit dim in here, doesn't it?

> **隆介：**不过，光线是不是暗了点？

**Himeko:** It's always been this way.

> **姬子：**它一直是这样的。

**Ryusuke:** Have you tried swapping in a polarized conductor, or rinsing the light units in an alkaline solution?

> **隆介：**你有没有试过换一颗极性导体，或者用碱性溶液渍洗一下发光单元？

**Himeko:** Are you an artist or an electrician, dad?

> **姬子：**这是艺术家的建议吗？

**Ryusuke:** I am just speaking from experience. Don't forget your dad was the one who set up all the lighting in those classrooms.

> **隆介：**只是经验之谈。别忘了，教室的灯光都是爸爸布置的。

### Catch up with Ryusuke... What's he looking for?

**Ryusuke:** Is it there? Let me see...

> **隆介：**会在那里吗？让我看看……

*(Approach Ryusuke)*

**Ryusuke:** It's bigger on the inside. The phonograph also has music from all sorts of worlds, just like the versions I've heard before.

> **隆介：**里面比外面大。留声机还收录了各个世界的音乐，和我听过的一模一样。

**Ryusuke:** It's a shame I didn't get to see the legendary Infinite Ice Cream Machine.

> **隆介：**只可惜，没看见传说中的无限冰淇淋机。

**Himeko:** Dad, you actually believe something like that exists? I thought only kids would fall for it.

> **姬子：**爸爸，你都多少岁的人了，怎么会信车上有这种东西？

**Pom-Pom:** A long, long time ago, such a thing did exist on the Express.

> **帕姆：**很久很久以前，列车上的确有无限冰淇淋机。

**Pom-Pom:** When the Express passed the desert planet Talan, Granholm left the machine there. Now it has become a popsicle planet.

> **帕姆：**途径荒漠星球塔兰的时候，格兰霍姆把它送了出去。现在，那里已经是雪糕星球了帕。

**Ryusuke:** Conductor, long time no see. Your fur looks a lot smoother than last time.

> **隆介：**列车长，几日不见，您的毛发又柔顺了不少。

**Pom-Pom:** Really? Thank you very much.

> **帕姆：**真的吗？谢谢夸奖。

**Ryusuke:** Hey. My daughter Himeko finally opened up to having a heart-to-heart with me.

> **隆介：**偷偷告诉你，我家姬子终于愿意敞开心扉，和我促膝长谈了。

**Pom-Pom:** What a special moment. Pom-Pom will leave you to it, then.

> **帕姆：**那真是一件值得庆祝的事，列车长就不打扰你们了。

**Pom-Pom:** They say family is one of the most precious treasures. Pom-Pom believes we should cherish them.

> **帕姆：**都说家人是最珍贵的宝物之一，要好好珍惜帕。

**Ryusuke:** See? I told you! This time, Dad wins!

> **隆介：**看，我就说有吧。这次是爸爸赢喽？

**Ryusuke:** Himeko, you may be a fully capable navigator now, but sometimes experience still beats youth.

> **隆介：**虽说姬子已经是独当一面的领航员了，但有时候，姜还是老的辣呀。

**Ryusuke:** Hahahahahahaha.

> **隆介：**哈哈哈哈哈哈哈。

**Himeko:** Aren't you tired of talking nonstop?

> **姬子：**说了这么多话，你也累了吧？

**Ryusuke:** Are you actually worried about me? Oh, I am so touched!

> **隆介：**这是在关心爸爸的身体健康吗？我好感动。

**Himeko:** Perfect, I'll make you some coffee.

> **姬子：**正好，给你泡杯咖啡润润喉咙。

**Ryusuke:** Huh? Huh!!

> **隆介：**啊？啊！

**Ryusuke:** Ah, well, might as well swing by the Meeting Car to say hi to that Intellitron buddy.

> **隆介：**哎，也好，顺路去会议车厢和那智械兄弟打个招呼吧。

### Head to the Party Car and make Ryusuke a cup of coffee

> *Mr. Ryusuke is very interested in the Astral Express, and his unique insights into the onboard facilities go far beyond the scope of a mere artist. Is he trying to act like he knows the Express well, or pretending to be unfamiliar with it? Mr. Ryusuke's a guest, after all. Brew him a cup of hot coffee.*

**Pom-Pom:** Pom-Pom finds Himeko's father very easygoing.

> **帕姆：**真是一位自来熟的父亲帕。

**Ryusuke:** Himeko, come on. Let's walk together.

> **隆介：**姬子，快过来，别离爸爸太远。

*(Talk to Pom-Pom, optional)*

**Pom-Pom:** They say family is one of the most precious treasures. Pom-Pom believes we should cherish them.

*(Go to Party Car)*

**Ryusuke:** Intellitron buddy, long time no see.

> **隆介：**智械兄弟，好久不见啊。

**"Shush":** Welcome to the Party Car, Mr. Ryusuke.

> **「闭嘴」：**欢迎来到派对车厢，隆介先生。

*(Approach Ryusuke)*

**"Shush":** Rumor has it that the drinks in the "World's End" Tavern are all brewed from jokes. Since we are in Planarcadia, any single joke before a drink would be second to none.

> **「闭嘴」：**传闻，「世界尽头」的饮料都由笑话酿造，身处二相乐园，一个笑话自然是调饮前的不二之选。

**"Shush":** Since there is only a single joke, it is quite literally second to none.

> **「闭嘴」：**因为只有「一」个笑话，所以「不二」。

**"Shush":** Mr. Ryusuke, the tension detected in your micro-expressions has increased by 33% since our last encounter.

> **「闭嘴」：**隆介先生，您微表情中的紧张情绪，比上次增加了33%。

**Ryusuke:** Nonsense. Himeko is brewing up a cup of coffee just for me. Is there anything in the world more wonderful than that?

> **隆介：**怎么会，姬子要亲自给我泡咖啡，还有比这更幸福的事吗？

**"Shush":** A cup of hot coffee right before the end. This must be what they mean by "life is short and bitter."

> **「闭嘴」：**在赴死前饮下热咖啡，何尝不是一种「人生苦短」。

**Ryusuke:** Now I see why they call you "Shush." Well, since I am still among the living, let me tell you a joke for a change.

> **隆介：**我算知道你为什么叫「闭嘴」了，趁我还活着，给你讲个笑话吧。

**Ryusuke:** A long time ago, there was a truly terrible Nameless. THEY snuck aboard as a passenger, seeking a certain answer.

> **隆介：**很久以前，有个极其糟糕的无名客，祂以乘客的身份偷偷来到这里，想要一个答案。

**Ryusuke:** Now, one day, THEY ordered a drink from a service robot on the Express and told it a joke...

> **隆介：**有一天，祂向列车的家用机器人点了杯饮料，讲了一个笑话——

**"Shush":** A long time ago, there was a truly terrible Nameless. THEY snuck aboard as a passenger, seeking a certain answer.

> **「闭嘴」：**很久以前，有个极其糟糕的无名客，祂以乘客的身份偷偷来到这里，想要一个答案。

**"Shush":** I have retrieved the same joke from the archived logs. It is indexed under the title "An Unfunny Joke."

> **「闭嘴」：**我在废弃的历史记录中检索到了相同的笑话，它被命名为「一个不好笑的笑话」。

**Ryusuke:** But did you know? The first time the robot heard that joke, it laughed with pure joy.

> **隆介：**但你知道吗？机器人第一次听见这个笑话的时候，笑得可开心了。

**Himeko:** Don't go filling "Shush" with nonsense. Its database is already enough of a mess.

> **姬子：**别教「闭嘴」怪东西，它的数据库已经够乱了。

**Himeko:** Dad, here's your coffee.

> **姬子：**爸爸，请。

**Ryusuke:** This cup is huge! Himeko, tell me, are you still holding a grudge against me?

> **隆介：**这么大一杯？！姬子，难道你还在生我的气？

**Himeko:** What are you talking about? Now, drink up while it's still hot.

> **姬子：**说什么胡话？趁热喝了吧。

**"Shush":** Analyzing components. Results show coffee concentrate, Heldorin butterfly scales, a species of fish from a gas giant, trace amounts of iron particles, and various other unknown substances...

> **「闭嘴」：**饮品成分检测：咖啡原浆、赫尔多林蝶鳞粉、某种气态行星鱼类、微量铁粒子及其余未知成分若干……

**Ryusuke:** Mr. "Shush"! You must be exhausted after telling all those "cool" jokes. How about a nice "hot" cup of coffee to warm you up?

> **隆介：**「闭嘴」先生！讲这么多「冷」笑话一定累了，来杯「热」咖啡如何？

**"Shush":** My Inspiration Circuit is vibrating. My radar indicates that the Conductor requires my immediate assistance. Good luck, Mr. Ryusuke.

> **「闭嘴」：**灵感回路在颤动，我的雷达告诉我，列车长需要我的帮助，隆介先生，祝您好运。

Ryusuke gazes at his coffee cup with steely determination, as if the liquid swirling within were molten magma from the core of a planet.

**Ryusuke:** Himeko, if I do not make it out of here, please deliver my resignation to Ms. Dollie. Tell her I will not be coming in tomorrow, which is really no different from any other day.

> **隆介：**姬子，如果我死在这里，记得帮我把辞呈交给啵啵娃老师，告诉她，明天我不上班了，就和往常一样。

In the blink of an eye, Ryusuke grabs his coffee and downs it in one swift gulp.

**Ryusuke:** The coffee tastes like heaven!

> **隆介：**简直绝了！

**Himeko:** Want another cup?

> **姬子：**要再来一杯吗？

**Ryusuke:** Himeko, Ms. Dollie told me that back when you were a school assistant, you used to bring a massive pot of coffee for everyone every single day. They still talk about it to this day.

> **隆介：**姬子，听啵啵娃老师说，你做校务助理的时候，每天都会带一大壶咖啡慰劳大家，他们至今都印象深刻。

**Ryusuke:** It would be selfish of me to keep this all for myself. I simply must bring some back for the other teachers to sample.

> **隆介：**这咖啡不能让我一人独享，得带给老师们一起尝尝。

**Himeko:** I am afraid it will be long cold by the time you get it to them.

> **姬子：**等带过去，恐怕早就凉了。

**Himeko:** But as you said, it is time for us to head to the school. We must return to the source of all these tragedies and put an end to the Bloodstained Games.

> **姬子：**但就像你说的，我们该去学校了，回到一切悲剧的源头，给血涂游戏画上句号。

**Ryusuke:** Are you truly ready to say your goodbyes? We can stay a little longer if you like. We still have plenty of time.

> **隆介：**你已经准备好告别了吗？可以再坐会儿，我们还有很多时间。

**Himeko:** We should go now.

> **姬子：**不用了。

**Himeko:** I have been ready for fifteen years.

> **姬子：**十五年前，我就准备好了。

### Head to Graphia Academy and bid farewell to the Bloodstained Games

> *In the old classroom, Belle and the others are still trapped in the terror of fifteen years ago, preparing for a school anniversary that will never come. Now that the Shuhu cells in Planarcadia are under control, there won't be any new Heralds of Death. It's time to say goodbye to the Bloodstained Games and put an end to this tragedy.*

**Himeko:** It has been fifteen years. Those ghosts have been repeating their final day over and over, trapped inside the classroom you imagenated.

> **姬子：**十五年了，那些幽灵在你幻造出的教室里，不断循环着最后一天。

**Himeko:** Even if they are merely shadows of the past, they deserve to be set free.

> **姬子：**即使只是过去的影子，也该让他们解脱了。

**Ryusuke:** You are right. Let us use one final goodbye to close the chapter on the Herald of Death Incident.

> **隆介：**是啊，就用一场告别，为告死魔事件画上句号吧。

**Belle:** Senior, you are back! We are all waiting for you.

> **美玲：**前辈，你回来啦？大家都在等你呢。

**Himeko:** Sorry I'm late, everyone.

> **姬子：**各位，我来晚了。

**Belle:** It's okay. Thank you so much for coming.

> **美玲：**没关系，你能来就帮了大忙了。

**Meeya:** Himeko! We don't have enough time for the lobby! Could you pleeease use your amazing imagenesis skills to decorate the stage for us? I am begging you!

> **米娅：**大姐头！大堂那边实在来不及了，能用你无敌的幻造技术装饰下舞台吗，求求了。

**Fulwish:** Meeya! Stop bothering Himeko all the time. If you and the others hadn't slacked off on the rooftop, we wouldn't be in this mess right now!

> **满愿：**米娅，别总是麻烦姬子姐，要不是你们跑去天台偷懒，至于拖到现在吗？

**Meeya:** But the school anniversary is tomorrow! If I do not get this done, Ms. Dollie is going to skin me alive!

> **米娅：**但明天就是校庆日，再搞不定，啵啵娃老师会杀了我的！

**Belle:** Haha, you should start by tidying up those ribbons. Once we are finished with the classroom, we will all come and give you a hand.

> **美玲：**哈哈，你先收拾下彩带吧，等布置完教室，我们一起去帮你。

Laughter fills the classroom, but the living know the truth: It'll all happen again, just like every single day for the past fifteen years.

**Meeya:** Wait, did you hear that?

> **米娅：**那是，什么声音？

**Meeya:** It's him? He's back again!

> **米娅：**是他？他又来了！

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Belle:** Everyone, hide! Don't let them find us!

> **米娅：**大家，快躲起来！不要被找到！

**Belle:** Run, senior! The Herald of Death is back!

> **米娅：**快逃吧，前辈！「告死魔」又回来了！

*(Cutscene ends)*

**Himeko:** Don't be afraid, Belle. I'm here.

> **姬子：**别害怕，美玲，有我在。

**Himeko:** No more running away. We don't have to face any Herald of Death anymore.

> **姬子：**不用再逃了。今后，再也不会有「告死魔」了。

**Belle:** Really?

> **美玲：**真的吗？

**Himeko:** When have I ever led you astray?

> **姬子：**学姐什么时候骗过你们？

**Himeko:** Look, even the dean has arrived. Let us run through the anniversary program one last time.

> **姬子：**瞧，校长也来了，我们再彩排一遍校庆的流程吧。

**Fulwish:** Right now? But we still have so much left to do. The decorations are nowhere near finished.

> **满愿：**现在吗？可还有好多东西没布置完呢。

**Ryusuke:** It is alright. We have plenty of time. This sunset will linger for a very long while, just long enough for us to finish our celebration.

> **隆介：**没关系，时间很充裕的，黄昏会持续很久很久，足够完成校庆了。

**Ryusuke:** I hereby declare that the Graphia Academy Anniversary and the Graduation Ceremony of 1984 has officially begun!

> **隆介：**我宣布，乐园历1984年，绘世学院校庆兼毕业典礼，现在正式开始！

**Ryusuke:** Since the other grades are not with us today, we shall keep the proceedings simple. According to our school motto, every student must truly understand their "purpose" before they depart from these grounds.

> **隆介：**其他年级的同学都不在，我们就一切从简。按照本校校训，三年级学生离校前，理应明了「应做之事」。

**Ryusuke:** Students, have you all discovered who you wish to become?

> **隆介：**同学们，你们有想好准备成为怎样的人吗？

**Belle:** Why so serious? It's only a rehearsal! Well, in that case, I am going to be an artist. And not just any artist, I will be even better than you, senior!

> **美玲：**搞这么严肃？明明是彩排欸，那，我要做一个画家，比前辈还厉害的那种！

**Meeya:** I plan to study at the Intelligentsia Guild. I want to learn how to create even more interesting imagenae!

> **米娅：**我打算去博识学会进修，创造出更多有趣的幻造种。

**Fulwish:** I... I want to study cinematography and become a director. I want to capture every single moment of happiness in the arcadia.

> **满愿：**我、我想学习摄影，做一个导演，记录下乐园的幸福瞬间。

**Ryusuke:** I hope that in ten or even twenty years, you will all still hold these dreams close to your hearts.

> **隆介：**希望你们在十年、二十年后，依旧记得今天的梦想。

**Ryusuke:** And now, please welcome our distinguished graduate representative, Himeko, to the stage to offer her final blessing to the graduating class!

> **隆介：**下面，有请优秀毕业生代表，姬子同学发言，为毕业生们送上最后的祝福。

**Belle:** Our senior is doing this? That doesn't happen a lot.

> **美玲：**让前辈来吗？真是难得一见啊。

**Fulwish:** Himeko, are you nervous? Relax. Give us a smile.

> **满愿：**姬子姐，是不是紧张了？放轻松，笑一个吧。

**Himeko:** ...

> **姬子：**……

**Himeko:** I do not know what to say on an occasion like this.

> **姬子：**我不知道这种场合该说什么。

**Himeko:** All I know is that this is a comfort the living leave for themselves.

> **姬子：**我只知道，这是生者留给自己的慰藉。

**Himeko:** But, take that step forward. Go and embrace tomorrow. Become someone that the person you are today would be proud of.

> **姬子：**但，向前迈出这一步吧。去拥抱明天，成为值得让今天的自己骄傲的人。

**Himeko:** Congratulations, to the Class of 1984.

> **姬子：**恭喜你们，终于毕业了。

**Belle:** Senior, don't miss us too much.

> **美玲：**前辈，离了我们，可别觉得寂寞哦。

**Fulwish:** Himeko. See you around.

> **满愿：**姬子姐，以后再见啦。

**Meeya:** Himeko, what's with the sad face? It's not like we'll never see each other again. Farewell!

> **米娅：**大姐头，别苦着个脸嘛，又不是见不着了，拜拜啦。

**Himeko:** Belle, Fulwish, Meeya...

> **姬子：**美玲、满愿、米娅……

**Himeko:** Safe travels, everyone.

> **姬子：**大家，一路顺风。

**Himeko:** Fifteen years. The Bloodstained Games that altered the course of so many lives have finally come to an end.

> **姬子：**十五年，这场改变了无数人人生的血涂游戏，终于落幕了。

**Ryusuke:** If only I had not lost myself in my own grief. If only I had looked after that child properly, perhaps everything would have been different.

> **隆介：**假如我没有沉浸在悲伤里，多关照一下那孩子，或许一切都会变得不同。

**Ryusuke:** It was not only the Herald of Death that pushed her towards that abyss.

> **隆介：**将她推上绝路的，又何止是告死魔。

**Himeko:** Fulwish said that it took Planarcadia fifteen whole years to kill her.

> **姬子：**满愿说，二相乐园用十五年杀死了她。

**Himeko:** Will this world ever get better, dad?

> **姬子：**你觉得，这个世界还会变好吗？

**Ryusuke:** I don't know, but I'm willing to believe in it.

> **隆介：**我不知道，但我愿意相信。

**Himeko:** Dad, all these years, just what have you been doing?

> **姬子：**爸爸，这些年，你都在做什么？

**Ryusuke:** Is that concern I hear in your voice?

> **隆介：**这是在关心我吗？

**Himeko:** If you say so.

> **姬子：**就当是吧。

**Ryusuke:** After the Bloodstained Games, I traveled to many different worlds. Every time I set foot on a new planet, my thoughts always returned to you.

> **隆介：**血涂游戏后，我去了很多世界。每抵达一颗星球，我就会想起你。

**Ryusuke:** Until one day, I happened to see news about the Astral Express. The person on that screen was so different from the daughter in my memories. I was afraid to be sure, and I did not want to disturb the life you had found.

> **隆介：**直到有一天，我偶然看见星穹列车的新闻。屏幕中的你，和我记忆中的女儿相去甚远，我不敢确认，也不愿打扰。

**Ryusuke:** As long as I never saw you in person, I could keep telling myself that lie: My daughter had fulfilled her dreams to soar among the stars.

> **隆介：**只要不和你见面，我就可以欺骗自己：我的女儿已经实现了梦想，奔向群星。

**Himeko:** Vonwacq, Owenley, New Londinium... Step by step, I made my way into the very bedtime stories you used to tell me.

> **姬子：**翁瓦克、欧文利、新伦蒂尼恩…我用自己的双脚，走进了你为我讲述的睡前故事。

**Ryusuke:** Were they beautiful?

> **隆介：**它们美吗？

**Himeko:** In this vast universe, nothing holds more beauty than the lands en route, yet to be reached.

> **姬子：**偌大的宇宙中，不会有比「未抵之地」更美的事物了。

**Himeko:** You may have been a poor excuse for a father, but even so...

> **姬子：**即使是个不称职的父亲，但……

**Himeko:** Thank you for setting me on this Trailblazing path.

> **姬子：**谢谢你，让我走上「开拓」的路。

**Ryusuke:** Same here. Thank you for fighting so hard to be here alive.

> **隆介：**我也是。谢谢你，努力地活在世上。

**Ryusuke:** It's still early. Let's have a walk around the campus.

> **隆介：**时候还早，在学校里走走吧。

**Ryusuke:** I... have something very important to tell you, but it can wait.

> **隆介：**爸爸有件重要的事要告诉你，但一会儿再说也不迟。

### Take a stroll around the school with Ryusuke

> *Graphia Academy hasn't changed a bit, though the students have come and gone, class after class. Fifteen years ago, I was just like these kids, admiring the heroes' glory and longing for a day I could stand tall on my own.*

**Himeko:** Something very important?

> **姬子：**重要的事？

**Ryusuke:** Give me just a moment to gather my thoughts.

> **隆介：**给我点时间，酝酿一下。

*(Enter the hallway)*

**Ryusuke:** Nice weather today.

> **隆介：**今天天气真不错啊。

**Himeko:** ...

> **姬子：**……

**Ryusuke:** Haha...

> **隆介：**哈哈……

*(Go through door)*

**Ryusuke:** Let's go downstairs.

> **隆介：**走吧，到楼下去。

*(Approach male student)*

**Onlooking Student:** Oh my god, it's her!

*(If you try to leave the area)*

**Ryusuke:** Himeko, slow down a bit. Your old man's arthritis is acting up again...

> **隆介：**再多夸点！我爱听！

*(Approach stairs)*

**Onlooking Student:** Himekat! Oh my gosh, I am such a huge fan of yours!

> **姬子：**哎，又不是在夸你。

**Ryusuke:** Go on, keep those compliments coming! I could listen to this all day!

> **隆介：**姬子，走慢点儿，爸爸关节炎犯了……

**Himeko:** Goodness, they are not talking about you.

*(Go down stairs)*

**Onlooking Student:** Senior Himeko! Oh wow, she actually looked at me!

**Onlooking Student:** Her navigator aura is incredible!

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Onlooking Student:** Astral Express, I love you!

**Onlooking Student:** Like a love song, baby!

**Onlooking Student:** Baseball Raccoon didn't come?

**Onlooking Student:** Himeko is so pretty.

**Ryusuke:** It gladdens me that my daughter is so popular. But... you all are supposed to be in class right now!

> **隆介：**虽然女儿受欢迎这点让我很高兴，但现在——应该是上课时间吧！

**Himeko:** The Herald of Death incident just ended. It is only right to let them breathe and relax for a while.

> **姬子：**告死魔事件刚结束，让他们放松一会儿也好。

**Ryusuke:** To think that it all happened only yesterday. Young people really do have a way of returning to their carefree selves so quickly.

> **隆介：**明明是昨天才发生的事，年轻人这么快就回到玩乐的状态了……

**Ryusuke:** I suppose it is because they believe that the Astral Express will always be there to save them.

> **隆介：**他们大概觉得，永远会有星穹列车来拯救自己吧。

**Ryusuke:** It brings back memories of you as a little girl. Whenever you heard someone mention the universe, the light in your eyes would outshine even the brightest supernova.

> **隆介：**让我想起小时候的你，只要听到「宇宙」，眼睛里的光就比超新星还亮。

**Himeko:** That is true. The very first imagenae I ever brought to life was modeled after the rocket atop the Phantasmoon's head.

> **姬子：**是啊。我第一次创造幻造种，就是临摹幻月头上的火箭。

**Himeko:** That little fellow had quite a temper. It was so determined to fly higher and higher. To this day, I still wonder if it ever truly reached the moon.

> **姬子：**那小家伙脾气太差，一个劲儿地往天上飞，到最后我也不知道，它有没有飞上月亮。

**Himeko:** Dad, do you still remember it?

> **姬子：**爸爸，你还记得吗？

**Ryusuke:** How could I ever forget? That thing came crashing down and poked a hole right through Ms. Seadrake's tail. I had to take you to apologize in person, I ended up redrawing the tail myself to make it right.

> **隆介：**怎么可能不记得，它掉下来给海龙老师的尾巴砸了个窟窿，我还专门带你去给人家赔礼道歉，重新把它的尾巴画了出来。

**Ryusuke:** These days, I wouldn't be able to handle that kind of trouble anymore. My poor old back wouldn't allow it.

> **隆介：**不过，现在要这么折腾，我的腰间盘突出可吃不消喽。

**Himeko:** Now that I am free from the Weathering Curse, you don't have to spend your life wandering the stars anymore. Why don't you just settle down here?

> **姬子：**我已经没有风化诅咒了，你也不需要满宇宙到处跑了，索性留在这里吧。

**Himeko:** I just can't face the thought of coming back to nothing but an empty home again.

> **姬子：**我不想下次回来的时候，又看见家里空空如也。

**Ryusuke:** ...

**Ryusuke:** Don't worry. I am not going anywhere.

### Take a look at the imagenesis classroom now

> *Compared to my peers, my future is far too brief for me to reach the stars. But through imagenesis and stories, Father carefully safeguarded the curiosity and dreams of a severely ill girl.*

*(If you try to leave the area)*

**Ryusuke:** Himeko, slow down a bit. Your old man's arthritis is acting up again...

> **隆介：**放心，以后我哪儿也不去。

*(Go through door)*

**Himeko:** The Imagenesis Classroom is ahead. So nostalgic.

> **姬子：**前面就是幻造教室了吧，真怀念啊。

*(Enter imagenesis classroom)*

**Himeko:** Ms. Dollie?

> **姬子：**啵啵娃老师，你在吗？

**Ryusuke:** She might've taken the day off.

> **隆介：**应该请假了吧。

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Himeko:** I never noticed it before, but on closer inspection, this classroom is so much bigger than it used to be.

> **姬子：**以前没注意，仔细一看，教室比以前大了好多。

**Himeko:** It must be because of that incident. As the culprit behind it all, you haven't forgotten, have you?

> **姬子：**是因为那件事吧，身为罪魁祸首，你应该没有忘记？

**Ryusuke:** Of course. Since I couldn't get you onto the real Astral Express back then, I figured drawing you a substitute instead would be easy enough.

> **隆介：**当然。爸爸没法让你坐上真的星穹列车，但画一辆「平替」，还是很简单的。

**Himeko:** "The principal himself imagenated an express and went tearing across the campus with his daughter in tow."

> **姬子：**「校长本人幻造出一辆列车，带着女儿在校园里横冲直撞。」

**Ryusuke:** It even made the front page of the Duomension City News back then.

> **隆介：**当年，这事可是上了二维市的头版头条。

**Ryusuke:** Looking back, you were always such a bright and spirited girl, which is why I encouraged you to reach for the stars. But when the Weathering Curse took hold... Did that dream only end up weighing you down?

> **隆介：**回过头看，你曾是个爱笑爱闹的女孩，所以我才培养你对星空的憧憬，但风化诅咒降临时，这些是不是反而给了你压力？

**Himeko:** If it weren't for those dreams you gave me, I wouldn't be the person I am today.

> **姬子：**如果没有那些憧憬，我也不会成为今天的我了。

**Himeko:** I'll never forget. Whenever the Weathering Curse flared up, you would always sit by my bedside and hold my hand, telling me the stories of Sir Kamor.

> **姬子：**我不会忘记。风化诅咒发作的时候，你就会坐在床边，握住我的手，为我讲述卡摩爵士的故事。

**Ryusuke:** That was always your favorite. Whenever your fever refused to break, you would cling to my sleeve and just wouldn't let go.

> **隆介：**那是你最喜欢的故事。每次高烧久久不退，你就会死抓着我的袖子不放手。

**Ryusuke:** So, I would start from the moment Sir Kamor invented the diving device, and go all the way to the second voyage of the Interstellar Explorers. Like clockwork, right about then, you would close your eyes and drift off to sleep.

> **隆介：**于是，我就从卡摩爵士发明潜航装置开始讲起，一直讲到星际拓荒队的第二次出航。每到这时，你就会合上眼睛，睡着了。

**Himeko:** Yeah, that was the first story you ever told me.

> **姬子：**嗯，那是你给我讲的第一个故事。

**Himeko:** And the very story that helped me make up my mind.

> **姬子：**正是因为这个故事，我才下定了决心。

**Himeko:** Even if I could never leave this place, if fixing the Express meant sending just one soul to the stars, that would be enough for me.

> **姬子：**就算不能离开乐园，只要能修好列车，把哪怕一个人送往星空，我也心满意足。

**Himeko:** Yet before I knew it, I'd already traveled far beyond anything I ever imagined as a child.

> **姬子：**可不知不觉间，我走过的路，已经比儿时的幻想还要长了啊。

**Himeko:** I think I understand how you felt back then. Perhaps one day, I'll set down my duties as the Navigator and come back to this place too.

> **姬子：**我似乎能理解你当时的心情了。也许有一天，我也会卸下领航员的担子，回到这里。

**Ryusuke:** What about the Astral Express?

> **隆介：**星穹列车不要紧吗？

**Himeko:** A crew of the most incredible Nameless will keep the Express sailing on.

> **姬子：**有一群最棒的无名客，会让它继续航行下去。

**Ryusuke:** Like a teacher watching their student, or a father his daughter... I understand that feeling.

> **隆介：**就像老师看着学生，父亲看着女儿，我明白这种感受。

**Ryusuke:** Then, your dad will always be here at home, waiting for you.

> **隆介：**那，爸爸会永远在家里等你。

**Himeko:** So what is this important thing you need to tell me about?

> **姬子：**你说有重要的事想告诉我，到底是什么？

**Ryusuke:** Haha, yeah, that important thing.

> **隆介：**哈哈，到底是什么呢？

**Himeko:** Dad, are you hiding something from me?

> **姬子：**爸爸，你是不是有事瞒着我？

**Ryusuke:** ...

> **隆介：**……

**Ryusuke:** Ah, I knew I couldn't hide it for long. Himeko...

> **隆介：**哎，果然藏不住啊。姬子……

**Ryusuke:** I am sick.

> **隆介：**爸爸生病了。

**Himeko:** What?

> **姬子：**什么？

**Ryusuke:** This isn't the right place for this discussion. Let's go to my office.

> **隆介：**这里不方便。我们去办公室说吧。

### Have a good chat with Ryusuke in the office

> *Father's sick? What's going on? When did this happen? I need to get to the bottom of this, and I need to know right now.*

**Himeko:** So...

> **姬子：**所以……

**Ryusuke:** It's the Weathering Curse.

> **隆介：**是「风化诅咒」。

**Ryusuke:** I know what you're about to ask. I do not share the Graphia lineage, which is why I believed I could find the "demon from beyond" and save you and your mother.

> **隆介：**我知道你想问什么，爸爸体内没有绘世一族的血脉。所以，我才相信自己能找到「界外天魔」，让你和你的母亲得救。

**Ryusuke:** But in the end, Ryusuke was just a useless man who couldn't save his wife or daughter...

> **隆介：**但就结果而言，隆介不过是个无能的男人。救不了妻子和女儿……

**Ryusuke:** Or even himself.

> **隆介：**也救不了自己。

**Himeko:** Tell me, what happened in the past fifteen years?

> **姬子：**告诉我，过去十五年都发生了什么？

**Ryusuke:** You've seen my portfolio, haven't you? Those worlds are just like Planarcadia. Every single one of them has fallen victim to the "demon from beyond."

> **隆介：**你看过我的作品集了，对吧？那些世界和二相乐园一样，都是「界外天魔」的受害者。

**Ryusuke:** In those places, "luck" is a very real thing, and the Demon From Beyond can manipulate "misfortune."

> **隆介：**在那些地方，「运气」是真实存在的。界外天魔能够操纵「不幸」。

**Himeko:** What does that even mean?

> **姬子：**什么意思？

**Ryusuke:** In ways beyond comprehension, you feel misfortune crashing down like a tidal wave. Solar storms, diamond rain, system failures, one disaster after another...

> **隆介：**冥冥之中，你会感觉厄运如洪水般袭来，太阳风暴、钻石雨、系统故障，接连不断……

**Ryusuke:** But if you make it out alive, bizarre strokes of luck come swarming back to you. And so the cycle repeats, washing over the world, tearing it apart.

> **隆介：**可如果你幸存下来，离奇的幸运又会蜂拥而至，如此往复循环，将世界洗刷、摧毁。

**Himeko:** It sounds more insane than the Phantasmoon Games.

> **姬子：**这听起来，比幻月游戏还不可思议。

**Ryusuke:** The universe is sheer madness, beyond all reason. I've survived over and over, yet my body has become as fragmented as Graphia once was.

> **隆介：**宇宙疯疯癫癫，就是这么不可思议。一次又一次，我活了下来，身体却和曾经的绘世一样，变得支离破碎。

**Ryusuke:** Is this his way of torturing the rebels? I don't know. But by the time I realized it...

> **隆介：**这是他折磨反抗者的方式吗？我不知道。但意识到的时候……

**Ryusuke:** There was no escape.

> **隆介：**已经无法挽回了。

**Himeko:** Why didn't you tell me earlier? I could've reached out to the IPC's doctors...

> **姬子：**为什么不早点说？我可以替你联系公司的医生……

**Ryusuke:** It's no use. I've been chasing after him my whole life. I know him better than you do.

> **隆介：**没用的。我追了他一辈子，比你更了解他。

**Ryusuke:** Himeko, remember when you first learned to paint? I told you then why we must paint the beauty that we see.

> **隆介：**姬子，还记得第一次学画时，我告诉你，我们为什么要将目之所及的美好画下来？

**Himeko:** "Because the ugly, the despicable, and the cowardly, they endure forever through evasion, like beasts driven by survival."

> **姬子：**「因为那些丑陋、卑劣、懦弱的，它们依靠逃避永远存在，就像求生的野兽。」

**Himeko:** "Beauty remains ever fleeting, just as heroes find their end in sacrifice and stars dissolve after they shine. Humans have surpassed their animal nature. We face our pain and our end to win a dignity that the vast universe cannot ignore."

> **姬子：**「而美丽总是转瞬即逝，英雄迎来壮烈的牺牲，星辰在闪烁后瓦解。人类战胜了兽性，直面苦难和死亡，从无情的宇宙中夺得尊严。」

**Himeko:** "So we must capture what is noble and bright to show that such things truly lived. As for the barbaric and the low, they will endure regardless, needing no witness to their existence."

> **姬子：**「所以，那些光辉的事物应当被描摹下来，以证明它们曾经存在。而野蛮低劣的总会生存下来，自不必有谁去见证。」

**Ryusuke:** But Himeko, you are no longer the one who paints. You have become the very splendor that I strive to capture.

> **隆介：**但姬子啊，你早已不是绘画者，而是我致力于描摹的，那片璀璨的光景本身。

**Ryusuke:** Fate works in mysterious ways. I am so glad to see you once more in my final moments, watching you fulfill your childhood dream. You are standing before me, as the navigator of Astral Express.

> **隆介：**造化弄人。我很高兴，能在弥留之际再见到你，看到你实现儿时的梦想，作为列车的领航员站在我面前。

**Himeko:** I should've come back earlier. I'm so sorry.

> **姬子：**对不起，我回来得太晚了。

**Ryusuke:** No, I am the one who came back too late.

> **隆介：**是我回来得太晚了。

**Ryusuke:** I've thought about it a thousand times. On that final night of the Bloodstained Games, if only someone had been there to sit by your side...

> **隆介：**我无数次想过，血涂游戏结束的那个晚上，如果能有一个人坐在你身旁，该有多好。

**Ryusuke:** After everything, does this tardy father still even deserve to hear what his daughter wants to say?

> **隆介：**现在，我这个迟到的父亲，还有资格聆听女儿的心声吗？

**Himeko:** ...

> **姬子：**……

**Himeko:** If you're willing.

> **姬子：**只要你愿意。

- ▸ I'll tell you about everything that happened.

> ▸ 我会把过去的事讲给你听。

**Ryusuke:** Thank you, Akivili...

> **隆介：**谢谢你，阿基维利……

**Ryusuke:** Thank you, Himeko.

> **隆介：**谢谢你，姬子。

**Himeko:** All of this happened after you went away. You were jumping from one job to the next, and for four long years, I had heard nothing from you.

> **姬子：**那是你离开后发生的事，你的工作首尾相接，整整四年都渺无音讯。

**Himeko:** During those four years, I found the stranded Express deep in the canvas world and kept trying to repair it. Watching the Phantasmoon grow fuller, I remembered the mask that had been handed down through our family for generations.

> **姬子：**那四年里，我在画界深处找到了搁浅的列车，不断尝试修好它。看着日趋满盈的幻月，我想起了那枚家族代代相传的面具。

**Himeko:** If I could claim victory in the Phantasmoon Games, then perhaps, by harnessing the divine authority of Elation, it might just be possible to set the Express in motion once more.

> **姬子：**如果能在幻月游戏中取胜，借助欢愉的神权，让列车重新起航也不是不可能。

**Himeko:** But the Herald of Death was a complete madman, and he smashed every dream to pieces...

> **姬子：**但告死魔是个彻头彻尾的疯子，所有梦想都会被他打得粉碎……

### Head to Dovebrook District and continue the adventure

> *...Himeko, are you scared? ...Himeko, I'm here. ...That is not your responsibility. If you are afraid, let me handle it. ......*

Fifteen years ago...

**Young Himeko:** Huff... huff...

> **少女姬子：**呼，呼。

**Young Himeko:** These bloodstains... The Herald of Death is heading to Dovebrook.

> **少女姬子：**这些血迹…告死魔，在往鸽川的方向前进。

**Young Himeko:** He's already killed so many people. When will it be enough!?

> **少女姬子：**还要杀多少人才肯满足！

> **？？？：**■■■■■■■

**Young Himeko:** You're asking if I'm afraid?

> **少女姬子：**你问我害怕吗？

**Young Himeko:** Of course I am, after seeing all that carnage along the way.

> **少女姬子：**我当然怕啊，在沿途目睹了那样的惨状后。

**Young Himeko:** Belle, Meeya... Everyone was splattered everywhere, like spray paint.

> **少女姬子：**美玲、米娅…大家像颜料管那样，洒得到处都是。

**Young Himeko:** Twisted and crushed.

> **少女姬子：**被扭曲、挤压。

**Young Himeko:** They watched themselves turn into... while they were still alive...

> **少女姬子：**在还活着的时候，眼睁睁地看着自己变成……

**???:**■■■■■■■

> **？？？：**■■■■■■■

**Young Himeko:** Mm. I'm okay. I'm fine.

> **少女姬子：**嗯，我没事的，我很好。

**Young Himeko:** If I don't stop him right here and win the Phantasmoon Games, there will be no tomorrow for anyone, let alone a new journey for the Astral Express.

> **少女姬子：**如果不在这里阻止他，赢下幻月游戏，别说让星穹列车重新起航，所有人都不会有明天。

**Young Himeko:** And someone needs to avenge those students at the academy.

> **少女姬子：**学校的那些后辈，也需要有人替他们讨回公道。

**???:**■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■

> **？？？：**■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■

**Young Himeko:** I know hunting down the Herald of Death isn't my responsibility. If I turn around and walk away, no one will blame me.

> **少女姬子：**我当然知道，讨伐告死魔不是我的责任，就这么转身逃走，没有人会责怪。

**Young Himeko:** But I am already here. I can't let him get away.

> **少女姬子：**可是，都走到这里了， 我不能放任他离开。

### Head forward and examine traces of the Herald of Death

> *Blood. Blood everywhere. Bright red, still wet, resembling brushstrokes. It trails down the street, stretching toward Dovebrook. Almost caught up to him. But how does one defeat something this terrifying?*

**Young Himeko:** Yeah, I'm not really scared anymore. Really.

> **少女姬子：**嗯，我已经不怕了，真的。

*(Try to leave the area, optional)*

**Young Himeko:** Don't run. If I turn back now, there's no one else to hold that demon back.

> **少女姬子：**不可以逃走…否则没人能阻止那个恶魔了。

*(Try to go left, optional)*

**Young Himeko:** Wrong way. It's over there...

> **少女姬子：**方向不对，是那边……

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Young Himeko:** The suppression squad sent by the Department of Aberration Defense... They're all gone. Completely wiped out.

> **少女姬子：**异常防御部派来的镇压小队也全军覆没了。

**Young Himeko:** They struggled through the pain as the Herald of Death dragged and smeared them across the ground...

> **少女姬子：**他们痛苦地挣扎过，被告死魔在地上拖拽、涂抹……

**Young Himeko:** But these stains... Is he really so far gone that he's using himself as "paint"?

> **少女姬子：**不过这些痕迹…他已经疯到，用自己来做「颜料」了？

**Young Himeko:** Even so, he still managed to break through the blockade ahead... That's terrifying.

> **少女姬子：**即便如此，还能突破前面的封锁线，真是可怕。

**Young Himeko:** I think I've got it.

> **少女姬子：**我大概想到办法了。

**Young Himeko:** But first, I need to buy some time.

> **少女姬子：**但在那之前，需要更多的时间。

**???:**■■■■■■

> **？？？：**■■■■■■

**Young Himeko:** Alright, but buying time is easier said than done.

> **少女姬子：**嗯，可拖延时间，也不是件容易的事啊……

**Young Himeko:** It's too narrow here. We need more room. The best bet is to pin the Herald down on that bridge.

> **少女姬子：**这里太狭窄了，若要一个足够空旷的战场…得想办法，把告死魔的行动限制在那座桥上。

**Young Himeko:** I'll take all the wishpower I've gathered since the Phantasmoon became full and use it as fuel to paint "it" into existence.

> **少女姬子：**从幻月满盈以来积累的愿力，我要以它们为燃料，把「它」画出来。

**Young Himeko:** The sheer destructive force unleashed from creating a massive object will break him and leave him completely incapacitated.

> **少女姬子：**创造出庞大的质量，用瞬间爆发出的强大破坏力，彻底剥夺他的行动能力。

**???:**■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■

> **？？？：**■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■

**Young Himeko:** Yeah... By burning all the Wishpower, I will be forfeiting my victory.

> **少女姬子：**是啊，烧掉所有的愿力，无异于放弃获胜的可能。

**Young Himeko:** But even if I did win the games and restart the Express... What would happen to this city? To my friends, my teachers, and everyone else? Where would they have left to go?

> **少女姬子：**但，哪怕我真能赢下游戏，启动列车，这座城市，我的朋友、老师，还有其他的人，他们又该去哪儿？

**Young Himeko:** To set the Express in motion with Aha's power, while standing by as innocent people are slaughtered by monsters... To soar into the stars, while turning a deaf ear to the screams on the ground.

> **少女姬子：**为了用阿哈的神力让列车起航，眼睁睁地看着无辜的人被怪物杀死；为了飞往星空，不顾地上惨叫的人们。

**Young Himeko:** Someone who does that... has no right to call themselves a Nameless.

> **少女姬子：**这样的人，也没资格做无名客了。

**Young Himeko:** Ugh, I knew I should have stuffed an orbital cannon or a molecular saw into the case. Using that bigger-on-the-inside Trailblaze tech, just like the Astral Express.

> **少女姬子：**哎，早知道就该往箱子里塞个轨道炮、单分子锯什么的。用开拓命途那种里面比外面大的技术，像星穹列车那样。

**???:**■■■■■■■■

> **？？？：**■■■■■■■■

**Young Himeko:** Right. When this is all over, I will have to give it a try.

> **少女姬子：**好啊，等有机会，我会试试的。

### Continue forward and examine the body on the ground

> *Must find a way to create a massive object, and use that sheer destructive force unleashed from it to completely incapacitate him. Using up all the Wishpower accumulated in the mask should be enough to paint "it."*

**Young Himeko:** Another body...

> **少女姬子：**又一具尸体……

*(Approach the marked location)*

**???:**■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■

> **？？？：**■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■

**Young Himeko:** Black Birch the Masked Fool... The one who turned Gothum City upside down not long ago!?

> **少女姬子：**假面愚者「黑桦木」？不久前才把歌坛市闹得天翻地覆的那个？

**Young Himeko:** Only two Supplicants are left in the Games now.

> **少女姬子：**现在整场幻月游戏，就只剩下两个谒者了。

**???:**■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■

> **？？？：**■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■■

**Young Himeko:** Just like those legends I grew up with. A hero confronting a monster to protect everyone. Now that responsibility falls to me.

> **少女姬子：**就像我儿时读过的英雄故事那样，一个英雄站在大家面前，与怪物相持而立。现在，轮到我站在大家面前了。

**Young Himeko:** I will take him down. After that I will repair the Express myself and see it fly over the horizon and break out of the atmosphere.

> **少女姬子：**我会击败他，然后用自己的手修复列车，亲眼看着它飞跃地平线，冲出大气层。

**???:**■■■■■■■■

> **？？？：**■■■■■■■■

**???:**■■■■■■■■■■■■■

> **？？？：**■■■■■■■■■■■■■

**Young Himeko:** The very first stop then?

> **少女姬子：**起航前的第一站吗？

**Young Himeko:** It'll certainly be quite memorable.

> **少女姬子：**可真是，令人印象深刻啊。

### Step forward and face the Herald of Death

> *There is no room for hesitation, nor any chances to try again. We must put an end to the Herald of Death's rampage right here. —Right now.*

**Young Himeko:** This stench... It's about time.

> **少女姬子：**这股腥臭味，就是现在……

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Herald of Death:** So exquisite! These flowers blooming in Planarcadia. I can hear them, dancing at the tips of my nerves.

> **告死魔：**多美啊，这些绽放在二相乐园的花朵，我能听见，它们在我的神经末梢舞蹈。

**Herald of Death:** What is that? You say this is not enough?

> **告死魔：**什么，你们说还不够？

**Herald of Death:** Still not enough! I need more pigment. I will mix my colors with screams of terror and pray for the catecholamines to drown me.

> **告死魔：**远远不够！我要更多的颜料，用恐惧的尖叫来调色，祈祷儿茶酚胺将我溺亡。

**Herald of Death:** Department Head! I shall show you the error of your ways! This flesh is the culmination of life. Its own kin is right here, the giant tree running through Ahatopia!

> **告死魔：**部长！我要向你证明，你研究的方向错了！这血肉才是生命的尽头，它的胞亲就在这里，贯穿哈托彼亚的巨树！

**Herald of Death:** I shall become her. We will be as one. No. I will surpass her. I will devour her and become the one and only existence.

> **告死魔：**我将成为她，和她融为一体。不，我要超越她，吞噬她，成为独一无二的存在。

**Herald of Death:** Another new shade. Dazzling, shattered crimson... Are you perhaps the color I've been missing all along?

> **告死魔：**又是新的色彩，耀眼、破碎的殷红…你会不会才是我所缺少的那抹颜色呢？

**Herald of Death:** Step into this masterpiece! Become one with me, and lay the final cornerstone for the future of human evolution!

> **告死魔：**来加入这幅画吧，和我融为一体，为人类的进化垒砌最后的基石！

*(Begin battle animation)*

**Ryusuke:** Stop right there!

> **隆介：**慢着！

**Ryusuke:** So you actually conquered the Herald of Death and set the Astral Express in motion once more at the end of the Bloodstained Games?

> **隆介：**你是说？在血涂之夜的结尾，你战胜了告死魔，并重新启动了星穹列车？

**Ryusuke:** Himeko, I believe in your bravery. When confronted by death, you will undoubtedly choose to resist.

> **隆介：**姬子，我相信你的勇气。在死亡面前，你一定会选择抗争。

**Ryusuke:** But you were so frail and sickly. How could you possibly defeat the Herald of Death who slaughtered every single Supplicant? And how could you hope to repair such a massive, stranded Express?

> **隆介：**但体弱多病的你，要如何击败将谒者们屠杀殆尽的告死魔？又要如何修复偌大一辆搁浅的列车？

**Ryusuke:** The answer is clear. It would be impossible for you. That is, unless...

> **隆介：**答案很简单，你不可能做到。除非……

**Ryusuke:** Unless you had another person helping you.

> **隆介：**有另一个人在协助你。

**Ryusuke:** Don't deny it yet.

> **隆介：**别急着否认。

**Ryusuke:** I've been tested by you enough along the way. So now give your father a little room to breathe.

> **隆介：**这一路过来，爸爸让你试探得够多了。也给我一点空间吧。

**Ryusuke:** While I was gathering your belongings, I found some of the manuscripts you left behind. There were two distinct sets of handwriting upon those pages. Every trace in that house proves that two people once shared a life there.

> **隆介：**在为你收敛遗物时，我找到了一些你留下的稿纸，上面残留着两种截然不同的笔迹，家里的种种痕迹都证明，那里曾有两人共同生活。

**Ryusuke:** Legend says that during the Canvas Age, the Grand Artist Whitebow painted his late daughter onto a scroll and breathed life into her.

> **隆介：**相传在入画时代，大画师白虹将已逝的女儿绘于卷上，赋予其生命。

**Ryusuke:** Suppose, just suppose, that my brilliant daughter created a likeness of herself, a self-portrait so perfect that it breathed and moved with her.

> **隆介：**如果，我是说如果，我那天才的女儿绘制出一张宛如自画像的肖像，而这张肖像又获得了生命，与她同行。

**Ryusuke:** If that is the truth, then every mystery is explained.

> **隆介：**如此一来，一切的疑问都解释得通了。

**Ryusuke:** During that night of the Bloodstained Games, a girl at death's door and an imagenae she created followed the trail of the Herald of Death all the way to Dovebrook.

> **隆介：**血涂游戏的那晚，一个病入膏肓的女孩，和另一个被她画出的幻造种跟随告死魔的痕迹，来到了鸽川。

**Ryusuke:** As a monster bearing Shuhu's Flesh and a Supplicant lost to madness, Marcus Antali defeated the pair before him without lifting a finger.

> **隆介：**作为身负倏忽血肉的怪物，已经疯狂的谒者，马库斯•安塔利不费吹灰之力，便击败了眼前的二人。

**Ryusuke:** Driven by pure instinct, he slowly closed in on them. He intended to use their very lives as pigment to paint a new Bloody Bloom.

> **隆介：**被本能驱动的他缓缓向她们逼近，试图以她们作为颜料，绘制出新的「绽放血手」。

**Ryusuke:** Little did he know that he was walking straight into a trap of their design.

> **隆介：**可他却不曾料想，一切都在对方的计划当中。

**Ryusuke:** The moment he drew near, a massive iron fist was imagenated from the sky above. It crashed down and crushed him to dust.

> **隆介：**在他靠近的瞬间，一只幻造出的钢铁巨拳从天而降，将他砸得粉碎。

**Ryusuke:** It was the night of July 22, 1984 of the Arcadian Calendar. A colossal shockwave, radiating from Dovebrook District, shook the earth. As the dust settled, a strange urban legend found its way into every corner of the world.

> **隆介：**乐园历1984年7月22日晚，巨大的震荡以鸽川区为中心，向周边地区扩散。与此同时，一则都市传说在全球传播开来。

**Ryusuke:** Over ten thousand sightings reported a giant arm descending from the sky that evening. In the hours that followed, the Department of Aberration Defense confirmed that the Herald of Death was completely incapacitated.

> **隆介：**那天晚上，上万例目击报告宣称，有巨人的臂膀从天而降。而后不久，异常防御部检测到告死魔失去了行动能力。

**Ryusuke:** The theories were endless. Was it a divine act by Aha, or a weapon built by the IPC? No one knew for sure. Yet, the silhouette of that giant remained etched in every mind as a testament to heroism.

> **隆介：**阿哈的神迹、公司的武器…真相众说纷纭，但巨人的身影留在人们心中，化作英雄的象征。

**Ryusuke:** It was just an arm, yet beneath the brush of a girl who yearned for the stars, it became an extension of humanity's reach.

> **隆介：**它并不完整，却是一个向往星空的女孩，其画笔之下，人类四肢的延伸。

### Head to Graphia Academy and continue the adventure

> *That was a touching fairy tale. Too bad, I'm not satisfied.*

**Ryusuke:** The girl achieved her dream, and the stars set sail once more. Truly a most romantic fairy tale. And now...

> **隆介：**女孩实现了梦想，星星也再度启程，这简直是最浪漫的童话。现在……

**Ryusuke:** Unfortunately, I have to destroy this fairy tale myself.

> **隆介：**很遗憾，我要亲手打碎这个童话了。

**Himeko:** You don't believe me?

> **姬子：**你在怀疑我？

**Ryusuke:** I have no idea who you truly are. But that little girl who longed for Trailblazing, my frail and sickly daughter...

> **隆介：**我不知道你是什么来头，但那个向往「开拓」的小女孩，我体弱多病的女儿……

**Ryusuke:** From the very beginning, "Himeko" never left Planarcadia.

> **隆介：**自始至终，「姬子」没有离开过二相乐园。

**Himeko:** That is not something you should joke about.

> **姬子：**这个玩笑不好笑。

**Ryusuke:** You know better than I do whether I'm joking or not.

> **隆介：**是不是玩笑，你比我更清楚。

**Ryusuke:** By the way, things outside aren't looking good. The arcadia is drowning in problems. Is it really wise to stay here instead of helping the younger crew members?

> **隆介：**说起来，外面的情况可不乐观。乐园麻烦不断，不去帮帮后辈们，真的好吗？

**Himeko:** Those kids are all grown up now. They can handle their mission. And as for myself...

> **姬子：**孩子们已经长大了，能完成任务。至于我……

**Himeko:** I am staying right here, to deal with the biggest problem of all.

> **姬子：**我站在这里，就是为了解决最大的麻烦。

Returning to the Trailblazer's POV...

When you have a chance to make a choice, make one that you know you won't regret.

### Discuss the token with Graphia

> *Nothing stirs around the Blue Maw. It looks no different from when we first saw it, as if no one had ever leaped down. And with that, Silver Wolf and I have accompanied Blade on his final journey. I should ask Graphia about the token. I need to keep it safe for Evanescia until she wakes up.*

**Silver Wolf:** ...

> **银狼：**……

**Graphia:** Are you intending to stay here until the outcome is revealed?

> **绘世：**你们，要一直留在这儿，直到结果揭晓吗？

> **银狼：**……

> **银狼：**他可能很久以后才会回来，也可能不回来了。

**Silver Wolf:** Or maybe if I just complain and say, "I wish Blade were here," he'll just show up.

> **银狼：**或者，我只要抱怨一声「要是刃在就好了」，他就会出现。

**Silver Wolf:** Anyway, it was his choice. We already said our goodbyes.

> **银狼：**无论如何，这是刃的选择。我们道过别了。

**Silver Wolf:** Thank you. For staying with him until the very end.

> **银狼：**谢谢你们，送了他最后一程。

**Graphia:** Now, it is time to deal with your matters.

> **绘世：**现在，该处理你的事了。

**Graphia:** Gray-haired boy,/girl, you seek me out with her token in hand. What is it that you desire?

> **绘世：**灰发少年/少女，你带着她的信物寻我，所为何事？

- ▸ I've already told you why I am here.

> ▸ 我和你说过我的来意。

**Graphia:** Already told me?

> **绘世：**来意？

- ▸ Didn't you ask me to come here?

> ▸ 是你让我来这儿的？

**Graphia:** I don't quite follow.

> **绘世：**我不太明白。

> **绘世：**驱舟来到此地前，我们应该是第一次见面。

**Graphia:** I only answered the call of Wishpower because of that mask, the one painted by Evanescia. Without it, I would not be here.

> **绘世：**若非你持有绯英绘制的面具，我也不会应愿力召唤而来。

**Graphia:** So, who is this "me" you speak of?

> **绘世：**…所以，你口中的「我」是谁？

**???:** Me, of course.

> **？？？：**当然是，我。

**Silver Wolf:** Not you again... A meanie will always be a meanie.

> **银狼：**混球永远是混球，又是你……

**Asat Pramad:** Misfortune and I share one thing in common: we both enjoy dropping in uninvited.

> **归寂：**我和厄运有个共同点：喜欢不请自来。

**Asat Pramad:** A reunion after a thousand years. Graphia, it has been a long time.

> **归寂：**时隔千年的重逢。绘世，好久不见。

**Graphia:** Asat Pramad...

> **绘世：**归寂……

- ▸ So you finally showed yourself, Lord Ravager.

> ▸ 你终于现身了，绝灭大君。

- ▸ I knew it. This goes much deeper than I thought.

> ▸ 我就知道，没那么简单。

- ▸ Graphia's archnemesis...

> ▸ 绘世的宿敌…

**Asat Pramad:** No, no, we're far more than just archnemeses...

> **归寂：**不不，我们可不只是「宿敌」这么简单……

**Asat Pramad:** Back at Inkford Hermitage, what you heard was only half the story.

> **归寂：**先前在渡画泉隐，故事只讲到一半。

**Asat Pramad:** A thousand years ago, amidst the ruins of Ahatopia, I found a baby girl on the verge of death.

> **归寂：**千年前，我在哈托彼亚的废墟中看见了一名濒死的女婴。

**Asat Pramad:** Her cold, lifeless body reminded me of the legend of "Elation." Aha heard an infant cry and burst into laughter.

> **归寂：**她冰冷的躯体，让我想起「欢愉」的传说。阿哈听见婴儿的啼哭，于是放声大笑。

**Asat Pramad:** Too bad I never did get the joke. So, I came up with a much better one.

> **归寂：**可惜，我不明白这有什么好笑的。所以，我编了个更有趣的笑话……

**Asat Pramad:** I infused that child with the power of "Destruction," giving her the rebirth of a Voidranger. I taught her to paint her monstrous beasts using the life of the Voracity as pigment, all to carry out the Legion's divine will.

> **归寂：**我将「毁灭」的力量赐给那个孩子，让她如虚卒一般重生。我教导她以「贪饕」的生命为颜料，绘制凶恶的巨兽，施行军团的天旨。

**Asat Pramad:** Graphia, your name was your commission. To paint the world and "carve a trace of our lasting glory, just before the world meets its destruction."

> **归寂：**绘世，你的名字是「画出世界」——「刻下荣光存在的痕迹，在世界毁灭的前夕。」

**Asat Pramad:** You were doing so well. Right up until you saw that Express crash. It gave you ideas, Graphia. Unrealistic, foolish ideas.

> **归寂：**你一直做得很好。直到…目睹列车坠毁，它带给了你不切实际的幻想。

**Graphia:** Since that day, I have had nothing to do with you or your "Destruction."

> **绘世：**自那天起，我和你们的「毁灭」再无瓜葛。

**Graphia:** Your failure began the moment you tried to destroy the Express. All you did was turn the spirit of the Nameless into a rallying cry for rebellion.

> **绘世：**摧毁列车，是你失败的开始。无名客的精神成了反抗的旗帜。

**Graphia:** I took the power you forced upon me and used it to save the innocent. You commanded me to record the end of worlds, but instead, I created a sanctuary.

> **绘世：**你给我的力量，我用它拯救无辜。你让我记录世界的毁灭，我却创造了一座天国。

**Asat Pramad:** So you think putting on a human mask makes you one of the "Trailblazers." How amusing...

> **归寂：**自以为戴上人类的面具，就能成为「开拓」的一员。哈哈……

**Asat Pramad:** Look at yourselves. That Weathering Curse is still eating away at you and your kin. It is the inescapable price for being a child of Destruction who dares to betray her fate.

> **归寂：**睁开眼看看吧，风化诅咒仍在灼烧你和你的后裔，那是身为毁灭子嗣，却要背弃命途的惩罚。

**Asat Pramad:** World in Canvas? A total disaster, yet you still try to dress it up as a victory.

> **归寂：**画中世界？一场彻头彻尾的惨败，竟也能被你渲染成胜利。

**Asat Pramad:** Do you know why I haven't spared Ahatopia a single glance for an entire millennium?

> **归寂：**知道这一千年，我为什么对哈托彼亚不闻不问么？

**Asat Pramad:** Because you bore me.

> **归寂：**因为——你，让我感到无聊。

**Graphia:** ...

> **绘世：**……

**Asat Pramad:** But now, things have changed. Someone far more interesting than you has returned. And unlike you, she is the real deal, a genuine Nameless.

> **归寂：**但现在，情况不同了。一个比你更有趣的人回来了。和你不同，她是个货真价实的无名客。

**Asat Pramad:** That is why I have returned. Simply to tell you all this.

> **归寂：**所以，我回来了。我来这里，只是为了跟你说这些。

- ▸ That genuine Nameless...

> ▸ 另一位无名客…

**(Trailblazer):** Himeko?

> **开拓者：**姬子？

> **银狼：**瓜吃够了，我的拳头已经准备好往你脸上砸了。

**Graphia:** For a thousand years, I have painted them over and over again. Every innocent life you extinguished during the Cindearth Age...

> **绘世：**这千年里，我一遍又一遍地描绘烬土纪元里，死于你手的无辜生命……

**Graphia:** Tell me, Asat Pramad. Are you prepared to face their fury?

> **绘世：**归寂，准备好接受它们的怒火了吗？

**Asat Pramad:** I have more important things to do than linger here. But this little parting gift of yours...

> **归寂：**我还有更重要的事，恕不奉陪。至于你准备的这份饯别礼物……

> **归寂：**不如，就留给他们/她们享用吧？

### Check on your companions

> *With Blade's help, Graphia managed to break free from Destruction's rampage. Asat Pramad has already arrived at Planarcadia. Transmit this news to your companions as soon as possible.*

**Blade:** I hope I am not too late.

> **刃：**希望我没有来晚。

**Silver Wolf:** Are you kidding? Your timing is impeccable, Bladie!

> **银狼：**哪儿的话，你到得太及时了，阿刃！

**Silver Wolf:** But isn't this a bit much? How did you even change outfits? What happened down there?

> **银狼：**但是，过分了吧，怎么连衣服都换了一身？下面发生了什么？

**Blade:** I used the Leviathan's maw to imprison Shuhu. But that doesn't matter.

> **刃：**我借助兽口，将倏忽囚禁了起来。但这不重要。

> **银狼：**重要的是，你活了下来，同时完成了任务。

> **刃：**我会留在这里，成为谒者，加入幻月游戏。

**Silver Wolf:** What? You're going to stay here and poke your nose into this world's business too!?

> **银狼：**啊？你也要留在这个世界多管闲事？！

**Blade:** Let us settle this first. Are you okay?

> **刃：**先解决完眼前的事情再说吧。你还好吗？

**Graphia:** Thank you, all of you.

- ▸ We will stop Asat Pramad!

> ▸ 我们会阻止归寂的！

**Graphia:** I will do everything I can to help you...

> **绘世：**我也会尽力帮助你们的——

- ▸ Are you really alright?

> ▸ 你真的没事吗？

**Graphia:** As long as the canvas remains, Asat Pramad cannot kill me. You have fulfilled what Evanescia entrusted to you.

> **绘世：**只要画卷依然存在，归寂就无法杀死我。你已经完成了绯英的嘱托。

**Graphia:** It was... all me... For a thousand years, I was the one obsessed with bearing every responsibility alone.

> **绘世：**是我…是我在这漫长的千年来，执迷于将一切责任背负在自己身上。

**Graphia:** Asat Pramad was right after all. I have been a ghost of a thousand years past, arrogant enough to think my seal over the World in Canvas was unbreakable. I really believed that accumulated Wishpower would be enough to strike him down when he returned.

> **绘世：**归寂说得一点没错，我被困在了一千年前，自以为画中世界的封印无人能破解，自以为只靠累积的愿力，就能在归寂再次现身时给他致命一击。

**Graphia:** We are up against the same nightmare that survived a whole Cindearth Age. The same monster that an entire era could not even overcome.

> **绘世：**我们面对的还是那个鏖战了一整个烬土纪元，仍然没能战胜的对手。

**Silver Wolf:** Just how did he even sneak into the World in Canvas?

> **银狼：**这家伙到底是怎么潜入画中世界的？

**(Trailblazer):** The first time I stepped into Inkford Hermitage, a strange wispae tried to latch onto me, but it didn't succeed...

> **开拓者：**在踏进渡画泉隐前，曾有个奇怪的尘灵试图附在我身上，但它没有得逞……

**(Trailblazer):** Maybe that was the first step in Asat Pramad's plan to infiltrate this place.

> **开拓者：**也许，那就是归寂试图渗透的第一步。

**(Trailblazer):** Then, he took on Graphia's form to tamper with my mask and even taught me the way to summon the real Graphia. In the end, he got what he wanted.

> **开拓者：**而后，他又伪装成绘世的模样，对我的面具动了手脚，还教给了我召唤真正绘世的方法…最终让他得逞了。

**Silver Wolf:** So, this guy has only been after one thing this whole time.

> **银狼：**所以，这家伙心心念念想踏进这里的目的恐怕只有一个。

- ▸ To weaken Graphia.

> ▸ 削弱绘世的力量…

- ▸ To break the barrier of the World in Canvas.

> ▸ 破坏画中世界的隔绝…

**Blade:** And to awaken the husk of "the Voracity." We need to leave this place as soon as possible. The giant maw that swallowed me is starting to stir.

> **刃：**还有…唤醒「贪饕」的兽蜕。我们得尽快离开这儿了，那个吞下我的巨口，似乎正在不断活化。

**Silver Wolf:** But he mentioned he had something more important to do... What is that even supposed to mean? He has so much on his plate, no way he can split himself into pieces and handle everything at once, right?

> **银狼：**但是那家伙还说要去赴一个重要的约定…那是啥？他日理万机，总不见得掰成好几截一块行动吧？

**Graphia:** A thousand years ago, the Demon Lord From Beyond invaded Ahatopia. The power he unleashed was beyond all comprehension.

> **绘世：**千年之前，界外天魔主入侵哈托彼亚，他所展现的是某种不可思议的力量……

**Graphia:** He could appear in places thousands of miles apart at the exact same time, as if he possessed countless incarnations.

> **绘世：**他会在同一时间现身相距千里之遥的地方，犹如拥有无数身外化身——

**Silver Wolf:** Whaaaaaaaat???

> **银狼：**啊？？？

**(Trailblazer):** He said he was meeting a "genuine Nameless..."

> **开拓者：**他说「要去见一位真正的无名客」……

**(Trailblazer):** ...Himeko, she's in trouble!

> **开拓者：**…姬子，有麻烦了！

### Head to Graphia Academy and continue the adventure

> *Even the most elaborate punchline feels cheap without a proper setup. I've been preparing for a long, long time to tell this joke. So, for Aha's sake, just laugh.*

**Ryusuke:** The biggest problem... Do you mean me?

> **隆介：**最大的麻烦，是说爸爸我吗？

**Himeko:** You said that classroom was a secret place of your own creation, yet Fulwish seemed to know everything about it.

> **姬子：**你说那间教室是你幻造的隐秘场所，满愿却对它了如指掌。

**Himeko:** You are far too familiar with the Express, yet there is no way my father could have ever set foot on it.

> **姬子：**你对列车的情况过分熟悉，可父亲绝不会有登上它的机会。

**Himeko:** And another thing, when we first met up earlier, you said it had been fifteen years.

> **姬子：**还有，初次见面时，你说「十五年没见」。

**Himeko:** But my father clearly left much earlier than that. Your story is riddled with holes. Or rather, you never even intended to pretend.

> **姬子：**但父亲明明在更早的时候就离开了。漏洞百出，不如说，你根本没打算隐藏。

**Ryusuke:** You are the only person I can never seem to lie to. And about that last point, it was just a slip of the tongue.

> **隆介：**唯独在你面前，我没办法说谎。至于最后一条，只是口误而已。

**Himeko:** Stop this pathetic act. You are not him.

> **姬子：**别再惺惺作态了。你不是他。

**Himeko:** You, who dare to impersonate my father, show your true self.

> **姬子：**冒充我父亲的人，现出你的原形。

**Ryusuke:** It seems our pleasant little father daughter chat has come to an end.

> **隆介：**看来，愉快的父女谈心结束了啊。

**Ryusuke:** Buying time, keeping me pinned down, you played your part well along the way.

> **隆介：**这一路上，你做得很棒。拖延时间，牵制住我。

**Ryusuke:** If I proved to be more dangerous than anticipated, you would be right there to shield your companions.

> **隆介：**如果我比想象中更危险，你也能先一步，为同伴保驾护航。

**Ryusuke:** But "Navigator," you still overlooked one possibility. The most fatal one of all...

> **隆介：**但「领航员」，你还是漏算了一种可能性，最致命的那一种——

**Ryusuke:** Why don't you try to guess, at what exact point, did I take Ryusuke's place?

> **隆介：**猜猜看，是从什么时候开始，我取代了隆介？

**Himeko:** ...

> **姬子：**……

**Ryusuke:** It happened on the day that man, in his desperate search for the source of the Weathering Curse to save his family, was destroyed by me.

> **隆介：**答案是，从他为了妻子和女儿，追溯风化诅咒的源头，却反过来被我毁灭的那天起。

**Ryusuke:** That year, Himeko was six. At her mother's funeral, she met her father for the first time.

> **隆介：**那一年，姬子六岁。在母亲的葬礼上，她和她的父亲第一次见面。

**Himeko:** You are lying.

> **姬子：**你在说谎。

**Ryusuke:** I have told you only one lie in all these years, my dear daughter.

> **隆介：**这辈子，我只对女儿说过一句谎。

**Ryusuke:** "Nice to meet you. I am your father, Ryusuke."

> **隆介：**「初次见面。我是你的父亲，隆介。」

**Himeko:** All that effort, all those years spent by my side... What are you really planning?

> **姬子：**费尽心思，陪我度过如此漫长的时光，你究竟有什么阴谋？

**Ryusuke:** Even I find it hard to tell what goes on in this head of mine sometimes.

> **隆介：**我这个人啊，脑袋里在想什么，有时候连自己都搞不明白。

**Ryusuke:** But now as I look at your face, at that exquisite expression, it all makes sense...

> **隆介：**但现在，看见你的表情，我终于明白了——

**Ryusuke:** I had SO MUCH FUN playing a father.

> **隆介：**扮演一位爸爸，我*玩得*很开心。

**Ryusuke:** And the best part? That was not even the most fun I have had today.

> **隆介：**更美妙的是，这甚至不是今天最有乐子的事。

**Ashveil:** Hey! Can you hear me? Someone who looks like Asat Pramad just showed up at the tavern!

> **不死途：**喂！听得见吗？疑似归寂的人出现在了酒馆。

**Welt:** We are under attack by Asat Pramad! Dan Heng and I will do everything we can to cover Yao Guang...

> **瓦尔特：**我们遭遇了归寂的袭击，我和丹恒会尽可能掩护爻光——

**Ryusuke:** I have played along this far, so why not make it interesting? Let us add a dash of red to our little opening act.

> **隆介：**都陪你演到这儿了，何不再来点仪式感，为即将开场的好戏多点缀一份血色？

**Ryusuke:** You have proven your theory by tearing off the mask of "Ryusuke." As a courtesy, it's now time to strip away your disguise.

> **隆介：**你证明了自己的猜想，撕下了「隆介」的面具。礼尚往来，让我也撕开你的伪装吧。

**Ryusuke:** The ugliest lie of the "Trailblaze." The truth that has been hidden for fifteen years.

> **隆介：**「开拓」最丑陋的谎言，被隐瞒了十五年的真相——

**Red-Haired Woman:** ...

> **红发的女性：**……

**Red-Haired Woman:** "When the Phantasmoon is full again, I will be there as I have promised."

> **红发的女性：**「下一次幻月满盈，我一定会赴约的。」

**Red-Haired Woman:** "I will come back with so many stories to tell you."

> **红发的女性：**「带着满满当当的故事，回来讲给你听。」

**Ryusuke:** The girl looked up at the stars, but in her heart, she was terrified of setting out. She was afraid of the universe, and even more, she was afraid of the weight of responsibility.

> **隆介：**仰望星空的少女，却从心底里害怕启程。她害怕宇宙，更害怕那份沉重的责任。

**Ryusuke:** In the end, she'd left her wishes to someone else. As for her, she chose to lie in a grave and stay in the arcadia forever.

> **隆介：**最后，她将愿望托付给了另一个人，自己则躺在坟墓里，永远留在了乐园。

**Ryusuke:** Ryusuke's daughter, Himeko, is long dead. When she saw how vast the stars really were, she ran away.

> **隆介：**隆介的女儿，姬子，早就死了。面对无垠的星空，她逃走了。

**Ryusuke:** Just as the "Trailblaze" will always converge with the "Destruction," culminating in awe-inspiring obliteration.

> **隆介：**就像「开拓」总会与「毁灭」相交，然后，化作壮美的埃灭。

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Asat Pramad:** So you really are a fake.

> **归寂：**你果然是冒牌货。

**"Himeko":** Right back at you.

> **姬子：**彼此彼此。

**Asat Pramad:** O denizens of this arcadia, lost in its splendor... Once, I beheld the Astral Express rise again towards the stars here.

> **归寂：**沉溺于繁荣中的乐园众生啊——我曾在此目睹星穹列车再度飞向星空。

**Asat Pramad:** Since then, you have known fifteen long years of unbroken peace.

> **归寂：**此后，你们已享用了整整十五年平稳时光。

**Asat Pramad:** It's been a while, old friend.

> **归寂：**好久不见了，老朋友。

**Asat Pramad:** For fifteen years, it was I — Asat Pramad — who delivered every promise of beauty to this arcadia in advance...

> **归寂：**十五年来，是我，归寂！将所有美好的可能性提前赠予这片乐园——

**Asat Pramad:** Now...

> **归寂：**现在……

**Asat Pramad:** Please, indulge in these long-overdue surprises.

> **归寂：**请享用那些迟到的意外吧。

*(Cutscene ends)*

*(Visitor Verification)*

(Mortenax Blade has become a visitor to the Express. You might come across Mortenax Blade when you return to the Express)

*(Unlock Achievement A Jest That Fades Into the Void)*
